Þu leofusta broður, suiðe freondlice & suiðe fremsumlice ðu me tældesð, & mid eaðmode
Ðara byrðenna hefignesse, eall ðæt ic his geman, ic awrite on ðisse andweardan bec, ðylæs hi
& ic eac lære ðæt hira nan ðara ne wilnie ðe hine unwærlice bega;
& se ðe hi unwærlice & unryhtlice gewilnige, ondræde he ðæt he hi æfre underfenge.
Nu ic wilnige ðætte ðeos spræc stigge on ðæt ingeðonc ðæs leorneres, suæ suæ on sume hlædre,
& forðy ic hi todæle on feower: an is ðara dæla hu he on ðone folgoð becume;
Oðer hu he ðæron libbe;
Ðridda is hu he ðæron lære;
Feorðe is hu he his agene unðeawas ongietan wille & hira geðæf bion, þylæs he for ðy underfenge
Ac gemetgige hit se ege his agenra unðeawa, & befæste he mid his lifes bisenum ða lare ðæm ðe
& ðonne he god weorc wyrce, gemyne he ðæs yfeles ðe he worhte, ðette sio unrotnes, ðe he for
Ðylæs he beforan ðæs dieglan deman eagum sie ahafen on his mode & on his ofermettum aðunden,
Ac monige sindon me suiðe onlice on ungelærednesse, ðeah ðe hi næfre leorningcnihtas næren,
From ðære dura selfre ðisse bec, ðæt is from onginne ðisse spræce, sint adrifene & getælde ða
Ðætte unlærde ne dyrren underfon lareowdom.
Forðonðe nan cræft nis to læranne ðæm ðe hine ær geornlice ne leornode, forhwon beoð æfre suæ
Hua nat ðæt ða wunda ðæs modes bioð digelran ðonne ða wunda ðæs lichaman?
& ðeah ða woroldlecan læcas scomaþ ðæt hi onginnen ða wunda lacnian ðe hi gesion ne magon, &
& hwilon ne scomað ða ðe ðæs modes læceas beon scoldon, ðeah ðe hi nana wuht ongitan ne
Ac forðonðe nu eall se weorðscipe ðisse worolde is gecierred, Gode ðonc, to weorðscipe ðæm
Be ðam Crisð selfa cleopode, & ðus cwæð: Hi secað ðæt hi mon ærest grete & weorðige on
Forðon hie sua on ofermettum & mid upahafenesse becumað to ðære are ðære hirdelecan giemenne,
Ac sio tunge bið gescinded on ðam lariowdome ðonne hio oðer lærð, oðer hio liornode.
Suelcum monnum Dryhten cidde ðurh ðone witgan, & him suelc oðwat, ða he cuæð: Hie ricsedon,
Ealdormen hi wæron, & ic hie ne cuðe.
Ða ðe sua ricsieað, hi ricsiað of hira agnum dome, næs of ðæs hiehstan deman, ðonne hi ne beoð
Hie ðonne se eca & se diegla dema upahefeð suelce he hi nyte, & geðafiende he hit forbireð for
Ac ðeah hi on ðam hade fela wundra wyrcen, eft ðonne hi to him cumað, he cuið: Gewitað from
Nat ic hwæt ge sint.
Eft he hie ðreade ðurh ðone witgan for hira ungelærednesse, ða he cuæð: ða hierdas næfdon
Se ðe Godes bebodu ne gecnæwð, ne bið he oncnawen from Gode.
Ðæt ilce cuæð sanctus Paulus: Se ðe God ne ongit, ne ongit God hine.
Unwise lareowas cumað for ðæs folces synnum.
Forðon oft for ðæs lareowes unwisdome misfarað ða hieremenn, & oft for ðæs lareowes wisdome
Gif ðonne ægðer bið unwis, ðonne is to geðencanne hwæt Crisð self cuæð on his godspelle, he
Be ðæm ilcan se sealmscop cuæð: Sien hira eagan aðistrode ðæt hi ne geseon, & hiora hrygc simle
Ne cuæð he ðæt forðyðe he ænegum men ðæs wyscte oððe wilnode, ac he witgode sua sua hit
Soðlice ða eagan ðæt beoð ða lareowas, & se hrygc ðæt sint ða hieremenn;
Forðan ða eagan bioð on ðam lichoman foreweardum & ufeweardum, & se hrycg færð æfter ælcre
Sua gað ða lareowas beforan ðæm folce, & ðæt folc æfter.
Ðonne ðam lareowum aðistriað ðæs modes eagan, ðe beforan gan scoldon mid godum bisenum,
Ne eft ða gelæredan ðe swa nyllað libban swa hie on bocum leornedon, ðæt hi scoldon ne underfon
Monige eac wise lareowas winnað mid hira ðeawum wið ða gæsðlecan bebodu ðe hi mid wordum
Oft ðonne se hirde gæð on frecne wegas, sio hiord ðe unwærre bið, gehrist.
Be suelcum hirdum cwæð se witga: Ge fortrædon Godes sceapa gærs & ge gedrefdon hiora wæter
Sua ða lareowas hi drincað suiðe hluter wæter, ðonne hi ðone godcundan wisdom leorniað, & eac
Ac hie hit gedrefað mid hira agnum unðeawum, ðonne ðæt folc bisenað on hira unðeawum, nals on
Ðeah ðæt folc ðyrste ðære lare, hie hie ne magon drincan, ac hio bið gedrefed midðamðe ða
Be ðæm Dryhten cwæð eft ðurh ðone witgan: Yfle preostas bioþ folces hryre.
Ne dereð nan mon suiðor ðære halgan gesomnunge ðonne ða ðe ðonne noman underfoð & ða
Forðon hie nan monn ne dearr ðreagean ðeah hie agyltan, ac mid ðam beoð synna suiðe gebrædda
Ac hie woldon selfe fleon ða byrðenne sua micelre scylde, ða ðe his unwierðe wæron, gif hie mid
Ðurh ða cweorne is getacnod se ymbhwyrft ðisse worolde & eac monna lifes & hira gesuinces, &
Ðonne bið sio cweorn becierred ðonne se monn bið geendod;
Ðonne bið sio micle cweorn becierred ðonne ðeos weorld bið geendod.
Se ðonne to halgum hade becymð, & ðonne mid yflum bisnum oððe worda oððe weorca oðre on
Forðæm gif he on ðæm wel deð, he hæfð ðæs god lean, gif he yfle deð, læsse wite he ðrowað on
Be ðære byrðenne ðæs reccenddomes, & hu he scyle eall earfoðu forsion, & hu forht he sceal beon
Forðon we ðiss feawum wordum sædon, ðy we woldon gecyðan hu micel sio byrðen bið ðæs
Suiðe medomlice Iacobus se apostol his stirde, ða he cuæð: Broðor ne beo eower to fela lareowa.
Forðæm se wealhstod self Godes & monna, ðæt is Crist, fleah eorðrice to underfonne.
Se se ðe ealne ðone wisdom ðara uferrenna gæsta oferstigð & ær worolde ricsode on hefenum, hit
Ða se Hælend ðæt ongeat, ða becierde he hie & gehydde hiene.
Hwa meahte ieð monnum rædan butan scylde, ðonne se ðe hi gescop?
Ne fleah he ðy rice ðy his ænig monn bet wyrðe wære, ac he wolde us ða bisene astellan, ðæt we
& eac wolde for us ðrowian.
He nolde beon cyning, & his agnum willan he com to rode gealgan.
Ða weorðmynde cynehades he fleah, & ðæt wite ðæs fraceðlecestan deaðes he geceas, forðam
& eac ðæt ðæt we his ege & his brogan us ne ondreden, & for soðfæsðnesse ðæt we lufigen
Forðam for ðære orsorgnesse monn oft aðint on ofermettum, & ða earfeðu ðurh sar & ðurh sorge
On ðæm gesundfulnessum ðæt mod wierð upahafen;
& on ðæm earfeðum, ðeah hit ær upahafen wære, hit bið geeaðmedd.
On ðære gesundfulnesse mon forgiett his selfes;
On ðæm gesuincum he sceal hine selfne geðencean, ðeah he nylle.
On ðære orsorgnesse oft ðæt he to gode gedyde he forliesð, on ðæm earfoðum oft ðæt he longe ær
Suiðe oft monn bið ðære earfoðnesse lareowdome underðieded, ðeah he ær nolde his lareowes
Ac ðeah hine ðonne ða brocu getyn & gelæren, sona, gif he on rice becymð, for ðære weorðunge
Sua sua Saul se cyning, æresð he fleah ðæt rice, & tealde hine selfne his suiðe unwierðne.
Ac sona sua he ðone anwald onfeng ðæs rices, he astag on ofermetto, & hine bealg wið ðone ilcan
& ða he him from wolde, ða gefeng he hine, & toslat his hrægl, & hine geunarode.
Sua eac Dauit, ðe folneah on eallum ðingum Gode licode, sona sua he ða byrðenne næfde sua
Se ilca se monegum yfelum wið hine selfne forworhtum ær gearode, he wearð eft sua ungemetlice
Se ilcan Dauid ðe forbær ðæt he ðone kyning ne yfelode, ðe hine on sua heardum wræce gebrohte,
Se ilca Dauid miclum his agenes herges pleah, & monigne forsende, ðær he ymb his getreowne
Sio scyld hine suiðe feorr of ealra haligra rime atuge, ðær him eft ða gesuinc & ða earfeðu ne
Ond hu oft sio bisgung ðæs rices & ðæs recedomes toslit ðæt mod ðæs recceres.
Suiðe oft gedrefeð ða heortan sio manigfealde giemen ðæs underfangenan lareowdomes, & ðonne
Bi ðam cuæð Salomonn se snottra: Sunu min, ne todæl ðu on to fela ðin mod, & ðin weorc
Forðan oft ðonne mon forlæt ðone ege & ða fæsðrædnesse ðe he mid ryhte on him innan habban
He sorgað ymb ða, & bið ðara suiðe gemyndig, & forgiett his selfes, ðonne he suiðor his mod
Him bið sua sua ðam menn ðe bið abisgod on færelde mid oðrum cierrum, oððæt he nat hwider he
Ne wende na Ezechias Israhela kyning ðæt he gesyngade, ða he lædde ða ællðeodgan ærenddracan
Ac he onfunde ðeah Godes ierre on ðam hearme ðe his bearne æfter his dagum becom.
& ðeah he wende ðæt hit nan syn nære.
Oft ðonne hwæm gebyreð ðæt he hwæt mærlices & wundorlices gedeð, & his ðonne wundriað ða
Suaðeah mid ðy selflice se Dema bið genieded to ðæm ierre, & se Dema se ðe ðæt inngeðonc eall
We magon monnum bemiðan urne geðonc & urne willan, ac we ne magon Gode.
Hwæt se Babylonia cyning wæs suiðe upahafen on his mode for his anwalde & for his gelimpe, ða
Ða suigendan stefne suiðe hraðe se diegla Dema gehirde, & him suiðe undeogollice gondwyrde
Ða upahafenesse he arasode & hie getælde, ða he hine ascead of ðam woroldrice, & hine
& sua ðy ðearlan dome he forleas his mennisce.
Se ilca se ðe wende ðæt he wære ofer ealle oðere menn, him gebyrede ðæt he nyste self hwæðer he
Suaðeah, ðeah ic nu ðis recce, næ tæle ic na micel weorc ne ryhtne anwald, ac ic tæle ðæt hine mon
& ða untrymnesse hiera heortan ic wolde getrymman & gestiran ðære wilnunge ðæm unmedemum,
Bi ðæm ðe magon on ealdordome nytte beon on bisnum & on cræftum, & ðonne for hira agenre
Ac monige siendun mid miclum giefum monegra cræfta & mægene geweorðode, forðonðe hie hie
Ðæt is ðæt hie gehealdað hira lichoman firenlusta clænne;
Oðer is ðæt hi beoð on færhæfðnesse strenge strange;
Ðridde is ðæt hi beoð mid lara suetmettum gefylde;
Feorðe is ðæt hi beoð on ælengum ðingum & ælcre longunge geðyldige, & on forebyrde eaðmode;
Fifte is ðæt hie habbað ða arodnesse & ða bieldo ðæt hie magon anweald habban;
Siexte is ðæt hi beoð fremsume;
Siofoðe is ðæt hi beoð reðe & stræce for ryhtwisnesse.
Ða ðe ðonne ðyllice beoð, & him mon suelcne folgað beodeð, & hie him wiðsacað, oft him
Ðonne hie synderlice ðenceað hu hie selfe scylen fullfremodeste weorðan, & ne giemað to hwon
Be suelcum monnum Crist on his godspelle cuæð: Ne scyle nan mon blæcern ælan under mittan.
& eft he cuæð to Petre ðæm apostole: Petrus lufastu me?
He cuæð: ðu wast ðæt ic ðe lufige.
& ða cuæð Dryhten: Fed ðonne min sceap, gif ðu me lufige.
Gif ðonne seo feding ðara sceapa bið ðære lufan tacen, hwi forcwið ðonne se ðe him God suelce
Be ðam Paulus se apostol cuæð: Gif Crist for us eallum dead wæs, ðonne weorðað ealle menn
Hwæt is ðonne betere ða hwile ðe we libben, ðonne we ures flæsces lustum ne libben, ac ðæs
Be ðam cuæð Moyses: Gif hwa gefare & nan bearn ne gestriene, gif he broðor læfe, fo se to his
Gif he ðonne bearn ðærbig gestriene, ðonne cenne he ðæt ðam gefarenan breðer ðe hie ær ahte.
Gif he ðonne ðæt wif wille forsacan, ðonne hræce hio him on ðæt nebb foran, & his mægas hine
Ðis wæs ryht dom on ðære ealdan æ, & is nu us to bispelle.
Se ær gefarena broðor getacnað Crist.
He hine ætiede æfter ðære æriste, & cuæð: Farað & cyðað minum broðrum ðæt hie cumen to
Ðær hie me geseoð.
He gefor suelce he butan bearnum gefore, forðon he næfde gefylled ðagiet ðone rim his gecorenra.
Sua sua ðæs gefarenan broðor wif on ðære ealdan æ wæs geboden ðæm lifiendan breðer to
Gif hire ðonne se wiðsace, ðonne is cynn ðæt him spiwe ðæt wif on ðæt nebb, ðæt is ðæt hine tæle
Sua is cynn ðæt sio halige gesomnung tæle ælces ðara god ðe hit him anum wile to gode habban, &
Se bið eac mid ryhte oðre fet anscod, & hine mon scyle on bismer hatan se anscoda.
Be ðæm cuæð Crist on his gospelle: Sceawiað iowre fet, ðæt ge sien gearwe to ganganne on sibbe
Gief we ðonne habbað sua micle sorge & sua micle gieman urra niehstena sua sua ure selfra,
Gif we ðonne agiemeleasiað urra niehstena ðearfa, & ðenceað ymbe ure synderlice, ðonne bið us
Monige menn siendon, sua sua we ær cuædon, ðe beoð geweorðode mid miclum & mid monegum
Gif him ðonne God ryhtlice & stræclice deman wile, & he him for his mildheortnesse ne arað,
Hwæt ðenceað ða ðe on suelcum weorcum scinað, & magon hiera niehstum sua nytte beon, hwy
Hwæt se ancenneda Godes sunu of his fæder bosme wæs ferende to urre andweardnesse ðæt he ure
Bi ðæm ðe for eaðmodnesse fleoð ða byrðenne ðæs lareowdomes, ðonne hie beoð ryhtlice eaðmode
Ðonne siendon monige ðe fleoð for eaðmodnesse anre, forðon hie noldon ðæt hie mon ahofe ofer ða
Nis ðæs ðonne nan tweo, gif suelc eaðmodnes bið mid oðrum godum ðeawum begyrded, ðæt ðæt
Ne bið ðæt na soð eaðmodnes, gif mon ongiett ðæt ðæt Godes willa sie ðæt he ofer oðre beon
Ðonne he oferstæled bið, & him gereaht bið ðæt he oðrum mæg nytt bion on ðam ðe him mon
Ðætte oft ðæs lareowdomes ðenung bið swiðe untælwyrðlice gewilnad, & eac swiðe
Þeahhwæðre monige wilniað folgoðes & ealdordomes suiðe untælwierðlice, & monige beoð
Ðæt we magon sueotole ongietan, gif we geðenceað ða twegen witgan ðe God wolde sendan to
Oðer hiene his selfes willum gebead to ðære lare & to ðæm færelte.
Oðer for ðæm ege, ðe he ondred ðæt he hit sua medomlice don ne meahte, him wiðsoc.
Ðæt wæs Heremias.
Ða he hine sendan wolde, ða bæd he eaðmodlice ðæt he hiene ne sende & cuæð: Eala eala eala
Hwæt conn ic sprecan?
Ac Essaias, ða Dryhten acsode hwone he sendan meahte, ða cuæð Essaias: Ic eom gearo;
Send me.
Loca nu hu ungelic spræc eode of ðissa tuega monna muðe.
Ac hio wæs of suiðe gelicum willan, forðon hio aweoll of anum wille;
Ðeah heo an tu tefleowe, ðeah wæs sio æspryng sio soðe lufu.
Ymb ða we habbað tua bebodu: an is ðæt we lufigen God, oðer ðæt we lufien ure niehstan.
For ðære lufan Essaias wilnode hu he nyttosð meahte beon his nihstum on ðys eorðlican life, &
Hieremias ðonne wilnode singallice hine geðiedan to ðære lufan his Scippendes, & forðam he
Ðæt ilce ðæt he untælwyrðlice ondred to underfonne, ðæt ilce se oðer swiðe hergeondlice
Oðer ondred ðæt he forlure sprecende ða gestrion ðe he on ðære swigean geðencan meahte;
Oðer ondred ðæt he ongeate on his swygean ðæt he sumne hearm geswigode ðær ðær he freme
Ac we sculon swiðe smealice ðissa ægðer underðencean, forðonðe se ðe ðær wiðcwæð, na fullice
Ac forðæmðe hit swa earfoðe is ænegum menn to witanne hwonne he geclænsod sie, he mæg ðy
Ond næs swaðeah to anwillice ne forbuge he, swa we ær cwædon, ðonne he ongiete ðone
Ægðer ðissa gefylde Moyses ða he wiðsoc swa miclum ealdordome.
Ægðer ge he wolde ge he nolde, & ðeah for eaðmodnesse geðafode.
We witon ðæt he nære eaðmod, gif he underfenge ðone ealdordom swelces unrimfolces buton ege;
& eft he wære ofermod, gif he wiðcwæde ðæt he nære underðidd his Scippende.
Ac ægðer ðissa he dyde for eaðmodnesse & for underðidednesse.
He sceawode hine selfne, & pinsode, ða ða him ðuhte ðæt he hit doon ne meahte, & swaðeah
Hwæt se haliga wer ongeat þæt he hæfde Godes fultom, & swaðeah ondred ðæt he underfenge
God selfa tyhte Moyses on ðone folgoð, swaðeah he him ondred;
Ond nu fandiað swelce wræccan & teoð to, woldon underfon ðone weorðscipe & eac ða byrðenne;
& ða ðe beoð mid hira agnum byrðennum ofðrycte ðæt hie ne magon gestondan, hie willað lustlice
He ne mæg his agne aberan, & wolde ðeah maran habban.
Be ðæm ðe wilnað biscephad to underfonne, hu hie gegripað ðone cwide ðæs apostoles Paules hiora
Ac ða ðe willað gripan on swelcne folgað for hiera gitsunge hie doð him to leafe ðone cwide ðe
Gif he hit ða herede & on tyhte, eft he stierde ðære gewilnunge ða he cwæð: Biscepe gedafnað ðæt
Ðærbufan is geteald hwelc he beon sceal, gif he untælwierðe bið.
Mid oðrum worde he hierte, mid oðrum he bregde, swelce he openlice cwæde: Ic herige ðæt ge
Sua se micla cræftiga hiertende toscyfð, & egesiende stierð ofermetta mid ðære tælinge his
Eac is to geðencanne ðæt on ða tiid ðe se biscephad swa gehiered wæs, sua hwelc swa hine
On ða tiid wæs to herigeanne ðæt mon wilnode biscephades, ða ðe nan twio næs ðæt he ðurh ðone
Ðæt is to tacne ðæt mon endebyrðlice ðone biscepdom healde, ðæt he hine on godum weorcum
Forðon hit is gecweden: Se ðe biscephad gewilnað, god weorc he gewilnað.
Se ðonne for ðære gewilnunge swelcra weorca biscopdom ne secð, he bið ðonne him self gewita
Ne deð he ðonne ðæt an yfel ðæt he ne lufað ða halgan ðegnunga, ac eallinga he hie forsiehð;
Ond ðonne he fundað to ðæm weorðscipe ðæs folgoðes, his mod bið afedd mid ðære smeaunga
Ahefð ðonne his heortan forðy, & for ðære genyhte ðæs flowendan welan he blissað.
He licet eaðmodnesse, & secð mid ðam ðisses middangeardes gestreon.
On ðæm hiewe ðe he sceolde his gielpes stieran on ðæm he his strienð.
Mid ðy ðe he sceolde his gestreon toweorpan, mid ðy he hie gadrað.
Ðonne ðæt mod ðenceð gegripan him to upahefenesse ða eaðmodnesse, ðæt ðæt he utan eowað
Hu ðæt mod ðætte wilnað for oðre beon lihð him selfum, ðonne hit ðencð fela godra weorca to
Ac ðonne he wilnað to underfonne ða Are & ðone ealdordom, he ðencð on ðam oferbrædelse his
Ac on uteweardum his mode he liehð him selfum ymbe hine selfne bie ðæm godum weorcum;
Licet ðæt he lufige ðæt he ne lufað: ðisses middangeardes gilp he lufað, & he licett swelce he ðone
Ðonne he wilnað on his mode ðæt he sciele ricsian he bið swiðe forht & swiðe behealden;
Ðonne he hæfð ðæt he habban wolde, he bið swiðe ðriste.
Ðonne he to fundað, he ondræt ðæt he ne mote to cuman, ond sona swa he to ðære are cymð, swa
Hu mæg hit butan ðam beom ðætte ðæt mod ðe ær wæs keled of his gewunan for ðære wilnunge
Ac sona beoð ðæs modes eagan eft gewende to ðæm weorcum ðe hit ær worhte.
Ac ðence ælc mon ær hu nytwyrðe he sie & hu gehiersum ðæm ðe he ðonne mid ryhte hieran scyle
Ðonne mæg he witan be ðy, gif he hierran folgað habban sceal, hwæðer he ðonne don mæg ðæt
Hu mæg he ðonne ðæt lof & ðone gilp fleon ðonne he onahæfen bið, se his ær wilnode ða he butan
Hu mæg he ðonne beon butan gitsunge, ðonne he sceal ymb monigra monna are ðencan, gif he
Healde hine ðæt hine his agen geðanc ne biswice, ðæt he ne truwige ðæt he on ðæm folgoðe wille
Forðæmðe oftor on ðæm hieran folgoðe mon forlæt goodne gewunan, ðonne he hine ðæron
Swiðe eaðe mæg on smyltre sæ ungelæred scipstiera genoh ryhte stieran, ac se gelæreda him ne
Hwæt is ðonne ðæt rice & se ealdordoom butan ðæs modes storm, se simle bið cnyssende ðæt scip
Hwæt is nu ma ymbe ðis to sprecenne, buton se se ðe swelc ongieten sie ðæt he ða cræftas hæbbe
Se ðonne se ðe ðeonde bið on swelcum cræftum & geearnungum, swelce we ær spræcon, & ðonne
Ðæt is ðæt he ða Godes gifa ðe he onfeng ge on cræftum ge on æhtum ðæt he ða ne becnytte on
Ða ðonne ðe idle beoð swelcra giefa, & ðeah wilniað ðæs ealdordomes, healden hie ðæt hie mid
Ymb ðyllic is to geðencenne & to smeaganne, forðan se ðe biscephad underfehð, he underfehð
Gif he ðonne giet geswicen næfð his agenra unðeawa, hu mæg he ðonne oðerra monna mod
Se læce bið micles to beald & to scomleas ðe gæð æfter oðra monna husum læcnigende, & hæfð on
Hwelc se bion sceal ðe to reccenddome cuman sceal.
Ac ðone monn scyle eall mægene to bisscephade teon, ðe on monigum ðrowungum his lichoman
Suelcum ingeðonce gerist ðæt he for licuman tiedernesse ne for woroldbismere anum wið ða scire
Ne sceal he naht unaliefedes don, ac ðæt ðætte oðre menn unaliefedes doth he sceal wepan sua
His weorc sceolon beon ðæs weorðe ðæt him oðre menn onhyrien.
He sceal tilian sua to libbanne sua he mæge ða adrugodan heortan geðwænnan mid ðæm
He sceal geleornian ðæt he gewunige to singallecum gebedum, oð he ongite ðæt he mæge
Hwæt wenstu nu, gif hwelc forworht monn cymð, & bitt urne hwelcne ðæt we hine læden to
Gif he me ðonne cuð ne bið, ne nan monn his hiredes, ic wille him suiðe ræðe andwyrdan &
Gif we ðonne scomiað ðæt we to uncuðum monnum suelc sprecen, hu durre we ðonne to Gode
Oððe hu dear se gripan on ða scire ðæt he ærendige oðrum monnum to Gode se se ðe hine selfne
Oððe hu dearr he ðingian oðrum monnum, & nat hwæðer him selfum geðingod bið?
He mæg ondrædan ðæt he for his ægnum scyldum mare ierre gewyrce.
Ealle we witon bi monnum, se se ðe bitt ðone monn ðæt him ðingie wið oðerne ðe he bið eac
Healden hie hie ðonne hie gitsiað sua micles ealdordomes domes ðæt hie ne weorðen ealdormenn
Ac pinsige ælc mon hiene selfne georne, ðylæs he durre underfon ðone lareowdom ðæs folces ða
Ne wilnige se na beon ðingere for oðerra scylde se ðe bið mid his agenum gescinded.
Hwelc se beon sceal se ðe ðærto cuman ne sceal.
Bi ðon cuæð sio uplice stemn to Moyse ðæt he sceolde beodan Arone ðæt nan monn hiera cynnes
Se bið eallenga blind se ðe noht ne ongiet be ðam leohte ðære uplecan sceawunge, ond se se ðe bið
Be ðæm witgode Anna, ða hio cuæð: Dryhten gehilt his haligra fet, ond ða unryhtwisan sicettað
Se bið eallenga healt se ðe wat hwider he gaan sceal, & ne mæg for his modes untrymnesse, ðeah
Ðonne ne magon ðider fullice becuman ða stæpas ðæs weorces ðieder ðe he wilnað.
Be ðæm cuæð Paulus: Astreccað eowre agalodan honda & eowru cneowu, & stæppað ryhte, ne
Ðonne is sio lytle nosu ðæt mon ne sie gescadwis;
Forðæm mid ðære nose we tosceadað ða stencas, forðam is sio nosu gereaht to gesceadwisnesse.
Ðurh ða gesceadwisnesse we tocnawað good & yfel, & geceosað ðæt god, & aweorpað ðæt yfel.
Be ðæm is gecueden on ðære bryde lofe: ðin nosu is suelc suel se torr on Liuano ðæm munte.
Forðæm sio halige gesomnung ðurh gesceadwisnesse gesiehð & ongietað of huan ælc costung
Ac monige menn beoð ðe noldon ðone hlisan habban ðæt hie unwiese sien;
Angiennað ðonne oftrædlice mare seNcgean & smeagean suiðor ðonne him ðearf sie to
Ðæt is sio micle nosu & sio woo se ðe wile ungemetlice gesceadwis beon, & secð ðæt smealicor
Ðæt is ðonne se foreda foot & sio forude hond ðæt mon wite Godes biboda weg, & ðær nylle on
Se ðonne bið hoferede se ðe sio byrðen ofðrycð ðisse eorðlican gewilnunge, & næfre ne besyhð
Ac ealne weg fundað to ðeosum eorðlecum, ond ðonne hie gehierað awuht be ðæm gode ðæs
Be ðæm se salmsceop cwæð: Ic eom gebiged, & æghwonon ic eom gehiened.
Ond eft be ðæm ilcan scyldum sio Soðfæstnes ðurh hie selfe cwæð: Hiera sæd gefeollon on ða
Ðæt sindon ða ðe gehierað Godes word, & mid ðære geornfulnesse & mid ðære wilnunge ðisse
Se ðonne bið siwenige se ðe his andgit bið to ðon beorhte scinende ðæt he mæge ongietan
Hwæt on ðæs siwenigean eagum beoð ða æpplas hale, ac ða bræwas greatigað, forðam hie beoð
Swa sindon wel monege ðara ðe gewundiað hiera mod mid ðæm weorcum ðisses flæsclican lifes,
Se bið eallinga siwenige ðonne his mod & his andgit ðæt gecynd ascirpð, & he hit ðonne self
Be ðæm wæs wel gecweden ðurh ðone ængel: Smiriað eowre eagan mid sealfe ðæt ge mægen
Ðonne we smierewað ure heortan eage mid sealfe ðæt we mægen ðy bet geseon, ðonne we mid
Se ðonne hæfð eallinga fleah on his modes eagum, ðe on nane wisan ne mæg ryhtwisnesse
Ðurh ðone æpl ðæs eagan mon mæg geseon, gif him ðæt fleah on ne gæð, gif hine ðonne ðæt
Sua eac bi ðæs modes eagum is gecueden, gif ðæt ondgit ðæs menniscan geðohtes ongiett ðæt hit
Gif he ðonne self wenð ðæt he sie wis & gescadwislice ryhtwis, mid ðy he hiene bedælð ðære
Sua sua be sumum monnum cueden is: Hie sædon ðæt hie wæren wiese, & ða wurdon hie dysige
Soðlice se ðe hæfð singalne sceabb se ðe næbre ne ablinð ungestæððignesse.
Ðonne bi ðam sceabbe suiðe ryhte sio hreofl getacnað ðæt wohhæmed.
Ðonne bið se lichoma hreof, ðonne se bryne ðe on ðæm innoðe bið utaslihð to ðære hyde.
Sua bið sio costung æresð on ðæm mode, & ðonne fereð utweardes to ðære hyde, oððæt hio
Butan tweon gif ðæt mod ær ðæm willan ne wiðbritt, se wielm ðæs innoðes utabiersð & wierð to
Forðon wilnode sanctus Paulus ðæt he ðære hyde giocðan ofadrygde mid ðæm worde, ða he
Suelce he openlice cuæde: Mennisclic is ðæt mon on his mode costunga ðrowige on ðæm luste
Se ðonne hæfð teter on his lichoman se hæfð on his mode gitsunga, gif hiere ne bið sona
Butan tueon se teter butan sare he ofergæð ðone lichoman, & sua ðeah ðæt lim geunwlitegað;
Se giecða bið suiðe unsar, & se cleweða bið suiðe row, & ðeahhwæðere gif him mon to longe
Sua eac sio gitsung ðæt mod ðæt hio gebindeð mid ðære lustfulnesse hio hit gewundað, ðonne
Hio gehæt him æghwæs genog, ðeah ðæt ðonne ðæm mode licige & lustfullige, ðeah hit
Ðurh ða wunde he forliest ðone wlite his lioma, ðonne he ðurh ðæt woo weorc forliest ðone wlite
Ðæt trymede sanctus Paulus ða he cuæð ðæt ælces yfeles wyrttruma wære ðæt mon wilnode
Se ðonne se bið healede he ne mæg mid weorce begean ða scondlicnesse, & suaðeah bið ahefegod
Ðonan cymeð sio mettrymnes ðæm healedum, ðe se wæta ðara innoða astigð to ðæm lime, ðonne
Se bið eac eallenga healede se se ðe eall his mod bið aflogen to gæglbærnesse & to dole, ðonne
Sua hwelc ðonne sua ðissa uncysta hwelcre underðieded bið, him bið forboden ðæt he offrige
Ær ðioson we sægdon feam wordum hwelc se bion scolde ðe medome hierde & lareow bion
Ær ðiosum we rehton hwelc se beon sceolde ðe to ðæm biscepdome cuman sceolde;
Nu we willað reccan, gif he ær suelc to cyme, hu he ðæron libban scyle.
Hu se se ðe gedafenlice & endebyrdlice to cymð, hu he ðæron drohtian scyle.
Þæs biscepes weorc sceolon bion ofer oðra monna weorc sua micle beteran sua hit micel bið
Him gedafenað ðæt he geðence & geornlice smeage hu micel niedðearf him is ðæt he sie gebunden
Hwæt ðæm hierde ðonne wel gerisð ðæt he sie healic on his weorcum, & his word sien nyttwyrðu,
Him sculan eglan oðerra monna brocu suelce he efnsuiðe him ðrowige;
He sceal sorgian ymbe ealle & foreðencean;
He sceal bion for eaðmodnesse hira gefera ælces ðara ðe wel doo;
He sceal bion stræc wið ða ðe ðær agyltað, ond for ryhtwisnesse he sceal habban andan to hira
Ond ðeah for ðara bisgunge ne sie his giemen na ðy læsse ymb ða gehirsuman;
Ne eac for hira lufan geornfulnesse ne forlæte he ða ungehirsuman.
Ac ðis ðæt we nu feam wordum arimdon we willað hwene rumedlicor heræfter areccean.
Hu se lareow sceal bion clæne on his mode.
Se reccere sceal bion simle clæne on his geðohte, ðætte nan unclænnes hine ne besmite ðonne he
Hit is ðearf ðæt sio hond sie ær geclænsad ðe wille ðæt fenn of oðerre aðierran;
Gif sio ðonne bið eac fennegu, ðonne is wen ðæt hio ða oðre wiers besmite gif hio hire anhrinð.
Forðæm wæs ðurh ðone witgan gecueden: Dooð eow clæne, ge ðe berað Godes fatu.
Ða ðonne berað Godes fatu, ða ðe oðerra monna saula underfooð to lædanne on ða treowa hira
Geðencen hie ðonne betwuh him selfum hu suiðe hie sculon beon geclænsode ða ðe berað on hira
Forðy wæs ðurh ða halgan stemne beboden ðætte on Arones breostum sceolde beon awriten sio
Forðæm he bið gesett to bisene oðrum monnum, simle he sceal ætiewan on his lifes
On ðæm selfan hrægle, ðe he on his breostum wæg, wæs eac awriten ða naman ðara twelf
Ðonne birð se sacerd suiðe untællice awriten ðara fædra naman on his breostum, ðonne he
Ðonne stæpð se sacerd suiðe tælleaslice on ðone weg, ðonne he ða bisene ðara forðgefarenra
Suiðe ryhte ðæt hrægl is gehaten, ðæt se sacerd beran sceolde ðæs domes racu, forðam se sacerd
Be ðam is awriten ðæt mon sceolde writan on ðæm hrægle ðe Aron bær on his breostum, ðonne he
Ða domas he bær on his breostum beforan Gode Israhela bearna simle.
Sua sceal se sacerd giet simle beran ða domas awritene on his breostum Israhela bearna, ðæt is ðæt
& ðeah for ðære geornfulnesse ðære ryhtinge ne sie he to hræd ne to stið to ðære wrace, ac ðonne
Ac gif he geðencð ðone ege ðæs deman ðe ofer eall sitt, ðonne ne stierð he no his hieremonnum
Ac se ege ðonne he geeaðmed ðæt mod he hit geclænsað, ðylæs sio gedyrstignes his modes hine
Ac hit is micel ðearf ðæt mon hire suiðe hrædlice wiðbregde, ðylæs sio scyld ðe hiene ðurh
Forðon gif hio ne bið hrædlice aweg adrifen, he bið ofslægen mid ðæm sueorde ðære geðafunge.
Hu se lareow sceal beon on his weorcum fyrmest.
Se lareow sceal bion on his weorcum healic, ðæt he on his life gecyðe lifes weg his hieremonnum,
He bið genied mid ðæm folgoðe ðæt he sceal healice sprecan;
Geðence he ðonne ðæt him is efnmicel nied, siððan he hit gesprecen hæfð, ðæt he eac sua doo sua
Ðæt is ðæt he sprecende bebiet ðæt he ðæt wyrcende oðiewe, ðæt hit ðurh ðone fultum sie
Bi ðæm wæs gecueden ðurh ðone witgan: ðu ðe wilt godspellian Sion, astig ofer heane munt.
Ðæt is ðætte se sceal, se ðe wile brucan ðara godcundra ðinga & ðara hefonlicra lara, forlætan
Sua micle he mæg ieð his hieremenn geteon to beteran, & he bið sua micle sel gehiered sua he
Forðam bebiet sio halige æ ðæt se sacerd scyle onfon ðone suiðran bogh æt ðære offrunge, & se
Ðæt ðonne tacnað ðæt ðæs sacerdes weorc sculon beon asyndred from oðerra monna weorcum.
Nalles na ðæt an ðæt he god doo gemang oðrum monnum, ac eac synderlice sua suæ he on
Eac him mon scolde sellan ða breosð ðæs neates toeacan ðæm boge, ðæt is ðæt he geleornige ðæt
Nalles na ðæt an ðæt he on his breostum ðence ðætte ryht sie, ac eac ða spone ðe his ðeawa
Ne wilnige he nanes eorðlices ofer ðæt, ne he him ne ondræde nanne eorðlicne ege ðyses
Forðon ðurh ða uplecan stefne wæs beboden on ðære æ ðæt se sacerd scolde beon fæste bewæfed
Ðæt is ðæt he bio simle gestrymed & gefrætwod wið ælce frecenesse ge gæstlice ge mennisclice &
Forðæm ðonne he higað to ðæm godcundum ðingum anum, ðæt he ne ðyrfe an nane healfe
Gif ðonne mid nanum ðissa ne bið onwæced his inngeðonc, ðonne bið hit swutul ðæt he bið suiðe
Ðæt hrægl wæs beboden ðæt scolde bion geworht of purpuran & of tweobleom derodine & of
Æresð alra glengea & ymesð scolde scinan gold on his hrægle.
Ðæt is ðætte on his mode scine ealra ðinga fyrmesð ongit wisdomes.
Toeacan ðæm golde ealra glenga fyrmesð on his hrægle wæs beboden ðæt scolde bion se giem
Se ðonne tacnað ðæt eall ðætte ðæs sacerdes ondgit ðurhfaran mæge, sie ymb ða hefonlican lufan,
Eac ðæm golde & ðæm line wæs ongemang purpura, ðæt is cynelic hrægl, forðæm hit tacnað
Be ðæm geðence se sacerd, ðonne he oðre men healice lærð, ðæt he eac on him selfum healice
& geðence he simle sie sua æðele sua unæðele suæðer he sie ða æðelu ðære æfterran acennesse,
Be ðæm æðelum ðæs gæstes Petrus cuæð: Ge sint acoren kynn Gode & cynelices preosthades.
Bi ðæm anwalde, ðe we sculon ure unðeawas mid ofercuman, we magon beon getrymede mid
Ða medomnesse ðære strengio se salmscop ongeat, ða he cuæð: Dryhten, suiðe suiðe sint
Forðæmðe ðæt mod ðinra haligra bið aðened suiðe healice & suiðe stranglice to ðe, ðonne ðonne
On ðæs sacerdes hrægle wæs toeacan golde & iacincðe & purpuran, dyrodine twegera bleo.
Ðæt tacnað ðætte eal ða god & ða mægenu ðe heo doð beon gewlitegode mid ðære lufan Godes &
Sio lufu ðonne hio lufað ætsomne ægðer ge God ge his niehstan, hio scinð suiðe smicere on twæm
Se ðonne se ðe sua higað ealneweg to andweardnesse his scippendes, & agiemeleasað ða
Ac ðonne ðæt mod bið aðened on ða lufan ægðer ge Godes ge his niehstena, ne bið hit ðonne
Forðon is toeacan ðæm twiblion godwebbe ðæt scyle beon twiðræwen twin on ðæm
Of ðære eorðan cymeð ðæt fleax, ðæt bið hwites hiwes.
Hwæt mæg ðonne elles beon getacnod ðurh ðæt fleax butan lichoman clænnes, sio sceal scinan of
Forðæm bið gefæsðnod ðæt geðræwene twin to ðæm wlite ðæs mæssehrægles, forðam sio
Hu se lareow sceal bion gesceadwis on his swigean & nytwyrðe on his wordum.
Sie se lariow gemetfæsð & gescadwis & nyttwyrðe on his wordum, ðætte he ne suigige ðæs ðe
Forðæm sua sua unwærlicu & giemeleaslicu spræc menn dweleð, sua eac sio ungemetgode suige
Oft eac ða unwaran lareowas for ege ne durron cleopian, ondrædað him sumra monna unðonc, ne
Be ðæm sio Soðfæsðness cuæð: Ne healde ge mid suelcum eornoste ða heorde suelce hirdas
Ðæt ilce oðwat Dryhten ðurh ðone witgan, ða he cuæð: Dumbe hundas ne magon beorcan.
Ond eft he cidde, ða he cuæð: Ne come ge no togenes minum folce ðæt ge meahton standan on
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt he fare togeanes Israhela folce him mid to feohtanne ðæt he wiðstande mid his
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt he him mid feohte on ðæm dæge ðe him niedðearf sie, ðæt he wiðstonde ealle
Be ðæm wæs eft gecueden to ðæm scyldegan folce: Eowre witgan eow witgodan dysig &
Ða godan lareowas beoð oft gemenmede on halgum gewritum wietgan, forðæm hie gereccað ðis
Ða ðonne ðe sio godcundde stefn ðreade, & cuæð ðæt hie scolden leasunga witgian, ðæt sindon ða
Ðæt word ðære ðreaunge is cæg, forðæm hit oft anlycð & geopenað ða scylde ðe se him self ær
Be ðæm cuæð Paulus ðæt se lareow sceolde beon miehtig to tyhtanne on halwende lare, & eac to
Eft wæs gecueden ðurh Zacharias: Sio æ sceal beon soht on ðæs sacerdes muðe, & his weleras
Forðam myndgode Dryhten ðurh Essaiam ðone witgan & cuæð: Cleopa & ne blin, hefe up ðine
Forðæm se se ðe ðone sacerdhad onfehð, he onfehð friccan scire & foreryneles ða her iernað
Sua sculun ða sacerdas nu faran hlydende & bodiende beforan ðæm egeslican deman ðe him
Gif ðonne se sacerd bið ungerad ðæs lareowdomes, hwæt forstent ðonne his gehlyd?
Hwæt mæg he bodigean ma ðonne se dumba fryccea?
Ac for ðeosum wæs geworden ðæt se halega gæsð on tungena onlicnesse gesette ofer ða
Forðæm wæs beboden Moyse ðæt se sacerd scolde bion mid bellum behangen.
Ðæt is ðæt he hæbbe ða stefne ðære lare, ðylæs he abelge mid ðære suigean ðone dom ðæs
Hit is awriten ðæt he scolde inngongende & utgongende beforan Gode to ðam halignessum beon
Hit is gecueden ðæt se sacerd scolde sweltan, gif se sueg nære of him gehiered ægðer ge
Hit wæs awriten ðæt ðæs sacerdes hrægl wære behongen mid bellum.
Hwæt elles getacnað ðæs sacerdes hrægl butan ryht weorc?
Dauið se witga ðæt cyðde, ða he cuæð: Sien ðine sacerdas gegierede mid ryhtwisnesse.
On ðæs sacerdes hrægle wæron bellan hangiende.
Ðæt is ðæt ða weorc ðæs sacerdes & eac se sueg his tungan clypien ymb lifes weg.
Ac ðonne se lareow hine gegearwað to ðære spræce, behalde he hine geornlice ðæt he wærlice
Forðon gif he unendebyrdlice onet mid ðære spræce, & wilnað ðæt he ðy wisra ðynce, ðonne is
Be ðæm sio Soðfæsðnes cuæð: Habbað ge sealt on ieow, & sibbe habbað betweoh iow.
Ðurh ðæt sealt is getacnod ða word wisdomes.
Se ðonne se ðe fundige wislice to sprecanne, ondræde he him suiðlice, ðylæs his spræc gescynde
Be ðæm cuæð Paulus: Ne wilnien ge mare to witenne ðonne iow ðearf sie, ac witað ðæt ðæt iow
Be ðæm wæs gecueden mid ðære godcundan stefne ðæt on ðæs sacerdes hrægle scoldon hangigan
Hwæt elles is getacnod ðurh ða readan apla buton sio anmodnes ryhtes geleafan?
Sua se æppel bið betogen mid anfealdre rinde, & ðeah monig corn oninnan him hæfð, sua sio
Forðæm geðence se lariow ðæt he unwærlice forð ne ræse on ða spræce.
Ymbe ðæt ðe we ær spræcon sio Soðfæsðnes ðurh hie selfe cleopade to ðæm apostolum, & cuæð:
Sio anlicnes wæs gecueden ðæt sceolde bion on ðæs sacerdes hrægle ða readan appla ongemang
Ðæt is ðætte ðurh eall ðæt ðæt we ær spræcon sie underfangen & wærlice gehealden sio
Se lareow sceal mid geornfullice ingehygde foreðencean na ðæt an ðætte he ðurh hine nan woh
Be ðæm wæs suiðe wel gecueden ðurh Moyses ðætte se wer se ðe ðrowude oferflownesse his
Sua eac ða word ðære lare beoð sæd, & hi gefeallað on ða heortan ðe hiera hlyst, sua nytt sua
Ðurh ða earan ða word bioð onfangen, & on ðæm mode hie beoð acennedu ðurh ðæt ondgiet.
Forðæm heton woroldwise menn wordsawere ðone æðelan lareow Paulus.
Se ðonne se ðe ðolað flowednesse his sædes he bið unclæne gecueden.
Sua eac se ðe oferspræce bið, he bið nohte ðon læs mid ðære besmiten.
Gif he ðonne endebyrdlice his spræce forðbringð, ðonne mæg he cennan mid ðam ðæt tuder ryhtes
Gif ðonne unwærlice sio lar toflewð ðurh oferspræce, ðonne bið ðæt sæd unnnyt agoten, næs to
Be ðam Paulus cuæð, ða he manode his cneoht ðæt he scolde standan on ðære lare, he cuæð: Ic
Ðeah he cuæde ungedæftelice, he cuæð ðeah ær gedæftelice, forðæm sio ofersmeaung mirð ða
Hu se lareow sceal bion eallum monnum efnðrowiende & foreðencende on hiora earfoðum.
Ac sie se lareow eallum monnum se niehsta & eallum monnum efnðrowiende on hira gesuincum,
For ðysum wæs geworden ðætte Paulus, ðeah ðe he wære gelæded on neorxna wong he arimde ða
& doo ðæt wif ðæm were ðæt hio him mid ryhte doon sceal, & he hire sua some, ðylæs hie on
& hwene æfter he cuið: Ne untreowsige ge no eow betweoxn, buton huru ðæt ge eow gehæbben
Loca nu hu se halega wer, se ðe sua fæsðlice geimpod wæs to ðæm hefenlicum diogolnessum, &
& sua suiðe sua he wæs upahæfen to ðæm ungesewenlicum, he ðeah gehwyrfde his heortan eage,
Hefonas he ðurhfor mid his modes sceawunga, & suaðeah ðone ymbhogan ne forlet ðæs
Forðæm he wæs gefeged mid ðære lufan Godes & monna ægðer ge to ðam hiehstum ðingum ge
He wæs on himselfum mid ðæs halgan gæstes mægene suiðe healice upabrogden, & ðeah
Forðæm he cuæð: Hwa bið geuntrumod ðæt ic ne sie eac geuntrumod;
Oððe hwa bið gesciended ðæt ic eac ðæs ne scamige?
Eft he cuæð be ðæm ilcan: ðonne ic wæs mid Iudeum ic wæs suelc hie.
Ne cuæð he ðæt forðyðe he wolde his treowa & his geleafan forlætan suæ suæ hie, ac he wolde
& eft he cuæð: ðeah we nu ofer ure mæð ðencen & smeagean, ðæt we dooð for Gode;
Ðonne we hit eft gemetlæcað, ðonne doð we ðæt for eow.
He ongeat ðæt he oferstag hine selfne on ðære sceawunge ðære godcundnesse, & eft hine selfne
Be ðæm eac Iacobus se heahfæder, ða he smirede ðone stan ðe æt his heafdum læg to tacne ðæt
He geseah ane hlædre standan æt him on eorðan.
Oðer ende wæs uppe on hefenum, & æt ðæm uferran ende Dryhten hlinode, & englas stigon up
Forðæm ða godan lareowas upsceawiende no ðæt an wilniað secean & sceawian ðæt halige
Forðæm Moyses oft eode inn & ut on ðæt templ, forðæm he wæs ðærinne getogen to ðære
Ðærinne he sceawode on his mode ða diogolnesse ðære godcundnesse, ond ðonon utbrohte ðæm
& symle ymb ðæt ðe hine ðonne tueode, ðonne orn he eft innto ðæm temple, & frægn ðæs
He astealde on ðæm bisene ðæm reccerum ðe nu siendon.
Hie sculon, ðonne hie ymb hwæt tueoð ðæs ðe hie ðærute don sculon, cierran eft to hira agnum
Gif hie ðonne giet ðær tueonað, gongen ðonne to ðæm halgan gewritum, frine ðara hwæt hie don
Forðæm sio Soðfæsðnes self, ðæt is Krisð, ða he on eorðan wæs, he hine gebæd on muntum &
Forðæm ðonne sio lufu for mildheortnesse nieðerastigeð, & hio hie geðied to his niehstena
& sua micle sua hio estelicor ofdune astigeð, sua hio ieðelicor upastigeð;
Suelce hie sculon hie selfe atiowan, ða ðe oðrum, fore beoð, ðætte ða ðe him underðiedde sien
Forðæm ðonne ða yða ðara costunga ða synfullan ðrowiað, ðæt hi mægen iernan & fleon to ðæs
Forðæm eac wæs ðæt ðe beforan ðæm temple stod æren ceac onuppan twelf ærenum oxum, ðætte
Se ceac wæs sua micel ðæt he oferhelede ða oxan ealle, buton ða heafudu totodon ut.
Hwæt getacniað ðonne twelf oxan buton ða XII apostolas, & siððan ealle ða endebyrdnesse ðara
Bi ðon wæs gecueden on ðære æ: Ne forbinden ge na ðæm ðyrstendum oxum ðone muð.
Ðone cwide Paulus geryhte eft to biscepum ðara openlican weorc we gesioð, ac we nyton hwelc
Ða ðeah ðonne hi niðerastigað to aðweanne hiera niehstena scylda, ðonne hie him ondettað, hie
Ðætte sua hwelc sua inweard higige to gangenne on ða duru ðæs ecean lifes, he ðonne ondette
Ond suæ suæ ðara monna honda & fet wæren aðwægene on ðære ealdan æ on ðæm ceake
Oft eac gebyreð ðonne se scrift ongit ðæs costunga ðe he him ondetteð ðæt eac self bið mid
Hwæt ðæt wæter on ðæm ceake wæs gedrefed, ðonne ðær micel folc hiera fet & honda on
Sua bið ðæs sacerdes mod ðonne ðær bið micel folc on aðwægen hira scylda ðurh his lare.
Ðonne he underfehð ðæt fenn ðara ðweandra, him ðyncð suelce he forleose ða smyltnesse his
Ac nis ðæt na to andrædanne ðæm hirde, forðæmðe Dryhten hit eall swiðe smealice geðencð, &
Hu se reccere sceal bion ðæm weldondum monnum for eaðmodnesse gefera & wið ðara yfelena
Se ealdormonn sceal lætan hine selfne gelicne his hieremonnum: he sceal bion hira gefera for
& ðeah ðonne he ongiete ða scylda ðara ðweortiemena, ðonne geðence he ðone ealðordom his
& eft ongean ða godan & ða wellibbendan forsio he his ealdordom suæ suiðe ðæt he on allum
& eft wið ða wiðerweardan ne ondræde he ðæt he begonge his ryhtwisnesse, suæ suæ ic geman
Ic cuæð ðæt æghwelc monn wære gelice oðrum acenned, ac sio ungelicnes hira geearnunga hie
Hwæt ðonne ða ungelicnesse ðe of hira unðeawum forðcymeð, se godcunda dom geðencð ðætte
Forðæm ealle ða ðe fore oðrum bieon sculon ne sculon hi na sua suiðe ne sua oft geðencean
& ne gefeon hie na ðæt hie ofer oðre menn bion moten sua suiðe sua ðæs ðæt hie oðrum monnum
Hwæt hit is gesæd ðæt ure ealdan fæderas wæron ceapes hierdas.
Ond eac Dryhten cuæð to Noe & to his bearnum: Weahsað ge & monigfaldiað & gefyllað eorðan,
Ne cuæð he no ofer oðre menn ac ofer nietenu, ða he wæs forboden ofer menn, ða he wæs aliefed
Se monn is on gecynde betera ðonne dysig nietenu, ac he ne bið na betera ðonne oðre menn.
Forðæm hit næs na gecueden ðæt hie ne scoldon oðre menn ondrædan, ac nietenu.
Forðæm hit is ungecyndelicu ofermodgung ðæt se monn wilnige ðæt hine his gelica ondræde, &
Forðæm ðonne ða lareowas ongitað ðæt ða ðe him underðiedde beoð him to hwon God
Ne ofermodgiað ða scirmenn na forðy, ðeah hi for ðyslicum wielnien ðæt hie andrysne sien,
& ofer ða hi sculon ricsian næs na sua ofer menn ac sua sua ofer nietenu, forðonðe hie be
Hie sculon forðy ofdrædde licgean astreahte oðrum monnum underðiodde sua sua nietenu.
For ðiosum ðonne oft gebyreð ðæt se reccere on his mode wirð upahæfen, & wierð aðunden on
Ond gif hwæt welgedones bið, ðonne cnodað him ðæt ealle ða ðe him underðiedde bioð mid
& gif he hwæt yfeles deð, ne wiðcuið ðam nan mann, ac herigað oft sua suiðe sua hie hit lean
& mid ðy wyrð ðæt mod besuicen & genæt mid ðæra olicunga ðe him underðiedde beoð ðæt he
Ond ðonne he bið utane ymbhringed mid ungemetlicre heringe, he bið innan aidlad ðære
Ac forsiehð ða ðe him underðiodde beoð, & ne mæg ongietan ða ðe him beoð on gecynde & on
& ðonne hine selfne sua healice upahefeð on sumum ðingum, ond suaðeah bið getieged to
Ac sua he wierð self to ðæs onlicnesse ðe awriten is ðæt he gesio ælce ofermetto, se is kyning
Se wilnode synderlices ealdordomes, & forsieh ða geferræddene oðerra engla & hira lif, ða he
Butan tweon ðonne se monn oferhyð ðæt he beo gelic oðrum monnum, ðonne bið he gelic ðæm
Sua sua Sawl Israhela kyning ðurh eaðmodnesse he geearnode ðæt rice, ond for ðæs rices
For eaðmodnesse he wæs ahæfen ofer oðre menn, ond fær ofermettum he wæs aworpen.
Dryhten ðæt gecyðde ða ða he cuæð: ða ðu ðe selfum ðuhtest unwenlic, ða ic ðe gesette eallum
Æresð him ðuhte selfum ðæt ðæt he wære suiðe unmedeme, ac siððan he understungen &
Forðæm he hine æthof from oðerra monna geferrædenne, ond hine dyde oðrum monnum sua
Forðy he ongeat ðæt he ma mehte ðonne ænig oðer, ða wende he ðæt he eac mara wære.
Ðæt wæs wunderlicu gemetgung ðætte ða ða he him selfum wæs lytel geðuht, ða wæs he Gode
Sua oft ðonne ðæt mod aðint on ofermettum for ðære menige ðæs folces ðe him underðied bið,
Ðone anwald mæg wel reccan se ðe ægðer ge hine habban cann ge wiðwinnan.
Wel hine recð se ðe conn wel stræc bion & ahæfen wið ða unryhtwisan & wið ða scyldgan &
Ac ðæt mennisce mod bið oft upahafen, ðeah hit mid nane anwalde ne sie underled;
Ac hu micle ma wenstu ðæt hit wolde, gif ða wlenca & se anwald ðær wære to gemenged.
& ðeah suiðe ryhte stihtað ðone anwald se ðe geornlice conn ongietan ðæt he of him gadrige ðæt
We magon eac fullecor ongietan & tosceadan ða spræce, gif we sceawiað ða biesene ðæs forman
Ðurh Godes giefe he onfeng ðone ealdordom ðære halgan ciericean, ond ðeah he wiðsoc ðæt hine
Ða ða Cornelius for eaðmodnesse wel dyde ðæt he hine astrehte beforan him, he ðeah hine selfne
Hu, ne iom ic monn sua ilce sua ðu?
Ac ða ða he ongeat ða scylde on Annanian & on Saffiram, suiðe hrædlice he oðiewde hu
Ðæt rice & ðone anwald he na ne angeat wið Cornelius, ða ða he hine sua suiðlice weorðian
He wolde him ætfæstan his eaðmetto, & mid ðy he geearnode ðæt he ðuhte ðæt he wære his
He cuæð to him ðæt he wære his gelica: ðær he gecyðde his eaðmodnesse;
Ond eft on Annaniam & on Saffiram gecyðde his nið & his onwald mid ðære wræce.
Ond eft sanctus Paulus ne ongeat he na hine selfne beteran oðrum godum monnum, ða ða he
Ac sint fultemend eowres gefean, forðamðe ge stondað on geleafan Suelce he openlice cuæde:
Eft he spræc suelce he nysse ðæt he a furðor wære ðonne oðre broðor, ða he cuæð: We sint
Ond eft he cuæð: We sint eowre ðeowas for Cristes lufan.
Ac ðonne he gemette ða scylde ðe he stieran scolde, hrædlice he gecyðde ðæt he wæs magister &
Ðæt he cyðde ða he cuæð on his epistolan to Galatum: Hwæðer wille ge ðæt ic cume to eow, ðe
Suelce he cuæde: Hwæðer ic cume ðe mid ege ðe mid lufe?
Ðonne bið ðæt rice wel gereht, ðonne se ðe ðærfore bið suiðor wilnað ðæt he ricsige ofer monna
Ac ðonne ða ealdermenn ðreageað ða scyldgan, ðonne is him micel ðearf ðæt hie geornlice
Ond ðeahhwæðre, ðylæs he his eaðmodnesse forleose, geðence he ðæt he bið self suiðe gelic
Ond eac we magon suigende geðencean on urum inngehygde, ðeah we hit ne sprecen, ðæt hie
Ac eft ðonne we selfe gesyngiað, ne ðreað us nan monn, ne furðum ane worde ne tælð.
Forðam we beoð mid Gode sua micle suiðor gebundne sua we for monnum orsorglicor
Ac ðonne we ure hieremenn lærað & ðreageað, sua micle ma we hie gefreogað æfter ðam
Ond suaðeah on ðære heortan is a sio eaðmodnes to healdanne & eac on weorcum to læranne;
& betuh ðæm twæm is eallenga to geðencenne ðæt we to ungemetlice ða eaðmodnesse ne
Ðone ealdordom & ðæt riceter ðe se reccere for monigra monna ðearfe underfehð he hine sceal
Eahtige he hine selfne on his inngeðonce suelcne suelcne he ondrætt ðæt he sie.
Ond ðeah hit on sumum ðingum getacnad sie ðæt he hwelc gerisenlic wundor wyrcean mæge,
Ða ðe ofer oðre bioð giemen hie geornlice ðætte sua micle sua hira onwald bið mara gesewen
Bi ðam wæs suiðe ryhte gecueden ðurh sumne wisne monn, he cuæð to ðæm oðrum: To
Ond eft be ðam ilcan cuæð sanctus Petrus: Ne sint we nane waldendas ðisses folces, ac we sint
Beðæm ilcan eft sio Soðfæsðness, ðæt is Crist, ðurh hine selfne cuæð, ða he us speon to ðæm
Ne sie hit ðonne na sua betweoxn eow, ac sua hwelc sua wille betweoxn eow fyrmest beon, se
Sua sua monnes sunu, cuæð Crist be him selfum, ne com he na to ðam on eorðan ðæt him mon
For ðeosun illcan is eac gesæd on ðæm godspelle hwelc wite scolde ðrowian se upahafena ðegn
He cuæð ðonne: Se yfela ðeow cuið on his mode: Hit bið long hwonne se hlaford cume;
Ic mæg slean & ierman mine heafodgemæccan.
Itt him ðonne & drincð mid ðam druncenwillum monnum, ond læt his hlafordes gebod to
Ðonne cymð his hlaford on ðæm dæge ðe he ne wenð, ond on ða tiid ðæt he hine ær nat;
Hæfð hine ðonne siððan for ænne licettere.
& suiðe ryht deð for ðære licettunge ðe he licette ðæt he wolde habban ða ðenunga ðeawas &
Ond ða he ðæt hæfde, ða wolde he hit habban him to agnum anwalde, ond dyde him ðæt riceter
Ond suaðeah oft agyltað ða ealdormenn efnsuiðe on ðam ðe he bið to eaðmod ðam yflan
Suiðe ryhte se bið geteald to ðæm liceterum se ðe on lareowes onlicnesse ða ðenenga ðæs
Ond ðeah hwilum giet suiðor hie syngiað on ðam ðe hie healdað ma geferrædenne & efnlicnesse
Sua Heli se sacerd dyde.
He wæs mid leasre mildheortnesse ofersuiðed ðæt he nolde witnian his agne suna ða hie agylton,
Hit wæs onlicost suelce sio godcunde stemn to him cuæde: ðu weorðasð ðine suna ma ðonne me.
Ond eft ðurh ðone witgan wæs gecid hierdum, ða he cuæð: ðæt sceap ðæt ðær sceoncforad wæs
Se bringð ham ðone forlorenan se ðe mid geornfulnesse ðære hierdelican giemenne ðone ðe
Hwæt se foreda sconca bið gewriðen mid ðæm bende, sua beoð ða synna mid ðam lareowdome
Sua sua sio wund wile toberan, gif hio ne bið gewriðen mid wræde, sua willað ða synna
Ond suaðeah oft sio wund bið ðæs ðe wierse & ðy mare, gif hio bið unwærlice gewriðen, & him
Sua is eac ðearf ðæt se lareow, se bið saule læce, ðara synna wunde stirende gemetlice gewriðe
Ond eac him is to giemenne ðæt he ætiewe his hieremonnum ðæt he sie hiera fæder & reccere
Sua sua we io cuædon on ðeawa bocum be Iobe ðæt ægðer wære unnyt ge mildheortnes ge steor,
Forðæm scel bion on ðæm reccere ðæt he sie ryhtlice & mildheortlice rædende his hieremonnum
For ðioson ilcan wæs ðætte sio Soðfæstnes self cuæð, ðæt is Crist, ða he lærde ðurh ða tielunga
Witodlice ðæt win slit ða wunde, & se ele hie gesmeð & gehælð.
Ðis is ðearf ðæt se ðe wunde lacnigean wille giote win on, ðæt sio reðnes ðæs wines ða
Sua eac ðam lareowe is to monianne ða lieðnesse wið ða reðnesse, & of ðam gemonnge wyrce
Suiðe wel ymb ðæt tacnað sio earc on ðære ealdan æ.
On ðære wæron ða stænenan bredu ðe sio æ wæs on awriten mid tien bebodum, & wac sio gierd
Sua eac, gif ðara haligra gewrita &git bið on ðam breostum ðæs godan recceres, ðonne sceal
Ðæt is ðæt he ðreage his hiremenn.
& eac sceal bion on ðæm breostum ðæs monnan suetnes.
Ðæt is ðæt he him sie lieðe.
Be ðiosum illcan cuæð Dauið to Gode: ðin gierd & ðin stæf me afrefredon.
Mid gierde mon bið geswungen, & mid stæfe he bið awreðed.
Gif ðær ðonne sie gierd mid to ðreageanne, sie ðær eac stæf mid to wreðianne: sie ðær eac lufu,
Sie ðær eac reðnes, næs ðeah to stið;
Sie ðær eac onda, næs ðeah to ungemetlice grim;
Sie ðær eac arfæsðnes, næs ðeah wandigendre ðonne hit gedafenlic sie;
Ðætte ðonne sio ryhtwisnes & sio mildheortnes hi gegadrige on ðæm anwalde ðæs recceres, &
Hu se lareow ne sceal ða inneran giemenne gewanian for ðære uterran abisgunge, ne eft ða
Ne forlæte se reccere ða inneran giemenne ðæs godcundan ðiowdomes for ðære abisgunge ðara
Ðylæs he sie gehæft mid ðam uterran, oððe eft mid ðam inneran anum abisegad, ðæt he ne
Monige ðeah nyllað na geðencean ðæt hi beoð oðrum broðrum ofergesett, & him fore bion
Ac mid ealre heortan geornfulnesse begongað ða woroldcundan giemenne, & fægniað ðæs ðæt
& ðonne, ðonne hie hie ne habbað, dæges & niehtes hie fundiað to bigietenne, & beoð suiðe
Ac ðonne him eft gelimpð ðæt hi æmtige beoð ðære scire, ðonne bioð hie suiður on hira mode
Forðæm ðæt wære his willa ðæt he moste ymb swincan, ond ðyncð him gesuinc ðæt he bið
& sua hit gebyreð, ðonne he fægnað ðæt he sie abisgod mid woroldðingum, ðæt he ne conn oðre
Forðon aðreat ða hieremenn ryhtes lifes, ðonne hie wilniað gæstlice libban, be ðæm yfelum
Ðonne ætspornað hie, & weorðað mid ðæm ascrencte.
Sua eac ðær ðæt heafod bið unhal eall ða limu bioð idelu, ðeah hie hale sien, sua eac bið se here
Sua eac ðonne se biscep begæð ða ðeninga ðe eorðlice deman sceoldon, ðonne ne tyht nan mon
Forðy ne magon ða hieremenn begietan ðæt leoht ðære soðfæstnesse, forðæm ðonne sio
Forðæm suiðe ryhtlice se Aliesend monna cynnes, ða he us stierde urra womba oferfylle, he
& eac he geicte ðærto ege, ða he cuæð: ðylæs eow hrædlice on becume se færlica domes dæg.
Ðæs dæges tocyme hwelc he beo he cyðde, ða he cuæð: He cymð sua sua grin ofer ealle ða ðe
Ond eft he cuæð: Ne mæg nan mon twam hlafordum hieran.
Ond eac cuæð Paulus, ða ða he wolde arwierðra monna mod from ðisses middangeardes
Ða ða he lærde ðæt ðære ciricean ðegnas scoldon stilnesse ðære ðenunga habban, ða lærde he hi
He cuæð: Gif ge ymb woroldcunde domas beon scylen, ðonne nime ge ða ðe on ðæm hirede
Suelce he openlice cuæde: Gedoð ðæt hie sien on ðæm oðrum nytte, gif hie on ðæm oðrum ne
Be ðæm eac Moyses, se ðe wæs Gode sua weorð ðæt he oft wið hine selfne spræc, æt sume
Forðæm ða hlafordas & ða recceras scoldon ðencean ymb ðæt helicuste, & ða underðioddan
Ða recceras sceolon bion beforan ðæm folce sua sua monnes eage beforan his lichoman, his weg
Ðonne is ðearf ðæt ðæt dust ðisse eorðlican giemenne ne aðisðrige ðæt eage ðæs recceres,
Hu gerades mæg ðonne se biscep brucan ðære hirdelican are, gif he self drohtað on ðam
Forðæm ryhtan edleane Dryhten ðreade ðurh ðone witgan, ða he cuæð: Suelc ðæt folc bið, suelc
Ðonne bið se sacerd suelc suelc ðæt folc bið, ðonne he ðæt ilce deð ðæt hie doð, & his on ða
Ðæt ongeat Heremias se witga, ða ða he suiðe sarlice weop, & spræc suelce ðæt templ wære eal
He cuæð: Eala, hwy is ðis gold adeorcad?
& ðæt æðeleste hiew hwy wearð hit onhworfen?
Toworpne sint ða stanas ðæs temples, & licggeað æt ælcre stræte ende.
Huæt tacnað ðonne ðæt gold ðe is sua diorwyrðe ofer eall ondweorc, buton ða heanesse ðæs
Oððe hwæt getacnað ðæt æðele hiew buton ða arwyrðnes ðære æfesðnesse, ðe eallum monnum
Hwæt getacniað eac ða stanas ðæs halgan huses buton ðone had ðære halgan endebyrdnesse?
Hwæt getacnað eac sio rume stræt butan ðone widan weg ðisses andwerdan lifes?
Be ðam ruman wege sio Soðfæsðnes, ðæt is Crist, ðurh hine selfne he cuæð: ðæt is suiðe rum
Ac ðonne bið ðæt gold asueartod, ðonne sio halignes monnes lifes bið mid eorðlicum weorcum
Ond ðonne bið ðæt æðeleste hiw onhworfen, ðonne se æht ðara godra weorca, ðe he ær beeode,
Ac ðonne hwelc æfter halgum hade hine selfne fæstlice geimpað on eorðlicum weorcum, ðonne
& ða giemmas ðara halignessa licggeað toworpne æfter stræta endum.
Ðonne licggeað ða giemmas toworpne æfter strætum, ðonne ða menn ðe hie selfe to ðære
Soðlice ða gimmas ðara halignessa to ðæm wæron gemacod ðæt hi scoldon scinan on ðæs
Ac ðonne ða sacerdas to æfæsðnessum & weorðunga ures Aliesendes ne bædað ða ðe him
Eac is to witanne ðæt he ne cuæð na ðæt ða giemmas wæren forsceadne æfter ðæm strætum, ac
Forðæm ðeah hie woroldcundlice drohtigen, hie wiliniað ðæt hie ðyncen ða betstan, ond ðeah
& suaðeah hwilum sint to geðafianne for niedðearfe ðas eorðlican tielunga, & næfre ðeah to
Ond suaðeah monige underfoð heorde, & ðeah wilniað ðæt hie beon freo & æmtige synderlice
Ða ðonne hie eallinga agiemeleasiað ðone ymbhogan woruldcundra ðinga, ðonne ne gefultumað
Forðæm wyrð oft forsewen ðara monna lar, ðonne hie tælað & hatigað hiera hieramonna
Forðæm ðæt word ðære lare ne mæg ðurhfaran ðæs wædlan heortan, gif he næfð ða are ðe he on
Ac ðonne grewð ðæt sæd suiðe wel ðara worda, ðonne sio mildheortnes ðæs lareowes geðwænð
Forðæm is niedðearf ðæm reccere ðæt he mæge & cunne oðerra monna inngeðonc giendgeotan &
Sua sculon ða hierdas weallan ymb ða geornfulnesse ðære inneran ðearfe his hieremonna, ðæt
Niede sceal bion gebrocen ðæt mod ðara hieremonna, gif se lareow & se hierde agiemeleasað
Be ðæm se forma hierde sanctus Petrus geornfullice monode, & cuæð: Ic, eower emnðeowa &
Suiðe hræðe æfter ðon he gecyðde hwæðer he mænde, ðe ðæs modes foster ðe ðæs lichoman, ða
Mid ðæm wordum fullice he us warode & lærde ðætte ðonne hie gefylden & gebeten ða wædle
Ðas ilcan geornfulnesse ðara hierda sanctus Paulus aweahte, ða he cuæð: Se ðe ne gimð ðara ðe
& suaðeah betuoxn ðissum simle is to ondrædenne & geornlice to behealdenne, ðonne hie ða
Forðæm oft ða heortan ðara reccera, sua sua we ær cuædon, ðonne hie mid ðissum hwilendlicum
Ac hit is ðearf ðætte sio giemen, ðe hie hira hiremonnum utan don scylen, sie wel gemetgod.
Be ðæm suiðe wel wæs gecueden to Ezechiele ðæm witgan ðætte ða sacerdas ne scoldon no
Suiðe ryhte ða sacerdas sint gehatene sacerdas, ðæt is on Englisc clænseras, forðæm hie sculon
Ðæt feax ðonne on hira heafde getacnað ða uterran geðohtas, ðæt grewð & scinð ofer ðæm
Ða giemen ðises andweardan lifes ðæt getacnað.
Sua giemeleaslice oft sceacað ure geðohtas from us, ðæt we his furðum ne gefredað, ðon ma ðe
Ond suaðeah ealle ða ðe for oðrum beon sculon, sculon habban giemenne ðissa uterrena ðinga,
Suiðe ryhte wæs ðæm sacerde forboden ðæt he his heafod sceare, & eac ðæt he his feax lete
Ðæt is ðæt he ealle ða geðohtas of his mode ne aceorfe ðe he scyle his hieremonnum to nytte
Be ðæm wæs suiðe wel gecueden ðæt se efsigenda efsode his heafod, ðæt is ðæt he sua
Ðylæs, ðonne ðæt lif ðæs lichoman bið gescilðed, ðæt innegeðonc sie gebunden to ðære heortan
Sua sindon ða loccas to sparienne ðæm sacerde ðæt hi ða hyde behelien, & ðeah ðæt he hie
Ðætte se reccere his godan weorc for gielpe anum ne do, ac ma for Godes lufan.
Betueox ðissum is micel ðearf ðæt se reccere geornlice wacige & ðence ðæt hine ne cnysse sio
Forðam, ðonne he geornlice ongiett ða inneran & ða gæstlican ðing on his ingeðonce, & suiðe
Ðylæs ðonne he mid godum weorcum bið underwreðed, & from woruldmonnum ongiten suelce
Se bið eallinga Godes gewinna se se ðe wilnað ðæt he hæbbe ða weorðunga for his godan
Hwæt we genoh georne witon ðæt se esne ðe ærendað his woroldhlaforde wifes, ðæt he bið
Ac ðonne ðæt selflice gegriepð ðæt mod ðæs recceres, & he wilnað ungemetlice licigean, ðonne
Ðonne bið ðæt mod awacod ðæs recceres, ðonne he gesihð ðæt his hieremen agyltað, & he nyle
Ac ðone gedwolan his hieremonna ðe he stieran sceolde he oft to suiðe geðafað, ðonne he ne
Be ðæm wæs suiðe wel gecueden ðurh ðone witgan: Wa ðæm ðe willað under ælcne elnbogan
Se legeð pyle under ælces monnes elnbogan, se ðe mid liðum oliccungum wile læcnian ða men
Ðonne bið se elnboga underled mid pyle & se hnecca mid bolstre, ðonne ðæm synfullan menn
Ðonne hine mon ne cnysð mid nanre reðnesse ne nanre wiðercueðnisse, ðonne geðafað him
Ac ða recceras ðe hira agnes gilpes giernað, ðæm hie geðafigað ðyllic ðe hie ondrædað ðæt him
Ac ða ðe hi wenað ðæt him nan wuht laðes ne wiðerweardes don ne mæge, ða hie suiðe stiðlice
Ond hie næfre bilwitlice willað monigean, ac hie ofergietað ðære hirdelican lufan, & egesiað hie
Ðas ðonne wæron ðurh ðone witgan suiðe ryhtlice geðreade mid ðære godcundan stefne, ða he
Ðæt is be ðæm ðe ma lufigeað hie selfe & hiera agenne weorðscipe ðonne hiera Hlafurdes.
Hie ðonne ahebbað hie ofer hiera hieremenn, & ðenceað a hwæt hie don mægen, & ne ðenceað
Ac suiðe scamleaslice gielpað ðisses hwilendlican onwaldes, & licað him ðæt hie ðæt unaliefede

Forðæm nan man nis ðe eallunga sua libban mæge ðæt he hwilum ne agylte.

Forðæm eac ða godan recceras, ðonne hie ne recceað hwæðer mon hie selfe synderlice &
Ðonne is suiðe micel ðearf ðæt we mid micle cræfte betueox ðissum gemetgien ða gemetgunge
Ond ðeah wel gedafonað ðætte ða godan recceras wilnigen ðæt hie monnum licigen, forðæm
Nalles forðæm anum ðe hie wilnigen ðæt hi mon synderlice lufige, ac swelce sio hira lufu si sum
Ac hit is ðeah suiðe earfeðdæde ðæt mon lustlice ðone lareow gehieran wille ðe mon ne lufað.
Forðon se ðe for oðre beon sceal, he sceal tilian ðæt he licige, forðæm ðæt he mæge beon
& ðeah ða his lufe ne sece he no for him selfum, ðylæs he sie ongieten ðæt he sie wiðerwinna
Ðæt suiðe wel sanctus Paulus geopenude, ða he us cyðde ða degolnesse his geornfulnesse, &
& suaðeah eft sona be cuæð: Gif ic monnum cueme & licige, ðonne ne beo ic no Godes ðeow.
Hwæt ðonne Paulus ægðer ge licode ge ne licode;
Forðæmðe on ðæm ðe he wilnode licigean, nals no he, ðeahðe he cuæde, ac ðurh hine he
Ðætte se reccere sceal geornlice wietan ðætte oft ða unðeawas leogað, & licettað ðæt hi sien gode
Eac sceal se reccere witan ðæt ða unðeawas beoð oft geliccette to godum ðeawum & to
Monig mon deð micel fæsten, & hæfð ðone hlisan ðæt he hit do for forhæfdnesse, & deð hit ðeah
Monig bið agieta his goda & wilnað mid ðy geearnigan ðone hlisan ðæt he sie rumgiful, & wenað
& oft eac ungemetlico forgifnes bið gelicet, ðæt mon weneð ðæt hit sie mildheortnes.
Ond oft eac ungemetlicu irsung bið gelicet, ðæt menn wenað ðæt hit sie ryhtwislic anda.
Oft mon bið suiðe rempende, & ræsð suiðe dollice on ælc weorc & hrædlice, & ðeah wenað
Oft mon bið suiðe wandigende æt ælcum weorce & suiðe lætræde, & wenað menn ðæt hit sie
Forðæm is micel niedðearf ðæt se reccere ða ðeawas & ða unðeawas cunne wel toscadan, ðylæs
Oððe eft se gielpna & se agita for his goda mierringe gielpe, & wene ðæt he sie kystig &
Oððe eft se ðafetere, se ðe wile forgiefan ðæt he wrecan sceolde, to ecum witum geteo his
Oððe eft se ðe ungemetlice wricð ða scylda, ðæt he self suiður on ðæm ne gesyngige.
Oððe eft ðæt he ryhtlice & stiðlice wrecan sceolde, ðæt he ðæt ne forielde, ðylæs se ryhtwislica
Hu gesceadwis se reccere sceal bion on his ðreaunga & on his oleccunga, & eac on his
Eac is to wietanne ðætte hwilum bið god wærlice to miðanne his hieremonna scylda & to
Hwilum eft smealice & geornlice to seccanne;
Hwilum liðelice to ðreatianne;
Hwilum suiðlice & stræclice to ðrafianne.
Monige sint, swa swa we ær cuædon, ðe mon sceal wærlice liccettan, & ðeahhwæðre eft cyðan,

Be ðære ildinge suiðe wel Dryhten ðreade Iudeas, ða he ðurh ðone witgan cuæð: Ge sindon
He ilde, & ðafode ða scylda, & ðeah he him gecyðde;
Ðeah ðe he wið ða scyldgiendan swugode, he hit him ðeah suigende gesæde.
Ac monige scylda openlice witene beoð to forberanne, ðonne ðæs ðinges tima ne bið ðæt hit
Swa se læce, ðonne he on untiman lacnað wunde, hio wyrmseð & rotað.
Forðæm buton he ðone timan aredige ðæs læcedomes ðonne bið hit swutol ðæt se lacnigenda
Ac ðonne se lareow ieldende secð ðone timan ðe he his hieremenn sidelice on ðreatigean mæge,
Be ðæm is swiðe wel gecueden ðurh ðone salmsceop, he cwæð: ða synfullan bytledon uppe on
He sarette ðætte ða synfullan sceoldon bytlan onuppan his hrycge, swelce he openlice cuæde:
Ac manegu diglu ðing sindon nearolice to smeageanne, ðætte se reccere mæge ongietan be
Be ðæm wæs suiðe ryhte to Ezechiele ðæm witgan gecueden: ðu monnes sunu, ðurhðyrela ðone
Ða ic ða ðone wah ðurhðyreludne hæfde, cuæð se witga, ða iewde he me ane duru beinnan ðæm
Ic ða eode inn, & geseah ðær ða anlicnessa eallra creopendra wuhta & ealra anscunigendlicra
Hwæt elles meahte beon getacnod ðurh Ezechiel buton ða scirmenn, & ðurh ðone wah seo

He cuæð: ða ic hæfde ðone weall ðurhðyrelod, ða geseah ic duru.

Be ðæm wæs suiðe wel gecueden: Gong inn, & geseoh ða heardsælða & ða sconde ðe ðas her
Ðæt is ðonne suelce he ingaa & geseo ða scande, ðonne he ongiet be sumum ðingum oððe
Forðæm wæs eac gecueden: Ic ða eode inn, & geseah ða anlicnessa ealra creopendra wuhta &
Ða creopendan wuhta getacnigeað ða eorðlican geðohtas.
Ða nietenu ðonne beoð hwæthwuguningas from eorðan ahæfen, & suaðeah onlutað to ðære
Ða creopendan & ða scnicendan licgeað mid ealle lichoman on eorðan.
Ða nietenu ðonne, ðeah hie maran sien, hie beoð suiður ahæfen from eorðan, & suaðeah for
Ða creopendan wuhta beinnan ðam wage getacniað ða ingeðoncas ðe wealcað in ðæs monnes
Ða nietenu ðonne ðe he geseah binnan ðæm wage getacnigeað ðonne mon hwæt ryhtlices &
Ac for ðære gewilnunga woroldgielpes & gietsunga he onlytt ungerisenlice to ðissum eorðlicum,
Eac wæs gesewen on ðæm wage atifred ealle ða heargas Israhela folces, & eac sio gietsung ðe
Suiðe ryhtlice hit wæs awriten æfter ðæm nitenum ðæt ða heargas wæron atiefrede, forðam ðeah
Forðy wæs suiðe wel gecueden ðæt hit wære atiefred, forðæm ðonne mon smeað on his mode
Eac is to wietanne ðæt æresð bið se wah ðurhðyrelod, & siððan mon wyrcð duru to.
Gif sio ðonne ontyned bið, ðonne mæg mon geseon gif ðær hwelc dieglu scond inne bið, sua se
Feorrane ðu meaht geseon, gif se wah bið ðyrel, ac ðu ne meaht geseon hwæt ðærinne bið
Sua ðu meaht ælcne unðeaw on ðæm menn æresð be sumum tacnum ongietan, hwæs ðu wenan

Forðæm ðæt ge eower mod gemetgien on ðæm niðe, ðonne ge eow selfum ondrædað ðæt ðæt ge

Hwæt tacnað ðonne Ezechhiel se witga buton ða lareowas, to ðæm is gecueden: Genim ðe ane
Ða halgan lareowas ðonne him nimað tigelan, ðonne hie ðara eorðlicra monna heortan underfoð
Ðonne hie lecgeað ða tieglan beforan hie, ðe him beboden wæs ðæt hi scolden ða ceastre

Be ðæm wæs suiðe ryhte gecueden: Berað hire to hlæd, & ymbsittað hie, & gað to mid rammum.

Eac he aræð ceastre wið Hierusalem, ðonne he ðæm ryhtlicum inngeðonce his hieremonna
& eac he bierð rammas ymbutan ðæt mod his hieremonna, ðonne he him gecyð mid hu

Be ðæm is git suiðe ryhtlice gecueden to ðæm witgan: Genim ðe ane iserne hierstepannan, &
Ðurh ða pannan is getacnod se wielm ðæs modes, & ðurh ðæt isern ðæt mægen ðara ðreatunga.

Oððe hwa bið gescended, ðæt me forðæm ne scamige?

Bi ðæm wæs suiðe ryhte gecueden to ðæm witgan: Sete iserne weall betuh ðe & ða burh.
Ða isernan hierstepannan he tæhte for iserne weall to settanne betuh ðæm witgan & ðære byrig,
