Ðæt ilce Dryhten God us bisnade ðurh Moysen, ða he cuæð: Gif hwa gonge bilwitlice mid his
To wuda we gað mid urum freondum sua oft sua we sceawiað ura hieremonna unðeawas;
& bilwitlice we heawað ðone wudu, ðonne we ðara gyltendra scylda mid arfæsððes ingeðonces
Ac sio æcs wint of ðam hielfe, & eac us of ðære honda, ðonne ðonne sio lar wint on reðnesse
Sio æcs wient of ðæm hielfe, ðonne of ðære ðreatunga gað to stiðlico word, & mid ðam his
Suaðeah ðæt geðreatade mod bið suiðe raðe gehwierfed to fiounga, gif him mon to ungemetlice
Ac se se ðe unwærlice ðone wuda hiewð, & sua his freond ofsliehð, him bið nidðearf ðæt he
Ðæt is ðæt he gehweorfe to hreowsunga, & sua fleo to ðara ðreora burga sumere, ðæt is tohopa
Se to anra ðara burga gefliehð, ðonne mæg he bion orsorg ðæs monnsliehtes;
Ðeah hine ðær meten ða niehstan ðæs ofslægenan, ne sleað hi hiene na: forðæm ðonne se ðearla
Hu swiðe se reccere sceal beon on his smeaunga abisgod ymb ða halgan æ.
Ac eall ðiss aredað se reccere suiðe ryhte, ðonne he for godes lufum & for Godes ege deð ðæt
Forðæm se eorðlica geferscipe hine tiehð on ða lufe his ealdan ungewunan, he sceal simle higian
Ac his mod bið suiðe ieðegende & suiðe abisgad mid eorðlicra monna wordum, forðam hit is
Ac him bið ðearf ðæt he hine genime simle be ðære leornunge haligra gewrita, & be ðam arise.
For ðiosum ðingum manade Paulus Timotheum his cniht, & cuæð: ðonne ic cume, ðonne beo ðu
& eft Dauit be ðam ilcan spræc, ða he cuæð: Loca, Dryhten, hu suiðe ic lufige ðine æ;
Ealne dæg ðæt bið min smeaung.
Eft bi ðys ilcan Dryhten bebead Moyse hu he scolde beran ða earce, ða he cuæð: Wyrc feower
& hat wyrcean twegen stengas of ðæm treowe, ðe is haten sethim, ðæt ne wyrð næfre forrotad;
& befoh utan mid golde: & sting ut ðurh ða hringas bi ðære earce sidan, ðæt hie man mæge
Ne tio hie mon næfre of.
Hwæt mæg ðonne elles seo earc tacnian buton ða halgan ciricean, on ðære sculon hangian ða
Ða saglas ðonne, ðe mon ða earce big beran sceal, sticiað eallne weg inn on ðam hringum ða
Sua sindon to seceanne stronge & unaðrotene lareowas & ðurhwuniende on ðære lare haligra
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt mon ða earce bere on ðæm saglum, ðætte ða godan lareowas ða halgan
Ða saglas is beboden ðæt scoldon beon mid golde befongne.
Ðæt is, ðonne ða lareowas mid wordum oðre menn lærat, ðæt hi eac selfe on hira agnum
Be ðam saglum is suiðe gesceadlice gecueden ðæt hie sculon simle stician on ðam hringum, &
Forðæm is eac gecueden ðætte simle ða ofergyldan saglas sceolden stician on ðæm gyldnum
Ðæt is ðonne ðonne ðara lareowa hieremenn hwæthwugu gæsðlices to him secað, & hi frinað,
Ac ðonne sticiað ða saglas suiðe singallice on ðæm hringum, ðonne ða lareowas simle on hira
Ond ðonne hi hebbað suiðe arodlice ða earce up, ðonne hi suiðe hrædlice bioð gearwe to læranne
Bi ðæm suiðe wel se forma hierde ðære halgan ciricean, ðæt is sanctus Petrus, manode oðre
Suelce he openlice cuæde: Ne bregden ge no ða stengas of ðæm hringum, ðylæs sio earc si
Hu micel scyle bion ðæt toscead, & hu mislice mon scyle menn læran mid ðæm cræfte ðæs
Nu ðonne oð ðiss we rehton hwelc se hierde bion sceal;
Nu we him willað cyðan hu he læran sceal, sua sua hit lange ær us ðære eadegan gemynde wer
Forðæm oft sio ilce lar ðe oðrum hielpeð, hio dereð ðæm oðrum;
Sua sua manegra cynna wyrta & grasu beoð gegaderad, þe neat batigað fore, sumu cuelað;
Sua sua mid liðre wisðlunga mon hors gestilleð, sua eac mid ðære illcan wistlunga mon mæg
Sua beoð eac monige læcedomas ðe sume adle gelytliað, & sume gestrongiað;
Sua eac hlaf ðe strongra monna mægen gemiclað, he gelytlað cilda.
For ðære ungelicnesse ðara hieremonna sculun beon ungelic ða word ðæs lareowes, ðæt he hiene
Hwæt cueðe we ðonne hwelce sin ða inngeðoncas monna buton suelce sumere hearpan strengas
Ealle he gret mid anre honda, ðy ðe he wile ðæt he anne song singen, ðeah he hie ungelice
Sua sceal æghwelc lareow to anre lufan & to anum geleafan mid anre lare & mid mislicum
On oðre wisan mon sceal manian weras, on oðre wif;
& on oðre wisan ealde, on oðre giong; & on oðre wisan earme, on oðre eadige;
& on oðre wisan ða bliðan, on oðre ða unrotan;
& on oðre wisan ða underðieddan, on oðre ða ofer oðre gesettan;
On oðre wisan ða ðeowas, on oðre ða hlafurdas;
& on oðre wisan ða woroldwisan, on oðre ða dysegan;
& on oðre wisan ða scamleasan, on oðre ða scamfæstan;
& on oðre wisan ða ofermodan, on oðre ða wacmodan;
Ond on oðre wisan ða ungeðyldegan, on oðre wisan ða geðyldegan;
& on oðre wisan ða welwillendan, on oðre ða æfstegan;
& on oðre wisan ða bilwitan, on oðre ða felaspræcan;
On oðre wisan ða halan, on oðre ða unhalan;
Ond on oðre wisan ða ðe for ege forberað ðæt hi yfel ne doð, on oðre wisan ða ðe sua
& on oðre wisan ða suiðe suigean, on oðre ða felaidelspræcæn;
& on oðre wisan ða slawan, on oðre ða ðe beoð to hrade;
& on oðre wisan ða manðuæran, on oðre ða grambæran;
& on oðre wisan ða eaðmodan, on oðre ða upahæfenan;
& on oðre wisan ða anwillan, on oðre ða ungestæððegan & unfæsðrædan;
& on oðre wisan ða ofergifran, on oðre ða fæstendan;
& on oðre wisan ða ðe mildheortlice hira agen sellað, on oðre ða ðe æfter oðerra monna ierfe
& on oðre wisan ða ðe nohwæðer ne oðerra monna ne reafiað, ne hiera agen rumedlice ne dælað;
& on oðre wisan ða ðe hira agen rumedlice sellað, & ne forlætað ðeah ðæt hie oðerra monna ne
& on oðre wisan ða ungemodan, on oðre ða gemodan;
& on oðre wisan ða wrohtgeornan ðe cease wyrceað, & on oðre ða gesibsuman;
Ond on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðe ða word ðære halgan æ ryhte ne ongietað, on oðre ða
Ond on oðre wisan ða ðe fullfremede ne beoð nohwæðer ne on ieldo ne on wisdome, & ðeah for
& on oðre wisan ða ðe ðisse hwilendlican are wilniað, & him nan gesuinc ne ðyncð ðæt hi hie
Ond on oðre wisan ða ðe beoð mid synscipe gebundene, on oðre ða ðe beoð frio ðara benda;
& on oðre wisan ða ðe ða ðurhtogenan synna wepað, on oðre ða ðe ða geðohtan wepað;
& on oðre wisan ða ðe ða ærgedonan wepað, & ðeah ne forlætað, on oðre ða ðe hi forlætað, &
& on oðre wisan ða ðe ða unaliefedan ðing doð, & hie eac herigað, & on oðre wisan ða ðe hie
& on oðre wisan ða ðe mid sumere unryhtwilnunga beoð færinga hrædlice ofersuiðede, on oðre
& on oðre wisan ða ðe ða lytlan scylda oftrædlice wyrceað, on oðre wisan ða ðe ða lytlan forgað,
& on oðre wisan ða ðe nan god nyllað onginnan, on oðre ða ðe hit onginnan willað, & næfre ne
& on oðre wisan ða ðe dearninga yfel doð, & god eawunga, & on oðre wisan ða ðe hira god helað
Hu nytt rehton we nu & rimdon ða cægan, buton we eac feawum wordum ætiewen hwæt hie
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to monianne weras, on oðre wif.
On oðre wisan sint to manianne weras, on oðre wif.
Ða weras mon sceal hefiglecor & stiðlecor læran, & ða wif leohtlecor;
Forðæm ðæt ða weras higigen to maran byrðenne, & ða wif mid oleccunga weorðen on
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to monianne ða iungan, on oðre ða ealdan.
On oðre wisan sint to læranne ða iungan, on oðre ða ealdan;
Forðæm oftor mid reðre manunga beoð ða iungan nytwyrðe gedone, & ða ealdan mid liðelicre
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða welegan, on oðre ða wædlan.
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða wædlan, on oðre ða welegan.
Ða wædlan sint to frefranne & to retanne, ðylæs hi sien to ormode for hira earfeðum.
Ða oðre sint to breganne, ðylæs hi sien for hiera wlencum to upahæfene.
To ðæm wædlan wæs gecueden ðurh Dryhten to ðæm witgan: Ne ondræd ðu ðe, forðæm ðu ne
& suiðe hræðe eac æfter ðæm he him olecte, ða he cuæð: ðu earma, ðu ðe eart mid ðy storme &
Ac sanctus Paulus ðreade ða welegan, ða he cuæð to his gingrum: Secgað ðæm welegum gind
Be ðæm we magon suiðe swutule oncnawan ðæt se eaðmodnesse lareow, ða ða he ymb ða
& eac we magon oncnawan ðæt, ðæt ða earman & ða untruman sient to retanne, & ða
Be ðæm Crist cuæð on his godspelle: Waa ieow welegum, ðe eower lufu eall & eower tohopa is
Ac ðæs is ðearf ðæt mon ðone frefre ðe on ðæm ofne asoden bið his iermða, & se is to
Ðætte ða sorgfullan ongieten ðæt him becumað ða welan ðe him gehatene sint, ðeah hi hi ðonne
& eac ða welegan ongieten ðætte ða welan ðe hie onlociað & habbað, ðæt hie ða habban ne
Ac ðæm lareowe is micel ðearf ðæt he ongiete hwa earm sie, hwa eadig, & hwone he læran scyle
Forðæm oft se welega & se wædla habbað sua gehweorfed hira ðeawum ðæt se welega bið
Forðæm sceal se lareow suiðe hrædlice wendan his tungan ongean ðæt ðe he ongiet ðæt ðæs
Ac sua micle liðelecor he sceal olecan ðæm welegan eaðmodan sua he ongiet ðæt he eaðmodra
& oft eac mon sceal ðone welegan ofermodan to him loccian mid liðelicre olicunga, forðæm ðæt
Forðæm oft hearda wunda beoð mid liðum beðengum gehnescode & gehælede, & eac ða
Ne sculon we eac forgietan hu hit wæs be Saule ðæm kyninge: ðonne him se wiðerwearda gæsð
Hwæt mæg ðonne elles tacnian Saules ungewitfullnes buton ða upahafenesse ðara welegena?
Oððe hwæt is elles getacnod ðurh Dauid buton eaðmodlic lif haligra monna?
Forðæm ðonne se unclæna gæsð becom on Saul, Dauid ðonne mid his sange gemetgode ða
Sua ðonne, ðonne ðæt mod ðara ricena for upahæfenesse bið to ierre gehwierfed, ðonne is cynn
Hwilum eac ðonne mon ðæm ricum cidan sceal, æresð mon sceal sprecan asciende, suelce he be
Ðonne mon ðonne ongiete ðæt he ryhte gedemed hæbbe, & he wene ðæt he ryht be oðrum
Forðæm com Nathan to cidanne ðæm cyninge Dauide, & licette, suelce he ymb sumes ðearfan
& eac se haliga monn ongeat ægðer ge ðæs cyninges scylde ge eac his hatheortnesse &
Sua se læce hyd his isern wið ðone monn ðe he sniðan wile;
Wenð, gif he hit him iewe, ðæt he him nylle geðafigean ðæt he hine sniðe.
Ac grapað suiðe fægre ymbutan ðæt ðæt he sniðan wile, & snið swiðe hrædlice.
Sua se witga dyde ðone cyning mid his wordum: ic wene ðæt he hine snide slaulecor, gif he him
Ðy hit wæs betre ðæt he grapude mid ðæm bispelle, ærðonðe he cidde, sua se læce grapað, &
Se læce, ðonne he cymð ðone untruman to sniðanne, æresð he sceawað ðæt cumbl, & siððan hine
Hyt ðonne his læceseax under his claðum oððæt he hine wundað: wile ðæt he hit gefrede, ær he
Forðæm he wenð, gif he hit ær geseo, ðæt he hit wille forsacan.
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða gladan, on oðre ða unrotan.
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða bliðan, on oðre ða unrotan.
Ðæm oferbliðum is to cyðanne ða unrotnessa ðe ðæræfter cumað, & ðam unbliðum sint to
Geliorngen ða bliðan on ðære ðreaunga ðæt hie him ondræden, & gehieren ða unbliðan ða lean
To ðæm bliðan is gecueden: Wa eow ða nu hliehað, forðam ge sculon eft wepan.
Gehieren eac ða unrotan ðone cuide ðe him is to gecueden ðurh ðone illcan lareow, ðæt is Crist,
Monige beoð ðeah bliðe & eac unbliðe ðara ðe for nanum woruldðingum nahwæðer doð, buton
Suaðeah is ðæm to cyðanne, ðæt hi hie warenigen ægðer ge wið ða ungemetlican blisse ge wið ða
Forðæm hira ægðer astyreð sumne unðeaw, ðeah hie ungewealdes cumen of ðæs lichoman
Ðæm oferbliðan oft folgað firenlusð, & ðæm unrotan ierre.
Forðæm is micel niedðearf ðæt mon hiene wið ðæt irre an & wið ða ungemetlican sælða
Ðonne is micel ðearf, ðonne him mon ðissa tuega hwæðer onðrætt suiður ðonne oðer, & wið ðæt
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to monianne ða ealdormen, on oðre wisan ða hieremenn.
On oðre wisan sint ðonne to manianne ða underðioddan, on oðre ða ofergesettan.
Ða underðieddan mon sceal sua læran ðæt hie elles ne sien genæt ne geirmed, & ða ofersettan
& ða underðioddan ðæt hie wiers ne don ðonne him man bebeode, ond ða ofergesettan ðæt hi
& ða unðerðieddan ðæt hi him eaðmodlice underlicgen, & ða ofergesettan ðæt hie gemetlice
Ðæm ofergesettan is to gecueden: Ne gremigen ge eowru bearn.
Geleornigen eac ða bearn ðæt hi sua hieren hira ieldrum sua sua hie selfe wieten on hira
Geleornigen eac ða fæderas & ða hlafurdas ðæt hie wel libbende gode bisene astellen ðæm ðe
Eac sculun wietan ða ofer oðre gesettan ðæt ðæt hie unaliefedes ðurhteoð, & oðre men bi ðam
Forðæm him is suiðe micel ðearf ðæt he sua micle wærlicor hine healde wið scylda swa he gere
Eac sint to manianne ða underðioddan & ða anlepan menn ðe æmtige beoð ðæs ðæt hie for oðre
Ða ofer oðre gesettan sint to manianne ðæt hie for hira monna gedwolan ne weorðen gedemde,
Se æmetiga & se anlipa is to manianne ðæt he sua micle sorgfulra sie ymb hine selfne, & sua
Ða ofergesettan sint to monianne ðæt hie sua oðerra monna giemenne gefyllen, ðæt hie hie selfe
Ac ðam ðe ðonne æmetig bið his agenne willan to wyrceanne, to ðæm is gecueden: ðu slawa, ga
Ða ðonne ðe ofer oðre bion sculon sint suiðe egeslice gemanode mid ðy worde ðe mon cuæð:
Hwelc magon beon maran gehat ðonne mon gehate for his freond ðæt he underfoo his saule on
Ðæt is suelce he hæbbe befæsð his hond oðrum menn, ðonne he gebint hine selfne to him mid his
Ðy him is micel ðearf, ðonne he tela lærð, ðæt he eac tela doo, & his lif on nan oðer ne wende,
Forðæm he eft sceal beforan ðæm ðearlwisan Deman mid gereccelicre rake gereccan ðæt he ðæt
Ond eft suiðe hraðe æfter ðæm se ilca Salomon cuæð: Do, min sunu, sua ic ðe lære: alies ðe
Forðæm ðu eart on borg began ðinum friend.
Ac iern nu & onette, awece hine.
Ne geðafa ðu ðinum eagum ðæt hie slapige, ne ne hnappigen ðine bræwas.
Sua hwa ðonne sua his lif to biesene bið oðrum monnum geset, ne sceal he no ðæt an don ðæt
Ne ðynce him no genog ðæt he ana wel libbe, buton eac ða ðe he fore beon sceal from ðære
Ðæt is suiþe wel ðær gecueden: Ne slapige no ðin eage, ne ne hnappigen ðine bræwas.
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt mon his eage læte slapian ðæt mon for his unwisdome & for his suongornesse
Ac ðonne hnæppiað ure bræwas, ðonne we hwæthwugu steorweorðes ongietað on ða ðe us
Ðonne hnappige we.
Ac ðonne we slapað fæste, ðonne we nohwæðer ne hit witan nyllað ne hit betan nyllað, ne
Ne slæpð se no fæsðe, ac hnappað, se ðe gecnawan mæg hwæt tælwierðe bið, & suaðeah for his
Æresð mon hnappað;
Gif he ðonne ðære hnappunge ne ge swicð, ðonne hnappað he oð he wierð on fæstum slæpe.
Sua eac oft gebyreð ðæm ðe for oðre menn beon sceal, ðonne he hwelc yfel ongiett, ðæt nyle
Ac ða sint to manianne ðe fore oðre beon sculan, ðæt hie geornlice ða ymb sion ðe hie ofer beon
Sua hit is cynn ðætte ða sien ðe fore oðre beon sculon, ðæt hie ægðer hæbben eagan innan ge
Ða underðioddan sint to manianne ðæt hie ðara unðeawas ðe him ofergesette bioð to suiðe & to
Ðylæs hie for ðære ryhtlæcinge weorðen upahæfene, & on ofermetto gewiten.
Ac hie sient suiðe georne to maniganne ðæt hi for hira unðeawum hie ne forsion, ne no ðy suiður
Ac gif hie hwæt sua healicra yfela on him ongieten ðæt hie hit niede sprecan scylen, ðonne don
Ac gif we nu onginnað reccan ongemong ðissum ymbe Dauides dæda sume, ðonne magon we ðis
Hit gelamp æt sumum cierre ðæt he wæs gehyd on anum eorðscræfe mid his monnum.
Ða Saul hine wolde secean uppe on ðæm munte, ða for he forð bi ðæm scræfe ðæt he oninnan
Ða gewearð hine ðæt he gecierde inn to ðæm scræfe, & wolde him ðær gan to feltune.
Ða wæs ðærinne se ilca Dauid mid his monnum, ðe longe ær his ehtnesse earfoðlice ðolade.
Ða cleopedon his ðegnas him to, & hine bædon, & geornlice lærdon ðæt he hine ofsloge.
Ac he him sona ondwyrde, & him suiðe stiernlice stierde, & cuæð ðæt hit no gedæfenlic nære
Hwæt tacnað us ðonne Saul buton yfle hlafurdas?
Oððe hwæt Dauid buton gode ðeowas?
Swa sua Saul elles ne meahte his wambe geclænsigan buton he to feltune eode, sua eac ne
Sua sua Dauid forbær ðæt he Saul ne dorste ofslean for Godes ege & for ðæm ealdum treowum,
Hie forberað æghwelce unryhte tælinge: sua sua Dauit forbær ðæt he ne slog mid his sueorde
Ac gif he ðonne eallunge forberan ne mæg for hira agnum unðeawum & for hiera
Sua sua Dauid cearf swiðe digellice suiðe lytelne læppan of Saules mentle his ealdhlafordes, sua
Ac gif hwæm gebyrige ðæt he for his agnum unðeawum on ða tælinge his hlafordes befoo,
Forðæm hit is awriten ðætte Dauid, ða he ðone læppan forcorfenne hæfde, ðæt he sloge on his
Swa scyle gehwelc mon forberan ðæt he mid ðæm sweorde his tungan his hlaford ne slea;
Ðæt is, ðeah he hine mid ryhte tælan mæge, ðæt he hit ne doo.
Gif him ðonne gewealdes gebyrige oððe ungewealdes ðæt on ðæs hwæt befoo ðe wið his willan
Forðæm ðonne we agyltað wið ða hlafordas, ðonne agylte we wið þone God ðe hlafordscipe
Be ðæm ilcan cuæð Moyses;
Ða he gehierde ðæt ðæt folc mænde to him Arone ymb hiera earfeðo, ða cuæð he: Hwæt is eower
Hwæt sint wit?
Wið God ge doð ðæt ge dooð.
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða hlafordas, on oðre wisan ða ðegnas eac ða ðeowas.
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðeowas, on oðre ða hlafordas.
Ða ðeowas sint to manianne ðæt hie simle on him hæbben ða eaðmodnesse wið hira hlafordas.
Ða hlafordas sint to manianne ðæt hie næfre ne forgieten hu gelic hira gecynd is, & hu gelice hi
Ða ðiowas sint to monianne ðætte hie hiera hlafordas ne forsion.
Hiera hlafordas hi forsioð, gif hie his willan & his bebodu forhyggeað.
Ðam hlafordum is eac to cyðanne ðætte hie wið Gode ofermodgiað for his agenre giefe, gif hie
Ðæm hlaforde is to cyðanne ðæt he ongiete ðæt he is efnðeow his ðeowe.
Ðæm ðeowan is beboden, & ðus to cueden: Beoð ge underðeodde eowrum woroldhlafordum.
& eft his is gecueden: ælc ðara ðe sie under ðæm gioke hlafordscipes, he sceal his hlaford
& eft hit is gecueden: Ge hlafordas, doð ge eowrum monnum ðæt ilce be hira andefne &
Geðencað ðæt ægðer ge hira hlaford ge eower is on hefenum.
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða dolan, on oðre ða wisan.
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðe ðisse worulde lotwrenceas cunnon, & ða lufigeað, on oðre
Ða lytegan sint to manianne ðæt hi oferhycggen ðæt hie ðær wieton, ða sarwisan sint to manianne
Ðæm lytegan is æresð to beleanne hiera selflice, ðæt hie ne wenen ðæt hie sien wiese.
On ðæm medwisan is to trymmanne swa hwæt sua hie ongietan mægen ðæs godcundan
Ac ymb ða lytegan we sculon suiðe suiðe suincan ðæt hie ðone wisdom forlæten ðe him selfum
Ne ðarf mon na ðone medwisan læran ðæt he ða lotwrencas forlæte, forðonðe he hie næfð.
Forðæm him is micle ieðre to gestieganne on ðone ryhtan wisdom ðonne ðæm lytegan sie to
Be ðysum illcan cuæð sanctus Paulus: Suelc eower suelce him selfum ðynce ðætte wisusð sie on
Be ðam medwisan is gecueden: Ne sculon ge beon to wise æfter ðæs lichoman luste.
& eft cuðæ Paulus: ða ðe woruldmonnum ðynceað dysige, ða geciesð Dryhten, forðæm ðæt he
& ðeah oft gebyreð ðæt ða bioð mid liðlicre race gehwyrfde, & eft ða medwisan oft mid bisenum
Ðæm lytegan ðonne is betere ðæt hie mid ryhtre race weorðen oferreahte & mid ðære race
Ðæm medwisan bið genog god ðæt he gecnawe oðerra monna weorc untælwierðe.
Be ðæm se æðela lareow sanctus Paulus, se sceolde læran ægðer ge wise ge unwise, ða he ongeat
& eft he cuæð to ðæm medwisan ða he ongeat ðæt hie mon mid sumum bisnum manian sceolde:
Ond eft cuæð Paulus: Gemunað eowerra foregengena ðara ðe eow bodedon Godes word, &
Forðon he ðus cuæð ðæt he ða lotwrenceas oferwunne & oferreahte;
& eac ða medwiisan to maran angienne mid ðære liðelican bisnunga gespone.
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða scamfæstan, & on oðre ða scamleasan.
On oðre wisan sint to læranne ða scamleasan, on oðre ða scamfæstan.
Ðæm scamleasan ne wyrð no gestiered butan micelre tælinge & miclum ðrean;
Ða scamfæstan beoð oft mid gemetlicre lare gebetrode.
Ða scamleasa nyton ðæt hie untela doð, buton hit mon him secge, & ðeah hit mon him secge, hie
Se scamfæsta hæfð genoh on ðæm to his bettrunge ðæt his lareow hine suiðe lythwon
Ðone scamleasan mon mæg ðy bet gebetan ðe hine mon suiður ðreað & sciend, ac be ðæm
Be ðæm Dryhten suiðe openlice tælde ða scamleasan Iudeas, & cuæð: Eower nebb sint sua
Ond eft he olehte ðam scamfæstan, ða he cuæð: ðære scame & ðære scande ðe ðu on iuguðe
& eft ða scamleasan Galathas suiðe openlice sanctus Paulus tælde, ða he cuæð: Eala ge
& eft he cuæð: Sua dysige ge sint ðætte ðæt ðæt ge gæsðlice underfengon, ge willað geendigan
Ða scylda ðara scamleasena he tælde, suelce he efnsuiðe him bære, & cuæð: Ic eom suiðe
Hit is god ðæt ge hit nu witon.
Næron naht æmetige, ðeah ge wel ne dyden.
Forðæm he spræc ðas word ðe he wolde ðara scamleasna scylda tælende geopenian, & ðara
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to monianne ða ofermodan & ða upahæfenan on hira mode, on oðre
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða modgan & ða fortruwodan, on oðre ða unmodgan & ða
Ða fortruwodan, ðonne hie him selfum to suiðe truwiað, hie forsioð oðre menn, & eac forcueðað.
Ða lytelmodan ðonne & ða unðristan, ðonne hie ongietað hiera unbældo & hiera unmiehte, hie
Ða modgan ðonne & ða fortruwudan, eall hiera agen ðæt hie synderlice ðenceað oððe doð hie
Ac ðæm lareowe is swiðe smealice to underseceanne be ðæm weorcum ðara ofertruwedena, ðæt
Swa we magon betst ða gedyrstigan gelæran ðætte , ðonne hie wenen ðæt hie hæbben betst
Hwilum eac, ðonne ða fortruwudan & ða anwillan wenað ðæt hie nane scylde ðurhtogen
Ða fortruwodnesse & ða anwilnesse an Corinctheum paulus ongeat suiðe wiðerweardne wið hine,
Sua ðætte sume cuædon ðæt hie wæron Apollan, sume cuædon ðæt hi wæron Saules, sume
Ac Paulus ða sona ða unclænan scylde beforan him alLum sæde, ðe an hiera ealra gewitnesse
He cuæð;
We gehierdon betueoxn eow unryhthæmed, ge sua unryht sua we furðum betwuxn hæðnum
Suelce he openlice cuæde: Hwæt wille ge for eowerre fortruwodnesse & for eowerre anwilnesse
Forðæmðe on eowerre towesnesse ge habbað gecyðed ðæt ge ures nanes ne siendon.
Ac ða lytelmodan & ða unðriestan we magon ðy ieð on ðæm wege gebringan godra weorca, gif
Oft we magon beon sua nyttran æt him, gif we hie myndgiað hira godna weorca, & ða secgeað,
Be ðam se ilcan Paulus cuæð, ða he ongeat ðæt folc ðe Salonicensa hatte, ðæt hie on his lare
Ða ongon he æresð herigean on him ðæt ðæt he fæsðrædes wiste, & sona æfter ðon suiðe liðelice
Ac sona æfter ðære liðelican spræce he cuæð: Ic eow healsige broður for ðæm tocyme Dryhtnes
Sua gedyde se soðfæsta lareow ðæt hie æresð gehierdon ða heringe ðe him licode forðæm ðæt
Ða he ongeat ðæt hie wæron onstryede mid ðæm wenan ðæt hi ðæs endes sua neah wendon, ða
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to monianne ða ungeðyldgan, & on oðre ða geðyldgan.
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ungeðyldgan, on oðre wisan ða geðyldegan.
Ðæm ungeðyldegum is to secganne ðæt hie ne agimeleasigen ðæt hi hira mod gebridligen, ðæt hi
Sua hit oft gebyreð ðæt sio hatheortness & sio hrædwilnes ðæt mod gebringð on ðæm weorce ðe
Forðæm him is to secgganne ðæt hie weorðað oft ascrencte on ðæm scyfe ðære styringe hira
Ac gif he ðonne ðære styringe ne wiðstent, ðonne gescient he ða godan weorc ðe he oft ær on
Hit is awriten on Paules bocum ðæt sio Godes lufu sie geðyld, & se ðe geðyldig ne sie, ðæt he
Forðæm for ðæm unðeawe ðære ungeðylde wirð utadrifen sio fostermodur ælcre leornunga &
Ne mæg he no ryhtlice geðyld læran, buton he self geðyldelice oðerra monna tionan geðolige.
Hwilum eac gebyreð for ðæm unðeawe ðære ungeðylde ðæt ðæt mod wierð gesticced mid ðære
Be ðam is awriten ðæt betera beo se geðyldega wer ðonne se gielpna, forðæmðe him bið leofre
Ac ðæm gielpnan bið leofre ðæt he secge on hine selfne gif he hwæt godes wat, ge ðeah he nyte
Ac he forlæt ðonne & towierpð eal ða godan weorc ðe he ær worhte, ðonne he forlæt ða
Forðæm wæs suiðe ryhtlice beboden Ezechiele ðæm witgan ðæt he scolde ðone Godes alter
Forðæm, gif se weobud ufan hol nære, & ðær wind to come, ðonne tostencte he ða lac.
Hwæt elles getacnað ðæt weobud buton ryhtwisra monna saula?
Forðæmðe nu eal ðæt se ryhtwisa to gode deð eal hit bið beorht to lacum beforan Godes eagum,
Hwæt tacnað ðonne ðæt holh on ðæm weobude buton godra monna geðyld?
Forðam, ðonne mon his mod geeaðmodgað ðæt he wiðerweardnesse & scande forbere, ðonne
Holh wæs beboden ðæt sceolde beon on ðæm weobude uppan, forðæm ðæt wind ne meahte ða
Ðæt tacnað ðæt ðæt geðyld sceal gehealdan ðara gecorenra monna mod, ðætte hit ne astyrige se
Wel hit wæs gecueden ðæt ðæt holh sceolde beon on ðæm weobude anre elne brad & anre elne
Be ðæm cuæð sanctus Paulus: Bere eower ælc oðre byrðenne betweoxn eow, ðonne gefylle ge
Ðæt is ðonne Godes æ ðæt mon hæbbe lufe & geðyld, ðæt micelne beam on ðinum agnan.
Sio gedrefednes ðære ungeðylde on ðæm mode ðæt is se smala cið, ac se yfela willa on ðære
Ðone ungeðyldegan ðonne suiðe lytel scur ðære costunga mæg onhreran, sua sua lytel wind mæg
Be ðæm cuæð Dryhten: ðu licettere, aweorp æresð of ðinum agnum eagan ðone greatan beam, &
Suelce he cuæde to ðæm unryhtwisan mode, ðe innan bið gnornigende, & utan licet geðyld:
Forðæm ðonne ðu ne wilnasð ðæt ðu oferswiðe & forlæte ða licettunge on ðe selfum, ðonne
& oft ðeah gebyreð ðæm geðyldgan, ðeah him mon hwæt wiðerweardes doo, oððe he hwelce
Ac ðonne he hit eft ofman æfter lytlum fæce, ðonne ofðyncð him ðæs ilcan ðe he ær forbær, &
Secð ðonne & smeað hu he hit gewrecan mæge, & ða monnðwærnesse ðe he ær ðurhtogen
Ac ðæm mæg beon suiðe hraðe geholpen from his lareowe, gif he him sægð hwonon ðæt cymð,
Ac hit gebyreð oftosð ðæt se bið ofersuiðed, se ðe ðurh diofles lare æresð bið onæled mid ðy
& se hæfð oftosð ðone weorðscipe, se ðe ær geðyldelic ða scande forbær.
Ac ðonne se diobul hæfð ðone ærran gewunnenne, & he bið under his geoc gegan, ðonne went he
Lætt ðonne an ðæt gefeoht sua openlice sume hwile, & ongienð hine diogollice læran, & slitan
Forðæm he hine ne meahte mid openlicum gefeohte ofersuiðan, sætað ðonne diogollice, & secð
Se geðyldiga ðonne eft, ðonne ðæt gestilled bið, ðonne went he eft ongean mid his mode, &
Ac hwam beoð ðonne ðas ðyllecan geliccran ðonne ðæm folce ðe on clænum felda weorðlicne
Ða geðyldegan sint to manianne ðætte hie hira heortan getrymigen æfter ðæs miclan sige, & ða
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða welwillendan, & on oðre ða æfestgan.
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða welwillendan, on oðre ða æfstegan.
Ða welwillendan sint to manianne ðæt hie sua fægenigen oðra monna godra weorca ðæt hie eac
Nimen him bisene on hira godan weorcum, & icen hie simle mid hira agenum, ðylæs hie sien to
Se ðe nu on ðæm gefeohte ðisses andweardan lifes nile suincan, ne his selfes plion, he ongiet eft
Suiðe suiðe we gesyngiað, gif we oðerra monna welgedona dæda ne lufigað & ne herigað, ac we
Forðæm is to secganne ðæm welwillendan monnum ðæt hi habbað sua micle mede oðerra monna
We herigað hira cræftas, & ðeah nyllað hi habban, forðæm we hiera nabbað nan lof.
We wundriað hu wel hie liciað for hira cræfte, & ðeah ne wilnigað no ðæt we sua licigen.
Ðæm welwillendum is to secganne, ðonne hie gesioð hiera geferena god weorc, ðæt hie eac
Ðæs ðy wyrse wite hie sculon habban on ende ðe him licað ðæt mon wel doo, & nyllað ðæm
Ac ða æfstegan sint to manianne ðæt hie ongieten hu blinde hi beoð, ðonne hie beoð unrote for
Hwa mæg beon ungesæligra ðonne se æfstiga?
Ðonne ðu gesiehsð ðæt he bið utan gedrefed, hu micle ma wenstu ðæt he sie innan for ðæs oðres
Ðæt god ðæt se oðer ðonne deð, ðæt meahte beon eac his god, ðeah he hit ðonne git don ne
Ealle ða ðe wunigeað on anum geleafan & on anum willan hie beoð sua sua manegu limu on
Ðonon hit gewyrð ðæt se fot gesiehð ðurh ðæt eage, & ðæt eage stæpð on ðæm fotum, ða earan
On ðæs lichoman gesceafte we underfengon ealle ða ðenunga ðe we nu ðiowiað & wyrceað.
Forðæm hit is micel sceand, gif we nyllað licittan ðæt we sien ðæt we sindon, forðæm butan
Geðencen be ðysum ða æfstigan hu micel mægen bið on ðære lufe ðæt hio gedeð ðæt oðerra
Ac ðæm æfstegum is to secganne, gif hie hie nyllað healdan wið ðæm æfste, ðæt hie weorðað
Forðæmðe he hefonrice mid his agenre scylde forworhte, ða ofðuhte him ðætte menn wæron to
Eac sint to læranne ða æfstigan ðætte hie ongieten under hu micelre frecenesse hie liecgað, & hu
Ne gefiolle he no on swæ opene scylde ðæt he his broður ofsloge, gif he ær ne geæfstgode ðætte
Be ðam is awriten ðæt Dryhten besawe to Abele & to his lacum, & nolde to Caine ne to his
Ða wearð Cain suiðe hrædlice irre, & hnipode ofdune, & se anda ða ðe he hæfde to his breðer,
Forðæm is to secganne ðæm æfstegum sua hwæt oðres godes sua on him ongieten bið.
Be ðæm is awriten ðætte ðis flæsclice lif sie æfesð, & he sie ðære flæsclican heortan hælo, &
Hwæt getacnað ðonne ðæt flæsc buton unfæsð weorc & hnesce, & hwæt ða ban buton stronglice
Oft ðeah gebyreð ðætte sume, ða ðe welwillende beoð on monegum weorcum, unfæste beoð

Ðæt is ðæt ða ban forrotigen for ðæm æfste ðæt he forleose sum suiðe god weorc for ðæm æfste.
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manienne ða bilwitan, on oðre ða ðweoran & ða lytegan.
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða biliwitan, on oðre ða lytegan.
Ða bilewitan sint to herigenne, forðæmðe hie simle suincað on ðæm ðæt hi tieligeað ðæt hie ne
Hie mon sceal eac læran ðæt hi hwilum suigien ðæs soðes, forðæm, sua sua sio leasung simle

Gehirað hwæt of ðæs wisan Salomonnes muðe wæs gecueden;

Forðæm ælc wag bið gebieged twiefeald on ðæm heale.

Ymb ða hwilendlican tida sanctus Paulus spræc, ða he cuæð: Nu is hiersumnesse tima & nu sint
Eac sint to manianne ða halan ðæt hie Gode wilnigen to licianne ðe hwile ðe hie mægen, ðylæs
Forðon wæs gesprecen ðurh ðone wisan Salomonn bi ðæm Wisdome ðæt se Wisdom wille sona
Ac eft, ðonne he ðone Wisdom habban wolde, & his wilnað, ðonne cuið se Wisdom to him: Ic
Ic ræhte mine hond to eow, nolde iower nan to locian;
Ac ge forsawon eall min geðeaht, & leton eow to giemeleste, ðonne ic eow cidde.
Hwæt sceal ic ðonne buton hliehchan ðæs, ðonne ge to lose weorðað, & habban me ðæt to
Ond eac cuið se Wisdom eft: ðonne hie to me clipiað, ðonne nylle ic hie gehieran.
On uhtan hie arisað, & me seceað, ac hi me ne findað.
Ac ðonne se mon his lichoman hælo forsihð, ðonne ðonne he wel trum bið to wyrceanne ðæt he
Forðæm eft suiðe ryhtlice Salomonn cuæð: Ne læt ðu to ælðiodigum ðinne weorðscipe, ne on
Hwa is ðonne from us fremde butan ða awiergdan gæstas, ða ðe from ðæs hefencundan Fæder
Oððe hwæt is ure weorðscipe on ðissum eorðlicum lichoman buton ðæt we sint gesceapene
& hwæt is elles se wælhreowa buton ða aworpnan englas, ðe hie selfe mid hiera ofermettum on
& on ðone ilcan deað hie wilniað eal moncynn to forspananne & to forlædanne.
Hwæt tacnað ðonne ðæt word elles ðæt mon ne selle his weorðscipe fremdum menn buton ðætte
& his gear geseleð wælhreowum, se se ðe in yfelra & wiðerweardra onwald forlæt ða hwile his
Ond eac cuæð Salomonn ðæt fremde ne scolden beon gefyllede ures mægenes, & ure gesuinc ne
Sua hwa ðonne sua on ðisse worulde hæfð Fulle hæle his lichoman, & nyle wisdomes & cræftes
Wiotodlice ða ðe hira lif on firenluste & on ofermodnesse geendigað, ne gefyllað hie godra rim,
Ðonne is æfter ðæm gecueden ðæt he sargige æt niehstan, ðonne his lichoma & his flæsc sie
Murcnað ðonne forðy ðæt he Gode nolde ðiowigan ða hwile ðe he meahte, forðon he ðonne
Forðæm cuæð se sealmscop: ðonne God hie slog, ðonne sohton hie hine.
Ongean ðæt sint to manianne ða mettruman ðæt hie ongieten & gefreden ðæt hie sua micle ma
Forðæm cuæð Dryhten to Iohanne ðam godspellere ðurh his engel, he cuæð: Ic ðreage & suinge
Forðæm eac cuæð Salomonn: Sunu min, ne agiemeleasa ðu Godes suingan, ne ðu ne beo werig
Be ðam ilcan se salmscop cuæð: Suiðe monigfalde sint ryhtwisra monna earfoðu.
Be ðæm eac se eadega Iob cwæð on his earfeðum, & geomriende cliopode to Dryhtne, & cuæð:
Eac is to cyðanne ðæm mettrumum, gif hie willen geliefan ðætte Godes rice hiera sie, ðæt hie
Be ðys ilcan is gecueden on kyninga bocum, sua sua hit geworden wæs, & eac us to bisene.
Hit is gecueden ðætte ða stanas on ðæm mæran temple Salomonnes wæron sua wel gefegede &
Ðæt ðonne tacnað us ðætte we scylen beon on ðisse ælðeodignesse utane beheawene mid
Ðonne sint eac to manianne ða unhalan ðæt hie geðencen mid hu monigfaldum ungetæsum &
Ac hwelc wite sceal us ðonne to hefig ðyncan ðære godcundan ðreaunga wið ðæm ðe we mægen
Forðæm cuæð sanctus Paulus: Ure flæsclican fædras lærdon us, & we hie ondredon;
Hie ðreadon us, & we weorðodon hie.
Hu micle suiðor sculon we ðonne beon gehiersume ðæm ðe ure gæsta Fæder bið wið ðæm ðæt
Ure flæsclican fædras us lærdon to ðæm ðe hira willa wæs, ac ðæt wæs to suiðe scortre hwile,
Eac sint to manianne ða mettruman ðæt hie geðencen hu micel hælo ðæt bið ðære heortan ðæt
Ðæt wære suiðe ryhte getacnod ðurh Balaham on ðære lettinge his færeltes, gif he mid his
Baloham ðonne fulgeorne feran wolde ðær hine mon bæd, ac his estfulnesse witteah se esol ðe
Ðæt wæs forðæmðe se assa geseah ðone engel ongean hine standan, & him ðæs færeltes
Sua eac, ðonne ðæt flæsc bið gelett mid sumum broce, hit getacnað ðæm mode for ðære suingan
Sua mon oft lett fundigendne monna, & his færelt gælð, sua gælð se lichoma ðæt mod, oððæt he
Be ðæm cwæð sanctus Petrus suiðe ryhtlice: ðæt dumbe & ðæt gehæfte neat ðreade ðone witgan
Ðonne ðreað ðæt dumbe neat ðone unwisan monn, ðonne ðæt gebrocode flæsc gelærð ðæt
Forðæm ne meahte Balaham geearnian ða Godes giefe ðe he biddende wæs,ða he Israhela folc
Forðæm he wearð untygða ðe he hwierfde his stemne nales his mod: ðæt wæs ðæt he spræc
Eac sint ða seocan to monianne ðæt hie ongieten hu micel Godes giefu him bið ðæs flæsces
Bi ðæm is eac awriten on Salomonnes cuidbocum ðæt sio wund wolde halian, æfterðæmðe heo
Ðonne aflewð ðæt sar of ðære wunde mid ðy wormse, ðonne ðæt sar ðære suingellan ðissa
Eac cuæð Salomon ðætte ðæt illce beo bi ðæm wundum ðe beoð oninnan ðære wambe.
Ðære wambe nama getacnað ðæt mod, forðæm sua sua sio wamb gemielt ðone mete, sua gemielt
Of Salomonnes cuidum we namon ðætte ðære wambe nama scolde tacnian ðæt mod, ða ða he
Ðæt Godes leohtfæt gindsecð & gindlieht ealle ða diogolnesse ðære wambe.
Suelce he cuæde: æresð he hiene onlieht mid his leohtfæte, ðonne he hiene gelieffæsð, & eft he
Ðæt bið ðonne, ðonne he deð ðæt he ongiet his agne unnytte ðeawas & geðohtas ðe wen is ðæt
Ðæt worms ðonne ðara wunda ðæt is ðæt broc ðæs lichoman, & ðæt sar innan ðære wambe, ðæt
Sio sorg ðonne aswæpð aweg ðæt yfel of ðæm mode.
Ðonne we beoð butan ðæm mode on ðæm lichoman gesuencte, ðonne beo we suigende
Forðæm gelimpð ðætte ðurh ða openlican wunda & ðurh ðæt gesewene sar ðæs lichoman bið
Eac sint to manianne ða mettruman to ðæm ðæt hie gehealden ða strenge ðære geðylde.
Him is to secgeanne ðæt hie unablinnendlice geðencen hu monig yfel ure Dryhten & ure Alisend
Se ilca se ðe ælce dæg saula gereafað of ðæs ealdan feondes honda, se ilca se ðe us ðwiehð mid
Se ilca se us gefreoð mid his forespræce from ecum witum, se ilca suigende geðafode
Se ðe us sealde ece are betwuxn his engla geferscipe, he geðafode ðæt hine mon mid fyste slog.
Se ðe us gehæleð from ðæm stice urra synna, he geðafode ðæt him mon sette ðyrnenne beag on
Se ðe us oferdrencð mid ðæs ecan lifes liðe, he gefandode geallan biternesse, ða hine ðyrstte.
Se ðe for us gebæd to his Fæder, ðeah he him emnmiehtig sie on his godhade, ða ða him mon on
Se se ðe deadum monnum lif gearuwað, & he self lif is, he becom to deaðe.
Forhwy ðonne sceal ænigum menn ðyncan to reðe oððe to unieðe ðæt he Godes suingellan
Hwa sceal ðonne, ðara ðe hal & good andgiet hæbbe, Gode unðoncfull beon, forðæm, ðeah he
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manienne ða ðe him ondrædað Godes swingellan oððe monna, &
On oðre wisan ða ðe beoð swa aheardode on unryhtwisnesse ðæt hi mon ne mæg mid nanre
On oðre wisan sint to monianne ða ðe him suingellan ondrædað, & forðæm unsceaðfullice
& on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðe on hiera unryhtwisnessum sua aheardode beoð ðæt hie
Ðæt is to cyðanne ðe him swingellan ondrædað ðæt hie ðissa eorðlicena goda to suiðe ne
& ne fleon eac ðis andwearde yfel, suelce hie hit adriogan ne mægen, forðonðe hit oft gode
Eac hie sint to monianne, gif hie geornlice wilnigen ðæt him yfel ðing losie, ðonne beo him
Nalles ðeah sua egeful ðæt hie ealneg ðurhwunigen on ðæm ege, ac mid ðam fostre ðære Godes
Forðæm Iohannis se godspellere cwæð on his ærendgewrite, ic cwæð: Sio fulfremede Godes
& eft cwæð sanctus Paulus: Ne underfengon ge no ðone Gast æt ðæm fulluhte to ðeowigeanne
Forðæm cwið eft eac se ilca lariow: ðær se Dryhtnes gast is, ðær is freodoom.
Gif ðonne hwelc mon forbireð his synna for ðæm ege anum ðæs wites, ðonne nafað ðæs
Ðonne nat ðæt mod ðæt him bið freodom forgiefen, ðonne hit bið gebunden mid ðæs ðeowutes
Ðeah monn nu good onginne for sumes wites ege, hit mon sceal ðeah geendigean for sumes
Se ðe for ðæm anum god deð ðæt he sumre ðreaunge yfel him ondrætt, se wilnað ðætte nan ðing
Ðonne bið suiðe sweotol ðætte him ðonne losað beforan Gode his ryhtwisnes, ðonne he ðurh his
Ongean ðæt ðonne sint to monianne ða menn ðe suingellan ne magon forwiernan ne na gelettan
Hie beoð to ðreageanne & to swinganne mid swa micle maran wite sua hie ungefredelicor beoð
Oft eac ða swelcan monn sceal forsion mid eallum forsewennessum, & unweorðian mid ælcre
Ðonne sint eac ðæm ilcan monnum suiðe ðearllice to recceanne ða godcundan cwidas, ðæt hie
Eac hie sculon gehieran ðæt on him bið gefylled Salomonnes cwide ðe he cwæð, he cwæð: ðeah
Ðæt ilce sarette se witga, ða ða he cwæð: ðu hie tobræce, & ðeah hie noldon underfon ðine lare.
Eft bi ðæm ilcan cwæð Dryhten: Ic ofslog ðis folc, & to forlore gedyde, & hie hie ðeah noldon
Bi ðæm ilcan eft cwæð se witga: ðis folc nis no gewend to ðæm ðe hie swingð.
Ymb ðæt ilce sargode se witga, sua sua god lareow deð, ðonne he his gingran suingð, gif hit him
Be ðæm cwæð se witga: We lacnodon Babylon, & hio ðeah ne wearð gehæled.
Ðonne bið Babylon gelacnad, nales ðeah fullice gehæled, ðonne ðæs monnes mod for his
Ðæt ilce eac Dryhten oðwat Israhela folce, ða hie wæron gehergeode & of hiera earde alædde,
Ða cwæð Dryhten: ðiss Israhela folc is geworden nu me to sindrum & to are & to tine & to
Suelce he openlice cwæde: Ic hie wolde geclænsian mid ðæm gesode ðæs broces, & wolde ðæt
Witodlice ðæt ar, ðonne hit mon slihð, hit bið hludre ðonne ænig oðer ondweorc.
Sua bið ðæm ðe suiðe gnornað on ðære godcundan suingellan, he bið on middum ðæm ofne
Ðæt tin ðonne, ðonne hit mon mid sumum cræfte gemengð, & to tine gewyrcð, ðonne bið hit
Sua hwa ðonne sua licet on ðære swingellan, he bið ðæm tine gelic inne on ðæm ofne.
Se bið ðonne ðæm isene gelic inne on ðæm ofne, se ðe for ðære suingellan nyle his ðweorscipe
Ðæt lead ðonne is hefigre ðonne ænig oðer andweorc.
Forðy bið inne on ðæm ofne geworden to leade se se ðe sua bið geðrysced mid ðære hefignesse
Bi ðæm ilcan is eft awriten: ðær wæs suiðe suiðlic gesuinc, & ðær wæs micel swat agoten, &
He us stiereð mid fyres broce, forðæmðe he wolde from us adon ðone rust urra unðeawa, ac we
Be ðæm cwæð eft se witga: Idel wæs se blawere, forðon hiera awiergdan weorc ne wurdon from
Eac is to witanne ðætte oft ðæm bið gestiered mid manðwærlicre manunga, ðæm ðe monn mid
Sua beoð eac ful oft ða wunda mid ele gehælda, ða ðe mon mid gesnide gebetan ne meahte.

Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manienne ða ðe to swiðe swige beoð, on oðre wisan ða ðe willað to
On oðre wisan sint to monianne ða suiðe suigean, on oðre wisan ða ðe beoð aidlode on
Ða suiðe suigean mon sceal læran ðætte hie, ðonne ðonne hie sumne unðeaw unwærlice fleoð,
Forðæm gebyreð oft ðæt hie beoð sua micle ungestæððelicor toflowene on hiera mode sua hie
Ac forðæmðe mon ne mæg utane on him ongietan for hiera suigean hwæt mon tæle, hie beoð
Ðeah sio tunge eaðmodlice licge, ðæt mod bið suiðe upahafen, & sua micle freolicor he tælð on
Eac sint to manianne ða suiðe suigean ;
Ðæt hie geornlice tiligen to wietanne ðæt him nis na ðæs anes ðearf to ðenceanne hwelce hie hie
Hit is awriten on Salomonnes cwidum: Sunu min, ongiet minne wisdom & minne wærscipe, &
Forðæm nan wuht nis on us unstilre & ungestæððigre ðonne ðæt mod, forðæm hit gewitt sua oft
Be ðæm cwæð se psalmsceop: Min mod & min wisdom me forlet & eft he gehwearf to him
Forðæm, ðonne monn his mod gehæft, ðæt ðæt hit ær gewunode to fleonne hit gemet.
Oft eac ða suiðe suigean, ðonne hie monige unnytte geðohtas innan habbað, ðonne weorðað hie
Hwæt we wieton ðæt sio diegle wund bið sarre ðonne sio opene, forðam ðæt worsm ðæt
Eac sculon weotan ða ðe ma swugiað ðonne hie ðyrfen, ðætte hie hiera sorge ne geiecen mid ðy
Eac hie sint to manianne, gif hie hiera nihstan lufien swa sua hie selfe, ðæt hie him ne helen
Se ðe ðonne hwæt yfeles ongiet on his nihstan, & hit forswugað, he deð sua sua se læce ðe
Hu, ne bið he ðonne swelce he sie his slaga, ðonne he hine mæg gehælan, & nyle?
Forðæm is sio tunge gemetlice to midliganne, nales ungemetlice to gebindanne.
Be ðæm is awriten: Se wisa suigað, oð he ongiet ðæt him bið nyttre to sprecanne.
Nis hit nan wundur, ðeah he swugie, & bide his timan, ac ðonne he nytwyrðne timan ongiet to
& eft hit is awriten on Salomonnes bocum, ðæm ðe Ecclesiastis hatton, ðætte hwilum sie spræce
Forðæm is gesceadwislice to ðenceanne hwelcum tidum him gecopust sie to sprecanne, ðætte,
Be ðæm suiðe wel cwæð se psalmsceop: Gesete Dryhten hirde minum muðe & ða duru
Ne bæd he no ðæt he hine elle fortynde mid gehale wage, ac he bæd dura to, ðæt he meahte
Ðy we sculon geleornian ðæt we suiðe wærlice gecope tiid aredigen, & ðonne sio stemn
Ongean ðæt sint to læranne ða oferspræcean ðæt hie wacorlice ongieten fram hu micelre
Ac ðæt mennisce mod hæfð wætres ðeaw.
Ðæt wæter, ðonne hit bið gepynd, hit miclað & uppað & fundað wið ðæs ðe hit ær from com,
Ac gif sio pynding wierð onpennad, oððe sio wering wirð tobrocen, ðonne toflewð hit eall, & ne
Sua deð ðæs monnes mod, ðonne hit gesceadwislice ne can his swigean gehealdan, ac hit abricð
Ðætte ne bið forðæmðe hit bið todæled & to monigfealda spræca, suelce he self hine selfne ute
Swa hit awriten is on Salomonnes cwidum ðætte se mon se ðe ne mæg his tungan gehealdan sie
Forðæm sio burg ðæs modes, ðe mid nanre suigean ne bið bityned sceal suiðe oft gefredan hiere
Oft ðonne ðæt hefige mod glit niðor & niðor stæpmælum on unnyttum wordum, oð hit mid ealle
Forðæm hit ær hit nolde behealdan wið unnyt word, hit sceal ðonne niedinga afeallan for ðæm
Æt ærestum lyst ðone monn unnyt sprecan be oðrum monnum, & ðonne æfter firste hine lyst
Swa he sæwð ðone sticel ðæs andan, oððæt ðærof aweoxð towesnes, & of ðære towesnesse bið
Be ðæm wæs suiðe wel gecweden ðurh ðone wisan Salomon, ðætte se se ðæt wæter utforlete
Se forlæt ut ðæt wæter, se ðe his tungan stemne on unnyttum wordum lætt toflowan.
Ac se wisa Salomon sæde ðætte suiðe deop pol wære gewered on ðæs wisan monnes mode, &
Ac se se ðe ðone wer bricð, & ðæt wæter utforlæt, se bið fruma ðæs geflites.
Ðæt is ðonne se ðe his tungan ne gemidlað, se towierpð anmodnesse.
Eft cwæð Salomon: Se gemetgað irre, se ðe ðone disigan hætt geswugian.
Forðæm se næfre ne mæg ryhtwisnesse & gesceadwisnesse healdan, se ðe ofersprecol bið.
Ðæt tacnode se salmsceop, ða he cwæð: Se oferspræcea wer ne wierð he næfre geryht ne
Eft cwæð Salomon bi ðæm ilcan: Ne bið næfre sio oferspræc butan synne.

Ðæt ðonne tacnað ðætte ðæs modes ryhtwisnes bið toflowen, ðe nele forhabban ða
Be ðæm cwæð Iacobus se apostol: Gif hwa teochhað ðæt he æfæst sie, & nyle gemidlian his
& eft he cwæð: Sie æghwelc mon suiðe hræd & suiðe geornful to gehieranne, & suiðe læt to
Eft bi ðam ilcan he gecyðde hwæt ðære tungan mægen is, he cwæð ðæt hio wære unstille, yfel &
& eft us manode sio Soðfæstnes ðurh hie selfe, ðæt is Crist, he cuæð: ælces unnyttes wordes ðara
Ðæt bið ðonne openlice unnyt word, ðætte gescedwise menn ne magon ongietan ðæt hit belimpe
Gif we ðonne sculon ryht agildan unnyttra worda, hwelc wite wene we ðæt se felaspræcea scyle
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðe bioð to late, on oðre ða ðe bioð to hrade.
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðe beoð to late, on oðre ða ðe beoð to hrade.
Ða slawan sint to manianne ðæt hie ne forielden ðone timan for hiera slæwðe ðe hie tiola on don
Ða hradan ðonne sint to manianne ðæt hie to unwærlice ne onetten, ðylæs hie forhradien ðone
Ðam slawum ðonne is to cyðanne ðætte oft, ðonne we nellað hwæthwugu nytwyrðes don, ðonne
Forðæm, ðonne we forslawiað ðone gecopestan timan, ðætte we ðonne ne beoð onælde mid ðære
Be ðæm wæs suiðe wel gecweden ðurh Salomon ðone snottran: Sio slæwð giett slæp on ðone

Be ðæm eft wrat Salomon, & cwæð: ælc idel mon liofað æfter his agnum dome.

Ac gif he eft cymð, & ðæt hus idel gemett, he hit gefylleð mid suiðe monigum.
Oft se slawa, ðonne he agælð & forielt ðæt weorc ðe him niedðearf wære to wyrceanne, ðonne
Be ðæm wæs suiðe ryhte gecweden ðurh Salomon ðone snottran: For ciele nele se slawa erian on
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt se slawa nylle erian for ciele, ðæt hwa sie gebunden mid hefignesse ðære
Forðæm is gecweden ðæt se slawa for ðæm ege ðæs cieles nylle erigean, forðæm we oft for ðæm
Hit is suiðe wel be ðæm gecweden ðæt he eft bedecige on sumera, & him mon ðonne noht ne
Sua bið ðæm ðe nu on godum weorcum ne swæt, & suiðe ne suinceð: eft ðonne sio sunne, ðæt
Be ðæm men wæs eft suiðe wel gecweden ðurh Salomon ðone snottran, he cwæð: Se ðe him
& se ðe him ælc wolcn ondrædt, ne ripð se næfre.
Hwæt getacnað ðonne se wind buton ða costunga ðæs awrigdan gæstes, & hwæt ðæt wolc ðe
Se wind drifeð ðæt wolcn.
Sua deð se unclæna gæst mid his winde;
He onstyreð unryhtwise men.
We cwædon ær ðæt se sceolde lytel sawan, se de him ðone wind ondrede;
& eft lytel ripan, se ðe him ða wolc ondrede.
Hwæt is ðonne ðætte sua hwelc sua him ondræt oððe deofles costunga oððe yfelra monna
Ongean ðæt is to cyðanne ðæm ðe beoð to hrade, ðonne hie forhradigað ðone timan godes
To swelcum monnum Salomon wæs sprecende, ða he his cnieht lærde, he cwæð: Sunu min, ne
Ac læt simle gan ðin eagean beforan ðinum fotum.
Ðonne stæppað ða eagan beforan ðæm fotum, ðonne ðæt ryhte & ðæt gesceadwislice geðeaht
Ac se ðe agimeleasað ðæt he ðence, ærðæmðe he do, se stæpð forð mid ðam fotum, & wincað
He gæð on ðone weg, ac he nat on hwæt he gæð, ac he wirð suiðe raðe on fielle.
Sua wirð se ðe beforan ðæm stæpum his weorca ne locað mid ðæm eagum gesceadwisra
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manienne ða monðwæran, on oðre ða grambæran.
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða monnðwæran, on oðre ða grambæran.
Forðæm oft gebyreð ðæm monðwæran, ðonne he wierð riece ofer oðre menn, ðæt he for his
Forðæm oft, ðonne mon læt toslupan ðone ege & ða lare suiður ðonne hit ðearf sie for
Ongean ðæt sint to manianne ða weamodan & ða grambæran, forðæm, ðonne hie underfoð ðone
Forðæm, ðonne ðæt ierre æfð anwald ðæs monnes, ðonne gehriesð he on sume scylde, sua ðæt he
Ða irran nyton hwæt hie on him selfum habbað, & eac ðætte wierse is, ðætte hie ful oft wenað
Forðæm, ðonne hie wenað ðæt hiera unðeawas sien sum god cræft, ðonne gadriað hie hie &
Oft eac ða monðwæran weorðað sua besolcne & sua wlace & sua slawe for hira
Oft eac ða grambæra leogað him selfum, ðonne hie wenað ðæt hie ryhtne andan hæbben.
Oft eac sio godnes ðære monnðwærnesse bið diegellice gemenged wið sleacnesse.
Oft eac ða grambæran wenað ðæt hiera unðeaw sie sumes ryhtwislices andan wielm.
Ac we sculon manian ða manðwæran ðæt hie hæbben ða monnðwærnesse, & fleon ðæt ðær
Ða grambæran we sculon monian ðæt hie ongieten hwæt hie on him selfum habbað.
Ða monnðwæran we sculon monian ðæt hie ongieten hwæt hi nabbað.
Ne forlæten ða ierran ðone andan, ac geðencen ðæt he sie gesceadwislic & gemetlic.
Leorniað hine ða manðwæran & lufigað, oððæt hie hiene hæbben.
Lytligen ða grambæran hiera gedrefednesse.
Ða monðwæran sint to monianne ðæt hie geornlice tiligen ðæt hie hæbben ryhtwislicne andan.
Ða grambæran sint to monianne, ðe wenað ðæt hie ryhtwislicne andan hæbben, ðæt hie ðone
Forðæm us ætiede se Halga Gæsð ægðer ge on culfran onlicnesse ge on fyres, forðæmðe ælcne
Ne bið se no gefylled ðæs Halgan Gæsðæs se ðe on ðære smyltnesse his monðwærnesse forlæt
Ic wene ðæt we mægen ðis openlicor gecyðan gif we sanctus Paulus lare sume ongemong
Oðer hira wæs haten Timotheus, oðer Titus.
He cuæð to ðæm Timotheo: Lære hie, & healsa, & tæl hira unðeawas, & ðeah geðyldelice.
To ðæm Tite he cuæð: Lær ðæt folc, & ðreata, & tæl, & hat, ðæt hie wieten ðæt ge sume anwald
Hwæt mænde sanctus Paulus, ða he his lare sua cræftelice toscead, & ðone oðerne lærde ðæt he
Titum he wolde onælan mid ryhtwislicum andan, Timotheum he wolde gemetgian.
Oðrum he wolde geiecean ðæt him wana wæs, oðrum he wolde oftion ðæs ðe he to fela æfde.
Oðerne he draf suiðe geornfullice mid sticele, oðrum he wiðteah mid bridle.
Wietodlice se mæra landbegenga, ðæt wæs sanctus Paulus, he underfeng ða halgan gesomnunga
Sumu treowu he watrode, to ðæm ðæt hie ðy suiður sceolden weaxan.
Sume he cearf ðonne him ðuhte ðæt hie to suiðe weoxsen, ðylæs hie to ðæm forweoxen ðæt hie
Sumu twigu he lehte mid wætere, ðonne hie to hwon weoxson, ðæt hie ðy suiður weaxan
Ac ða irsunga sindun suiðe ungelica: oðer bið suelce hit sie irres anlicnes, ðæt is ðæt mon wielle
Oðer ðara irsunga bið to ungemetlice & to ungedafenlice atyht on ðæt ðe hio mid ryhte irsian
Eac is to wietanne ðætte hwæthwugu bið betweoh ðæm irsiendan & ðæm ungeðyldgan, ðæt is
Ða suelcan we magon ealra betest geryhtan mid ðy ðæt we hie forbugen, ðonne ðonne hie beoð
Ac ðæt mod, ða hwile ðe hit bið oferdruncen ðæs ierres, eal ðæt him mon ryhtes sægð, hit ðyncð
Forðæm ðæt wif ðe Abigall hatte suiðe herigendlice forsuigode ðæt dysig hiere fordruncnan
Sua eac, ðonne ðæt gelimpð ðæt ða iersigendan menn oðrum monnum oferfylgeað to ðon suiðe
Ðæt we magon openlicor gecyðan, gif we æfneres dæda sume herongemong secgað, hu Assael
Hit is awriten ðæt Æfnere cwæde to Assaele: Gecier la, & gesuic, ne folga me,ðæt ic ðe ne dyrre
He forhogde ðæt he hit gehierde, & nolde hine forlætan.
Ða ðydde æfner hine mid hindewerde sceafte on ðæt smælðearme ðæt he wæs dead.
Hwæs onlicnesse hæfde Assael ða buton ðara ðe hiera hatheortnes hie suiðe hrædlice on
Ða ðonne hie beræsað on suelce weamodnesse hie sindon sua micle wærlicor to oferbuganne
Ðæs æfneres noma ðe ðone oðerne fleah is on ure geðiode fæder leohtfæt.
Ðæt getacnað ðætte ðara lareowa tungan ðe ðæt uplice leoht bodiað, ðonne hie ongietað
Sua, ðonne ðonne ða hatheortan hie mid nane foreðonce nyllað gestillan, ac sua wedende folgiað
Forðæm æfner, ða ða he ongean ðone cirde ðe hine draf, ne ofstong he hiene no mid ðy speres
Ðæt is ðonne suelc mon mid forewearde orde stinge, ðæt mon openlice & unforwandodlice on
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt mon mid hindewearde sceafte ðone ðydde ðe him oferfylge, ðæt mon ðone
Sua sua Assael suiðe hrædlice gefeol, sua ðæt ahrerede mod, ðonne hit ongiet ðæt him mon
Se ðe ðonne sua forbygð ðone wielm & ðone onræs his hatheortnesse, forðæmðe hine mon slea
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to monianne ða eaðmodan, on oðre wisan ða upahæfenan on hira mode.
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða eaðmodan, on oðre ða upahæfenan.
Ðæm eaðmodum is to cyðanne hu micel sio heanes is & hu soðlic ðe hie to hopiað, & eac
Ðæm upahæfenum is to cyðanne hwelc nawuht ðes woruldgielp is ðe hie clyppað & lufiað, & his
Gehieren ða eaðmodan hu ece ðæt is ðæt hie wilniað, & hu gewitende & hu unagen ðæt is ðæt hie
Gehieren eac ða upahæfenan hu gewitende ða ðing sint ðe hie gietsiað, & hu eciu ða sint ða hie
Gehieren ða eaðmodan ðære soðfæsðnesse stemne, ðæt is Crist ure lareow, he cuæð: ælc ðara ðe
Gehieren eac ða upahæfenan on hiera mode hwæt he eft cuæð, he cuæð: ælc ðara ðe hine selfne
Gehieren ða eaðmodan hwæt Salomon cuæð: Sio eaðmodnes iernð beforan ðæm gielpe, & heo
Gehieren eac ða upahæfenan on hira mode hu he eft cuæð, he cuæð: ær ðæs monnes hryre bið
Gehieren ða eaðmodan hwæt God cuæð ðurh Essaim ðone witgan, he cuæð: To hwæm locige ic
Gehieren ða upahæfenan hwæt Salomon cuæð, he cuæð: Hwæt ofermodgað ðios eorðe & ðis
Gehieren ða eaðmodan hwæt on psalmum gecueden is, ðætte Dryhten locige to ðæm eaðmodan.
Gehieren ða upahæfenan: Dryhten ongiet suiðe feorran ða heahmodnesse.
Gehieren ða eaðmodan hwæt Crist cuæð: Ne com ic to ðon on eorðan ðæt me mon ðenode, ac to
Gehieren ða upahæfenan hwæt Salomon cuæð, he cuæð ðæt ælces yfles fruma wære ofermetta.
Gehieren ða eaðmodan ðætte Crisð ure Aliesend hiene selfne geeaðmedde emne oð ðone deað.
Gehieren ða upahæfenan hwæt awriten is be hira heafde & be hiera lareowe, ðæt is dioful: hit is
Se ure fiond ðonne he wæs gesceapen ongemang eallum oðrum gesceaftum, ac he wilnode ðæt
Ac se ure Aliesend, ðe mara is & mærra eallum gesceaftum, he hine gemedomode to bionne
Ðæm eaðmodum is to cyðanne ðætte, ðonne ðonne hie hie selfe suiðusð eaðmedað, ðætte hie
Secgað eac ðæm upahæfenum ðætte, ðonne ðonne hie hie selfe upahebbað, ðæt hie ðonne
Ac hwæt is ðonne forcuðre ðonne sio upahæfenes?
Forðæm, ðonne heo bið atyht ofer hire andefnu, ðonne bið heo afeorrod suiðe feor from ðære
Hwæt mæg ðonne hierre bion ðonne sio soðe eaðmodnes?
Sio, ðonne hio nieðemesð gebygeð, ðonne bið hio gelicosð hiere Dryhtne, se wunað ofer eallum
Ðonne is ðeah betwux ðissum twam sum ðing ðe mon wærlice sceal geðencean, ðæt is ðæt
Sume, ða ða wenað ðæt hie eaðmode sien, hie doð for ege ðone weorðscipe mannum ðe hie
Oft eac ða upahæfenan, ðeah hie hwilum unforwandodlice sprecen, ðonne hie hwelces unðeawes
Ac hit bið oftor for ðære ungeðylde hiera upahæfenesse.
Ða eaðmodan ðonne bioð oft geðrycte mid ðære synne ðæs eges, ðonne hie ne durron unðeawas
Ac ða upahæfenan, ðonne hie licettað ðæt him ne sie nawuhtes cearu ofer ða ryhtwisnesse,
Forðæm sint to manianne ða upahæfenan ðæt hie ne sien bealdran & orsorgran ðonne hie
Eac is to geðencanne ðætte mon mæg oft ðy bet ða ofermodan ðreatian, gif hie mon ongemang
Him mon sceal cyðan ðara goda sum ðe hie on him habbað oððe ðara sum ðe hie habban
Sua we magon betesð ofaceorfan ðæt us on him mislicað, ðæt we æresð gedon ðæt hie gehieren
Forðæm hie beoð to myndgianne ðara goda ðe hie ær dydon, ðæt hie sien ðe lusðbærran to
Swa swa wildu hors, ðonne we hie æresð gefangnu habbað, we hie ðacciað & straciad mid bradre
Sua eac se læce, ðonne he bietre wyrta deð to hwelcum drence, he hie gesuet mid hunige, ðylæs
Sua mon sceal on ðæm upahæfenum monnum ðone fruman & ðone ingong ðære ðreatunga &
Oft we magon eac ða upahæfenan ðy bet gelæran to urum willan, gif we him cyðað hu micle
Ðy ieðelicor bið sio upahæfenes to gode gehwierfed, gif hie ongietað ðæt hiera eac oðre men
Be ðæm se ilca Moyses ðe God self lærde, & hine lædde ðurh ðæt westen mid ðy fyrenan sweore
He cuæð: We willað nu faran to ðære stowe ðe God us gehaten hæfð, ac far mid us, ðæt we ðe
Ða andsuarode he him, & cuæð: Ic nelle mid ðe faran, ac ic wille faran to minre cyððe, & to
Ða andswarade him Moyses: La, ne forlæt us, ac beo ure laðeow, ðu cans eal ðis westen, &
Ne spræc he hit no forðyðe his mod auht genierwed wære mid ðære uncyððe ðæs siðfætes,
La ah ðeahhwæðre se foreðancula wer, forðæmðe he spræc to ðæm upahæfenan, he bæd his
& bæd ðeah for ðæs oðres ðearfe, forðæm he tiohchode him ma to fultemanne;
He sohte hine him to latðeowe on ðæm wege, forðæm he teohchode hine to lædanne on lifes weg.
Ac he dyde sua sua ofermod gefera deð: he sceolde beon ðære spræce sua micle gefægenra sua
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða anwillan, on oðre ða ungestæððegan & unfæsðræda.
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða anfealdan stræcan, on oðre ða unbealdan.
Ðæm anfealdan stræcum is to cyðanne ðæt hie bet ne truwien him selfum ðonne hie ðyrfen,
Ðæm unbealdum is to cyðanne hu giemelease hie bioð ðonne hie hie selfe to suiðe forsioð,
Ac ðæm anstræcum is to cyðanne, ðær hie ne wenden ðæt hie selfe beteran & wisran wæren
Ac ðæm unbealdum is to kyðanne, gif hie be ænegum dæle wolden geðencean hwæt hie selfe
Ac to ðæm anstræcum is gecueden ðurh sanctus Paulus: Ne sculon ge no ðyncan eow selfum to
Ond eft he cuæð to ðæm unbealdum: Ne læte ge eow ælcre lare wind awecggan.
To ðæm anstræcum is gecueden ðurh Salomon: Hie etað ðone wæsðm hiera ægnes weges.
Ðæt is ðonne ðonne hie beoð gefyllede mid hiera ægnum geðeahte.
Eft cuæð Salomon be ðæm unbealdum: Dysigra monna mod bið suiðe unemn & suiðe ungelic,
He bið simle ryhtes geðeahtes geðafa, forðæm he bið suiðe arod & suiðe gereðre on ryhtum
Ac ðara monna mod bið suiðe unemn, forðæm hit gedeð hit self him selfum suiðe ungelic for
Eac is to wietanne ðætte sume unðeawas cumað of oðrum unðeawum sua ilce sua hie comon ær
Forðy us is to wietanne ðæt we magon hie sua iðesð mid ðreaunga gebetan, gif we ðone biteran
Ða anstræcan ðonne sint to monianne ðæt hie ongieten ða upahæfenesse hiora modes, &
Eac hie sint to manianne ðæt hie geðencen ðætte Crist, ðe simle anes willan wæs & God fæder,
& eac he gehett ðæt he sua don wolde, ðonne he eft come on ðæm ytemestan dæge, forðæmðe
Ðæt he cyðde, ða he cuæð: Ne mæg ic nane wuht don mines agnes ðonces, ac sua ic deme sua ic
Ac hwy sceal ænigum menn ðonne ðyncean to orgellic ðæt he onbuge to oðres monnes willan,
Ongean ðæt sint to manianne ða unbealdan & ða unfæsðrædan ðæt hie hera mod mid stillnesse
Sona aseariað ða twigu ðære hwurfulnesse, gif æresð se wyrtruma bið forcorfen æt ðære
Ðy mon sceal fæsðne weal wyrcean, ðy mon ær gehawige ðæt se grund fæsð sie, ðær mon ðone
Ðy sceal eac bion ofersuiðed sio unfæsðrædnes & sio unbieldo ðara geðohta, ðy mon hine
Ðære leohtmodnesse & ðære leasmodnesse sanctus Paulus hine ladode, ða he cuæð: Wene ge nu

Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðe hie selfe forgiefað gifernesse, on oðre wisan ða ðe
On oðre wisan sint to manigenne ða gifran, on oðre ða ðe forhæfdnesse doð, forðæm ðæm
Gif ðam gifran ungemetlicu spræc ne eglde, ðonne ne burne se weliga ðe suiður on ðære tungan
Mid ðy worde wæs getacnod ðætte ða ðe ælce dæg symbliað, on ðære tungann suiður syngiað
Ond eft ðæm gifrum suiðe hrædlice him willað fylgan leohtlicu weorc & unnyte.
Ðæt trymeð sio halige æ, ðær hio cuæð: ðæt folc sæt, æt, & dranc, & siððan aryson, & eodon
Sua oft se oferæt wierð gehwierfed to fierenluste, forðæm ðonne sio womb bið full & aðened,
Forðæm wæs gecueden to ðæm lytegan feonde, ðe ðæs ærestan monnes mod ontynde on ðæs

Forðæm ðæm ðe on giefernesse ongietene beoð wile folgian fierenlust.

Ðara koka ealdormon bið sio womb, forðæm eall hiera gesuinc & hiera ðenung belimpeð to
Ða weallas ðonne Hierusalem getacniað ða mægenu ðære saule.
Ða mægenu beoð aræredu mid wilnunge to ðære uplican sibbe.
Ac ðara koka ealdormon towierpð ða weallas Hierusalem.
Ðæt is, ðonne ðonne sio wamb bið aðened mid fylle for giefernesse, ðonne towierpð hio ðurh
Ongean ðæt ðonne is to cyðanne ðæm fæstendum, gif ðæs modes forhæfdnes ful oft mid

Ongean ðæt sint to manianne ða ofergifran, ðeah hie ne mægen ðone unðeaw forlætan ðære
Ac we sculun geðencean, sua oft sua we ure hand doð to urum muðe for giefernesse ofergemet,
Ongean ðæt sint to manianne ða fæstendan ðæt hie huru geornlice giemen, ðær ðær hie fleoð
Oft eac, ðonne ðæt mod ðæs fæstendan bið mid ðy irre ofseten, ðonne cymð sio blis
Be ðæm wæs suiðe ryhte gecueden ðurh ðone witgan: On eowrum fæstendagum bið ongieten
& sona ðæræfter he cuæð: To gemotum & to gecidum & to iersunga & to fystgebeate ge fæstað.
Willa belimpð to blisse simle & ðæt fystgebeat to irre.
On iedelnesse ðonne bið se lichoma mid fæstenne gesuenced, ðonne ðæt mod bið forlæten &
Ond ðeah hie sint to manianne ðæt hie no hiera fæsten ne gewanigen, ne eft ne wenen ðæt hit

Be ðæm cuæð Iohel se witga: Gehalgiað eower fæsten.
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt mon his fæsten gehalgige, ðæt he hit geice mid oðrum godum weorcum;
Ðonne mæg he eowian ðær Gode suiðe gelicweorðe forhæfdnesse.
Forðæm is to cyðanne ðæm fæstendum ðæt hie wieten ðæt hie ðonne Gode suiðe licwyrðe
Ac us is suiðe geornlice to gehieranne hwæt Dryhten ðreatigende cuæð to Iudeum ðurh
He cuæð: Eall ðæt ðæt ge fæstun & weopun on ðæm fiftan & on ðam siofoðan monðe nu
Ne fæsð se no Gode ac him selfum, se ðe ðæt nyle ðearfum sellan ðæt he ðonne on mæle læfð, ac
Ac sua he sceal etan ðætte hine sio gewilnung ðære gifernesse of his modes fæsðrædnesse ne
Gehieren ða oferetolan ða word ðe Krist of his agnum muðe cuæð, he cuæð: Behealdað eow ðæt
Gehieren eac ða fæstendan hwæt he eft cuæð, he cuæð: Ne geunclænsað ðæt no ðone mon ðæt
Gehieren ða oferetolan hwæt sanctus Paulus cuæð: Fulga nu se mete ðære wambe willan, & sio
& eft he cuæð: Ne gewunigen ge to oferetolnisse & to oferdruncennisse.
& eft he cuæð: Se ofermete ne befæsð us næbre Gode.
Gehieren eft ða fæstendan hwæt he to him cuæð, he cuæð ðæt ðæm clænum wære eal clæne, &
Gehiren eft ða oferetolan hwæt he to him cuæð, he cuæð ðætte hira wamb wære hiera God, &
Gehieren eac ða fæstendan hwæt he to him cuæð, he cuæð ðæt ðæm forhæbbendum hwilum
Gehieren ða oferetolan hwæt sanctus Paulus cwæð, he cuæð ðæt hit wære good ðæt mon foreode
Gehiren ða fæstendan hwæt he eft cuæð, he cuæð ðæt ge moston drincan gewealden wines for
Forðæm he ðæt cuæð ðæt he wolde ðæt ða oferetolan geleornoden ðæt hie to ungemetlice ne
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðe hiora agnu ðing mildheortlice sellað, & on oðre
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðe hira god mildheortlice sellað, on oðre wisan ða ðe ðonne
To manienne sint ða ðe hiera mildheortlice sellað ðætte hie ne aðinden on hiora mode to ðæm
Ac ða eorðlican hlafordas sint to ðæm gesette ðæt hie ða endebyrdnesse & ða ðegnunga hiora
Ðæm hlafordum is beboden ðæt hie him doon ðæt hira ðearf sie, & ðæm ðegnum is beboden
& ðeah oft agyltað ða hlafordas, & ða menn wuniað on Godes hyldo ða ðe ryhtwise beoð, & ða
Eac sint to manienne ða ðe ðonne mildheortlice sellað ðæt hie ðonne habbað, ðæt hie ðonne
Forðæm hie hie sculon sua micle estelicor dælan sua hie ongietað ðæt him lænre & unagenre bið
Hwy sculon hie ðonne beon forðæm upahæfene & aðundene on hira mode?
Him wære ðonne micel ðearf ðæt hie leten Godes ege hie geeaðmedan.
& eac him is micel ðearf ðæt hie geornlice geðencen ðæt hie to unweorðlice ne dælen ðæt him
Ac ðonne hie hit eall ryhtlice gedæled hæbben, ðonne ne teon hie nanwuht ðæs lofes & ðæs
Ac gehieren hwæt awriten is on ðæm ærendgewrite sancte Petres: Gif hwa ðenige, ðenige he
Ac gehieren hwæt awriten is on Kristes bocum, hit is awriten: ðonne ge eall hæbben gedoon ðæt
& eft, ðylæs ða rummodnessa sio unrotnes gewemme, gehierað ðone cuide ðe sanctus Paulus
& eft, ðylæs hie for ðæm gedale ðæs feos wilnigen ðisses lænan lifes, gehieren hie ðone cwide
Ðæt is, ðonne ðonne he his ælmessan dælð, ðæt ðær ne sie wið gemenged nan gielp ðisses
Ne he ne gieme hwelce hylde he mid ðære ælmessan gewriexle, ac gehiren hwæt awriten is on
Ac ðonne ðu feorme gierwe on ælmessan laða ðærto wædlan & wanhale & healte & blinde,
& ðætte mon ðonne ðurhteon mæge, ðæt he ðæt ne forilde, ac gehieren hwæt awriten is on
Ond eft, ðylæs mon unnytlice mierre ðæt ðæt he hæbbe, gehieren menn ðisne cwide: Heald ðine
& eft ða ðe to lytel sellað ðæm ðe micles ðorfton, sculon gehieran sancte Paules cuide, he cuæð:
& ðeah ne selle mon to fela ðæm ðe lytles ðyrfe, ðylæs hwa him self weorðe to wædlan, & him
Be ðam cwæð sanctus Paulus: Ne beo ge oðrum monnum sua gifole ðæt hit weorðe eow selfum
Ðæt is ðonne hiera genihtsumnes Godes milts, ða geearnað se se on his gæste bið wædla.
Ac ðonne ðæs sellendan mod ne cann ða wædelnesse geðolian, ðonne him micles oftogen bið
Ðonne oncann he hiene selfne for ðære hrædhydignesse ðe he ær to fela sealde.
Forðy mon scel ær geðencean, ær he hwæt selle, ðæt he hit eft forberan mæge butan hreowe,
Gehieren eac ða nanwuht ne sellað ðæm ðe hie lytles hwæthwugu scoldon, hwæt to him
Eft gehieren ða ða ðe hwæthwugu sellað ðæm ðe hie nane wuht ne scoldon, hwæt to him
Doo wel ðæm eaðmodum, & ðam arleasum nanwuht.
Ond eft hit is gecweden on Tobius bocum: Sete ðin win, & lege ðinne hlaf ofer ryhtwisra monna
Se ðonne itt & drincð mid ðæm synfullum, & him selð his hlaf & his win, se ðæm unryhtwisan
Sua eac monige welige menn on ðys middangearde lætað cuelan hungre Cristes ðearfan, & fedað
Ða ðonne ðe hira hlaf sellað ðæm synfullum ðe ðearfende beoð, nalles no forðæmðe hie synnfulle
Eac sint to manianne ða ðe nu hiera mildheortlice sellað, ðæt hie geornlice giemen ðæt hie eft ða
Ne fortruwige he hiene æt ðære cipinge, ne wene he no ðæt Godes ryhtwisnes sie to ceape,
Mare is ðæt mod ðonne se mete, & se lichoma ðonne ðæt hrægl.
Ac ðonne hwa ægðer ge mete ge hrægl ðearfendum rumodlice selð, & his mod & his lichoman
Ongean ðæt sint to manigenne ða ðe ðonne giet wilniað oðre menn to reafigeanne, ðæt hie
Me ðyrste, & ge me ne sealdon drincan.
Ic wæs cuma, & ge me noldon onfon.
Ic wæs nacod, & ge me noldon bewreon.
Ic wæs untrum & on cearcerne, & ge min noldon fandian.
Ðæm monnum is gehaten ðæt he wille cueðan: Gewitað from me, awiergde, on ece fyr, ðæt wæs
Ne scirð he no hwæðer hie reafoden, oððe hwelc oðer yfel fremeden, & swaðeah cwið ðæt hie
Be ðæm we magon geðencean hu micles wites ða beoð weorðe ðe oðre menn reafiað, nu ða sua
Nu ða sua micel wite habbað ðe hira agen nyllað sellan, geðenceað ðonne hwelces wites ge
Hwæt wene ge hwæt sio ðurhtogene unryhtwisnes geearnige, nu sio unðurtogene arfæsðnes swa
Gehieren ða reaferas, ða ðe higiað wið ðæs ðæt hie willað oðre menn bereafian, hwæt be him
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt monn gadrige ðæt ðicke fenn on hine, & hine mid ðy gehefegige, ðæt se
Ac hi scoldon gehiran ðone cwide ðe bi him gecweden is in Essaies bocum, hit is gecweden: Wa
Swelce he openlice cwæde: Hu feor wolde ge nu ryman eower land?
Wolde ge nu ðæt ge næfden nanne gemacan on ðys gemænan middangearde?
Ðu cuist nu ðæt þu wille geswican ðon, ær ðe ðu genoh hæbbe.
Hwonne bið ðæt, ðæt ðe swa ðynce, oððe hwonne bið ðæt, ðæt ðu nyte hwæder ðu maran
Ac ðu findst wið hwone ðu meaht flitan.
Ac gehiere ge feohgietseras hwæt be eow gecweden is on Salomonnes bocum, hit is gecweden:
Ac him meahte cuman, gif he hi to swiðe ne lufode, & he hi wel wolde dælan.
Ac forðæmðe he hi her lufað & hielt, he hi eac her forlæt butan ælcum wæsðme & ælcum
Ac ða ðe wilniað ðæt hi her beon gefylde mid eallum welum & mid ðæm willan beoð onælede,
Swa swa fleogende fugel, ðonne he gifre bið, he gesihð ðæt æs on eorðan, & ðonne for ðæm
Swa bið ðæm gitsere.
He gesihð ðone welan ðe he wilnað, & he ne geliefð ðæs grines ðe he mid gebrogden wyrð,
Ac ða ðe wilniað ðisses middangeardes gestreona, & nyllað wietan ðone demm ðe him æfter
On ðys andweardan life we onginnað æresð libban to ðæm ðæt we æt ytemestan onfon sumne
Se ðonne ðe wilnað ðæt wolde on ðæm angienne his lifes woroldspedig weorðan mid unryhte, se
Ac ðonne hie wilniað ðurh ða nawhtgitsunga ðæt hie hira woruldspeda ie icenn, ðonne weorðað
Ac ða ðe wilniað feola to begietanne, & eac ða ðe magon begietan eall ðæt hie wilniað, gehieren
Swelce sio Soðfæsðnes openlice cwæde: Hwelc fremu bið menn ðæt he gestriene eal ðæt him
Ac mon mæg ðy hraðor ðara reafera gitsunga gestillan, gif se lareow him gerecð hu fleonde ðis
Hu hrædlice se færlica deað he on lytelre hwile bereafode ðæs ðe hi on langre hwile mid
Ðeah hie hit hrædlice ætsomne ne gestriendon, hie hit ðeah swiðe hrædlice ætsomne forluron,&
Ac hit mæg eaðe gesælan, gif we him swelc secgað, ðæt hie eac mid us ða oðre tælen, & hie
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to monianne ða ðe nohwæðer ne oðerra monna ne wilniað, ne hiora
On oðre wisan ða ðe willað sellan ðæt hi gestrinað, & ðeah nyllað geswican ðæt hi oðre men ne
On oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðe nauðer ne oðerra monna ne wilniað, ne hira agen nyllað
On oðre ða ðe hira agen willað sellan, & ðeah ne magon forlætan ðæt hie oðre menn ne reafigen.
Ac ða ðonne ðe hira agen nyllað sellan, ne eac oðerra monna ne gitsiað, hie sint to manianne ðæt
Hwæt se ðonne unryhtlice talað, se ðe talað ðæt he sie unscyldig, gif he ða good, ðe us God teo
Ac ðonne hie nyllað sellan ðæt ðæt hie underfengon, ðonne mæstað hie hie selfe on hira
Forðæm, ðonne ðonne we ðæm ðearfum hiera niedðearfe sellað, hiera ægen we him sellað,
& ryhtlicor we magon cweðan ðæt we him gielden scylde ðonne we him mildheortnesse don.
Forðæm sio Soðfæstnes, ðæt is Crist, ða ða he lærde ðæt mon ælmessan wærlice sellan sceolde,
To ðæm cwide belimpð eac ðæs psalmscopes sang ðe he sang, ða he cwæð: Se todælð his god,
Nolde he no ða rummodnesse hatan mildheortness, ac ryhtwisnes, forðæm ðætte us from ðæm
Be ðæm cwæð Salomon: Se ðe ryhtwis bið, he bið a sellende, & no ne blinð.
Eac hie sint to manienne ðæt hie geornlice geðencen ðætte se fiicbeam, ðe on ðæm godspelle
Forðæm him wearð ierre se goda wyrhta forðæm he ofergreow ðæt land butan wæsðme.
Ðonne ofergrewð se fiicbeam ðæt lond, ðonne se gitsere hyt & heleð to unnytte ðæt ðæt
Swa se fiicbeam ofersceadað ðæt lond ðæt hit under him ne mæg gegrowan, forðæm hit sio
Ac manigra manna gewuna is ðæt hie hie mid ðissum wordum ladiað, & cueðað: We brucað
Gif we nauht ðæs ne dooð ðe us mon mid goode leanian ðyrfe, ne do we eac nan woh ðe us mon
Ac forðæm hie cueðað ðas word ðe hie belucað hiera modes earan ongean ða godcundan lare.
Hwæt se weliga ðe on ðæm godspelle getæld is, & him bi gecweden is ðæt he ælce dæge
& ðeah æfter ðisse worulde he underfeng helle wite, nalles no forðyðe he awuht unaliefedes
Eac sint to manianne ða fæsðfulan ðæt hie ongieten ðæt ðæt sindon ða forman læððo ðe hie Gode
Be ðæm cwæð se psalmscop: He ne sealde Gode nanne metsceat for his saule ne nænne
Ðæt is ðonne se medsceat wið his saule ðæt he him gielde god weorc for ðære giefe ðe he him
Be ðæm ilcan cwæð Iohannes se godspellere: ælc treow mon sceal ceorfan, ðe goode wæsðmas
Nu is ðonne sio æxs aset on ðane wyrttruman ðæs treowes, ac ondræden him ðone dynt swa
Ac him is ðearf ðæt hie forlæten ða orsorgnesse & ðæt dysig hiera slæwðe, ðylæs hie wyrðen
Swa swa ðæt treow ðe ða wyrtruman beoð færcorfene forsearað, swa hie magon ondrædan ðæt
Ongean ðæt sint to manianne ða ðe willað sellan ðæt ðæt hie habbað, & ðeah nyllað forlætan
Hie sint to manigenne ðæt hie geðencen, ongemang ðæm ðe hie wilniað ðæt hie gifule ðyncen,
Hwelc mæg him mare unslæwð becuman ðonne him becymð ðurh ða ælmessan, gif he hie to
Hu, ne bið he ðonne swelce he sæwe good, & him weaxe of ðæm ælc yfel?
Ac hie sint ærest to manianne ðæt hie cunnen hiora ægen gesceadwislice gehealdan, & siððan
Forðæmðe na se ðorn ðære gitsunga ne wyrð forsearod on ðæm helme, gif se wyrttruma ne bið
Swa wyrð eac gestiered ðæm gitsere ðæs reaflaces, gif he ær ðæm gedale cann gemetgian hwæt
Siððan hie ðonne ðæt geleornod hæbben, ðonne sint hie siððan to læranne hu hie scilen
Forðæm, gif hie sua ne doð, ðonne sculon hie eft niedenga gadrian oðer ierfe on ðæs ge wriexle
Oðer is ðæt hwa for hreowsunge his synna ælmessan selle, oðer is ðæt he forðy syngige &
Ac ðæt nis nan ælmesse, forðæm hio nanne swetne wæsðm forð ne bringð, ac sona on ðæm
Forðæm Dryhten ðurh Essaias ðone witgan forcwæð swelce ælmessan, & ðus spræc: Ic eom
& eft he cwæð: Arleasra offrung bið awierged, forðæm hie beoð brohte of unryhtum gestreonum
& oft bið genumen on ðearfendum monnum ðæt ðæt hie ðonne wenað ðæt hie Gode sellen.
Ac Dryhten gecyðde ðurh Salomon ðone snottran hu micel his irsung æfter ðære dæde bið, ða he
Hwæt bið ðonne unaberendlicre to gesionne ðonne ðæs bearnes cwalu beforan ðæs fæder
Be ðæm we magon ongietan mid hu micle irre Dryhten geðyldegað ða ælmessan ðe him man of
Ac ða reaferas geðenceað swiðe oft hu micel hie sellað, & suiðe seldon hie willað gemunan hu
Ac hie sceoldon gehieran ðone cwide ðe awriten is in Ageas bocum ðæs witgan;
He cwæð: Se ðe medsceattas gaderað, he legeð hie on ðyrelne pohchan.
An ðyrelne pohchan se legð ðæt he to metsceatte sellan ðencð, se ðe wat hwær he hiene leget, &
Swa bið ðæm ðe witan willað hwæt hie sellað, & nyllað wietan mid hwelcum woo hie hit
Forðæm hie doð swelce hie hit on ðyrelne pohchan fæten, forðæm hie gemunan ðone tohopan
Ðætte on oðre wisa sint to manianne ða geðwæran, on oðre ða ungeðwæran.
On oðre wisan sint to manigenne ða gesibsuman, on oðre ða ungesibsuman.
Ða ungesibsuman sint to manigenne ðæt hie gewisslice wieten ðæt hie na on to ðæs manegum
Hit is awrieten on sancte Paules bocum ðætte ðæs gæstes wæsðm sie lufu & gefea & ryhtwislicu
Se ðe ðonne ne giemð hwæðer he ða sibbe healde, ðonne forsencð he ðone wæsðm his gæstes.
Eft cwæð sanctus Paulus: ðonne betweoxn eow bið yfel anda & geflitu, hu ne beoð ge ðonne
Ond eft he cuæð: Seccað sibbe & god to eallum mannum;
Butan ðære ne mæg nan man God gesion.
Ond eft he manode, & cuæð: Geornlice gebinde ge eow tosomne mid anmodnesse & mid sibbe,
To ðæm gebanne ðæs tohopan nan monn mæg cunnan, butan he ðider ierne mid anmodnesse
& ðeah wel manige onfoð synderlicre giefe, & ðonne ofermodgiende forlætað ða giefe ðe mare
Sua sua manige doð, gemidliað hiera giefernesse,& atemiað hira lichoman ðæt hie magon bet
Ac se ðe wille ascadan ða forhæfdnesse from ðære anmodnesse, geðence se ðone cwide ðe se
Se tympano bið geworht of drygum felle, & ðæt fell hlyt, ðonne hit mon sliehð, & on ðæm chore
Se ðonne se ðe his lichoman swencð, & ða anmodnesse wið his niehstan forlæt, se hereð Dryhten
Oft eac, ðonne hwone mara wisdom uparæð ðonne oðre menn, ðonne wile he hiene ascadan from
Ac hie sceoldon gehieran ðone cuide ðe sio Soðfæsðnes self cwæð, he cwæð: Habbað sealt on
Ðæt sealt he nemde for wisdom, forðæm he wolde ðæt we hæfden ægðer ge sibbe ge wisdom,
Forðæm sua sua he bet wat, swa he wyrs agylt, & maran demm gedrihð him selfum mid ðæm
He mæg hine ðy læs beladian ðæt he næbbe wite geearnoð ðy he meahte mid his wisdome
Ac him wæs swiðe ryhte to gecueden ðurh Iacobus ðone apostol, he cwæð: Gif ge hæbben
Forðæm se wisdom nis ufan cumen of hefenum, ac he is eorðlic & wildiorlic & eac diofullic.
Ac se se ðe of Gode cymð, he bið godes willan & gesibsum.
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt he sie clænes willan & goodes, ðæt he clænlice & ryhtwislice ongiete ðæt ðæt
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt he gesibsum sie, ðæt he hiene nanwuht ne ahebbe ofer his gelican, ne from
Ðæm ungesibsuman is to cyðanne ðæt hie wieten ðætte swa lange sua hie beoð from ðære lufe
Be ðæm is awriten on Cristes bocum: Gif ðu wille ðin lac bringan to ðæm wiofude, & ðu ðonne
Læt inc geseman ær ðu ðin lac bringe;
Brieng siððan ðin lac.
Ðæt is ðæt hwa fare mid his mode æfter his niehstan, & him unne ðæt he to ryhte gecierre.
Of ðissum bebode we magon geðencean hu unaberendlic gylt sio towesnes bið, ðonne ða lac
Nu man ælc yfel mæg mid goode adilegian, is ðæt ðonne for micel scyld ðæt gedeð ðætte nan
Ac ða ungesibsuman sint to manienne, gif hie nyllen hiera lichoman earan ontynan to
Nu we magon gecnawan on ðara ungesceadwisra nietena gesibsumnesse hu micel yfel sio
Ongean ðæt sint to manienne ða gesibbsuman, ðonne hie lufigað ða sibbe ðe hie her habbað
Ac sio stillnes ðe hie ðær wilniað oft swiðe hefelice dereð hira ingeðonce, forðæm swa him ðios
Be ðysum ilcan cwæð Crist ðurh hiene selfne, ða ða he ðas eorðlican sibbe toscead & ða
Swelce he cwæde: Ic eow onlæne ðas gewitendan, & ic eow geselle ða ðurhwiniendan.
Gif ðonne ðæs monnes mod & his lufu bið behleapen eallunga on ða lænan sibbe, ðonn ne mæg
Ac swa is ðios andwearde sib to habbanne ðæt we hie sculon lufian, & ðeah oferhyggean, ðylæs
Eac sint to manianne ða gesibsuman ðæt hie to ungemetlice ðære sibbe ne wilnigen, ðylæs hie
Ðylæs, ðonne he him ondrædt ða towesnesse utane, he sie innan asliten from ðæm geðoftscipe
Hwæt is elles ðios gewitendlice sibb, buton swelce hit sie sum swæð ðære eccean sibbe?
Hwæt mæg bion dyslicre ðonne hwa lufige hwelcre wuhte spor on ðæm duste, & ne lufige ðæt
Be ðæm cwæð Dauid, ða he hine eallunga geðiedde to ðæm geðoftscipe ðære incundan sibbe, he
& for ðinum feondum ic aswand on minum mode, & mid fulryhte hete ic hie hatode, forðæm hie
Swa mon ðonne sceal fullfremedlice Godes fiend hatigean, ðætte mon lufige ðæt ðæt hie beoð, &
He sceal weorðan his life to nytte mid ðy ðæt he næte his unðeawas.
Ac hu wene we hu micel scyld ðæt sie ðæt monn aðreote ðære nætinge yfelra monna, & nime
Forðæm wæs ðæte Leuis kynn gefengon hiera sweord, & eodon ut ðurh ðone here, sleande ða
& forðæm hit is awriten ðæt hiera honda wæren gehalgode Gode, forðæmðe hie ne sparodan ða
Be ðæm wæs eac ðætte Fines forseah his neahgebura freondscipe, ða he ofslog his agene
& eft cwæð Crist ðurh hine selfne: Ne wene ge no ðæt ic to ðæm come on eorðan ðæt ic sibbe
Forðæm, ðonne we us unwærlice geðiedað to yfelra monna freondscipe, ðonne gebinde we us to
Be ðys ilcan wæs ðætte Gesaphað, se ðe ær on eallum dædum his lifes wæs to herigenne,
He wæs geleahtrad from Gode, ðurh ðone witgan him wæs to gecweden: ðu fultemodes ðæm
Ðæt wæs ðæt ðu adydes ða bearwas of Iudea londe.
Be ðæm we magon gehieran ðætte sua micle sua we us swiður geðiedað & gemodsumiað to ðæra
Eac sint to monigenne ða gesibsuman ðæt hie him ne ondræden ðæt hie ðas lænan sibbe ongean
Ond eft hie sint to manianne ðæt hie ðeah ða sibbe anwealge oninnan him gehealden, ða ðe hie
Ægðer ðara Dauid sægde ðæt he swiðe wærlice beheolde, ða he cwæð: Ic lufode ða ðe sibbe
Hie oncuðon hiene for ðære cease, & he wæs ðeah hiora freond;
Ne aðreat hine no ðæt he ða dysegan ne tælde, & ðeah ðe hie hine tældon, he hie lufode.
Be ðys ilcan cwæð eft Paulus: Ic wolde, gif hit swa beon meahte, ðæt ge wið ælcne monn
Forðæm he cwæð: Gif hit swa beon meahte, & eac he cwæð: Iowres gewealdes, forðæm he
Ac us is swiðe micel ðearf, ðeah ðeos hwilendlice sibb for ure cease gedrefed weorðe on ðæra
Forðæm he cwæð bi ðære sibbe: Iowres gewealdes, forðæm sio sib mid ryhte beon sceal ægðer
Gif hio ðonne of oðres gewite, on ðæs oðres hio ðurhwunige.
Be ðæm se ilca Paulus on oðre stowe monode his gingran, & ðus cwæð: Swa hwa swa urum
& eft æfter ðæm he cwæð: Ne scule ge wið hine gebæran swa swa wið feond, ac ge him sculon
Swelce he openlice cwæde: Forlætað ða uterran sibbe, & habbað ða innerran fæste, ðætte eower
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to monianne ða wrohtgeornan, on oðre ða gesibsuman.
On oðre wisan sint to monigenne ða ðe wrohte sawað, on oðre ða gesibsuman.
Ða wrohtgeornan sint to manigenne ðæt hie geðencen hwæs folgeras hie sindon.
Be ðæm aworpnan engle is awriten on ðæm godspelle ðæt he sewe ðæt weod on ða godan
Forðy wæs bi him gecweden: Sum mon ðis dyde ðe ure feond wæs.
Be ðæs ilcan feondes limum is ðus gecweden ðurh Salomon: Aworpen man bið a unnyt, & gæð
Her we magon gehieran, ða he be ðæm wrohtgeornan secgean wolde, ðæt he hine nemde se
Forðon, gif he ær on ðæs ofermodan engles wisan innan his geðance of Godes gesiehðe ne
Be ðæm is ryhtlice awriten ðæt he bicne mid ðæm eagum, & sprece mid ðæm fingrum, & trit mid
Forðæmðe innor bið se hierde, ðæt is se willa, se hielt ða leomu utan.
Forðæm, ðonne mon ða fæstrædnesse his modes innan forlist, ðonne bið he hwilum swiðe
Ac gehiren ða wrohtsaweras hwæt awriten is on ðæm godspelle, hit is awriten: Eadige beoð ða
Be ðæm worde we magon geðencean, nu ða sint Godes bearn genemned ðe sibbe wyrcað, ðætte
Forðæm, ðeah he hwelcne wæstm forðbrenge godes weorces, gif he ne bið of godum willan &
Geðencen be ðissum ða wrohtsaweras hu monigfaldlice hie gesyngiað, ðonne hie ðæt an yfel
Mid ðam anum yfele hie gefremmað unrim oðerra yfela, forðam ða ðe ða worhte sawað, hie
Forðonðe nan cræft nis Gode deorwyrðra ðonne sio lufu, ne eft ðæm deofle nan cræft leoftælra
Swa hwa ðonne swa ða wrohte bið sawende, & mid ðy ða sibbe ofslihð on his geferan, he bið
Ongean ðæt sint to manienne ða ðe ða sibbe sawað, ðæt hie swa micel weorc to recceleaslice &
Forðæm, gif ða ðweoran & ða unryhtwisan hiera yfel mid sibbe gefæstnigað, & tosomne
Be ðæm wæs ðætte sio godcunde stemn cwæð to ðæm eadgan Iobe ymb ða bodan ðæs idlan
& eft hio dyde sciella to bisene his heorðcneohtum, & ðus cwæð: ælces fisces sciell bið to
Swa eac ða his folgeras, swa hie unwiðerweardran & gemodran beoð, swa hie swiður hlecað
Swa eac se se ðe ða unryhtwisan tosomne sibbað, he seleð ðære unryhtwisnesse fultom &
Be ðæm cwæð se æðela lareow sanctus Paulus, ða he geseah ðæt folc Phariseo & Saducia
Hu, ne eom ic eower gefera, & eom Fariscisc swa same swa ge?
Ond forðæm min monn eft ðe ic bodige ymb ðone tohopan deadra monna ærestes.
Forðæm he cwæð ða word, forðæm ða Saducie antsacodon ðære æriste æfter deaðe, & ða
Swa he tosced ðara ehtera anmodnesse ðe hine ær woldon fordon, & Paulus com gesund ðonon.
Eac sint to manianne ða ðe on ðam beoð abisgode ðæt hie sibbe tiligað, ðæt hie ærest tilgen to
Forðon, ðonne ðonne hie geðencað ða ryhtan lufe, ðæt hie eac geðencen ðæt hie ne weorðen
Ond eft ðonne sume yfele menn swa gerade beoð ðæt hie ne magon godum monnum derian,
Ðætte hie ðurh ða menniscan sibbe mægen astigan to ðære godcundan sibbe, ðeah hio him
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðe ða halgan æ ryhtlice ongietan ne cunnon;
