On oðre wisan sint to manigenne ða ðe ða halgan æ ryhtlice ongietan ne cunnon;
On oðre ða ðe hie ryhtlice ongietan cunnon, & ðeah for eaðmodnesse swigiað ðæt hie hie ne
Ða sint to maniene ðe ða æ ryhtlice ne ongietað, ðætte hie geðencen ðætte hie ðone halwendan
Eac hie sint to manigenne ðæt hie geðencen ðæt ða halgan gewritu sint us to leohtfatum gesald,
Ac ðonne hwa ne cann ða ryhtlice ongietan, ðonne bið him ðæt leoht aðiestrod.
Ne gehwyrfde hine næfre ðæt unryhtwise ingeðonc to ðæm won andgiete, gif he ær nære on
Ac ðonne hie wenað ðæt hie wisran sien selfe ðonne oðre, ðonne forhyggeað hie ðæt hie folgien
Higiað ðonne ealle mægene ðæt hie ðæt gedwellen ðæt oðre menn ryhtlice & gesceadwislice
Be ðæm wæs swiðe wel gecweden ðurh Amos ðone witgan, he cwæð: Hie sindon ða Galatiscan
Ðæt folc wæs gehaten Galað on Ebrisc, ðæt is on Englisc gewitnesse heap, forðæm eal sio
Ðy is swiðe ryhte getacnod ðurh Galates naman sio halige gesomnung, forðæmðe ealle ða
Ðonne getacniað ða geeacniendan wif ða saule ðe ða gebodu angietað, & hie mid godcundlicre
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt mon his mearce bræde, ðæt mon his hlisan & his naman mærsige.
Ac ðonne mon snið ða bearneacnan wif on Galað hira mearce mid to rymanne, ðonne ða
Ac hie wilniað mid ðy ðæt hie mon herige, & cweðe ðæt hie sin wise lareowas.
Ac gif we wilnigen ðæt hie ðæs wos geswicen, ðonne sculon we hie ealra ðinga ærest &
Eac hie sint to manigenne ðætte hie ðurh hiera gedwolan & ðurh hiera ungeðwærnesse ða Godes
Be ðæm Dryhten siofigende cwæð ðurh Ossei ðone witgan, he cwæð: Ic him sealde hwæte & win
Ic him sealde genog, & ðæt hie worhton to diofulgieldum Bale hiera gode.
Ac ðonne we underfoð ðone hwæte æt Gode, ðonne we ongietað inweardlice ða æ, & onwreoð ða
& ðonne us selð God his win, ðonne he us oferdrencð mid ðære lare dioplices andgites.
& ðonne he us selð his ele, ðonne he us his bebodu openlicor gecyð, & mid ðæm ure lif liðelice
& ðonne he us seleð micel siolfor, ðonne he us selð micle getyngnesse & wlitige spræce ymb
Ðonne he us gewelegað mid golde, ðonne he ure heortan onlieht & gebierht mid ðæm sciman
Ðis is eall ryhtwisra monna offrung Gode, ac hit briengað eal ða gedwolmen Bale, mid ðæmðe
Ðonne hie doð ðone hwæte & ðæt win & ðone ele & ðæt siolfor to diofulgieldum, ðonne hie ða
Forðæm hie sint to manigenne ðæt hie geornlice geðencen, ðonne hie mid forhwirfede mode

Forðæm sint to monigenne ða ðe eaðmodlice nyllað læran Godes word, ðætte ðonne ðonne hie ða
Ac hie man sceal manian ðæt hie geðencen ðætte hie selfe ne geunðwærigen ðæm wordum ðe

Ac hie scoldon gehieran ðone cuide ðe sanctus Paulus cwæð to Corintheum, he cwæð: Sua sua
Se ðonne spricð of Gode beforan Gode, se ðe ongiet ðæt he ða word ðære lare from Gode
Hie sculon gehieran ðone cuide ðe awriten is on Salomonnes bocum, hit is awriten ðætte God
Se ðonne ðe mid Godes wordum his agenne gielp secð, he wile reafian ðone ðe hie him sealde
& ne ondræt him no, ðeah ðe he do God behindan hine, ðeah ðe him God geafe ðæt ðæt ðær
Eac hie sculon gehieran hwæt to ðæm lareowum gecweden is ðurh Salomon, hit is gecweden:
Ðonne drincð se lareow ðæt wæter of his agnum mere, ðonne he gehwirfð æresð to his agnum
Ðonne he drincð of ðæm wielme his agnes pyttes, ðonne he bið self geðwæned & wel gedrenced
Swiðe wel wæs ðios spræc mid ðy geieced ðe Salomon cwæð: Læt forð ðine willas, & todæl ðin
Ðæt is ðætte se lareow ærest sceal self drincan of ðæm wille his agenre lare, & siððan geotan
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt mon his wætru utlæte, ðæt se lareow mid ðy cræfte his lareowdomes utane on
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt mon his wætru todæle æfter cyninga herestrætum, ðæt mon ða godcundan
Ac forðonðe oft sio wilnung ðæs idlan gielpes gegripð ðæt mod ðæs lareowes, ðonne he ongiet
He mænde ða awiergedan gæstas.
Bi ðæm spræc Dauid swiðe cuðlice on psalmum, swa he hit oft acunnad hæfde, he cwæð:
We cwædon ær ðæt Salomon cwæde ðæt mon sceolde his wætru todælan, & ðeah him self eall
Swelce he openlice cwæde: Eow is micel ðearf ðæt ge swa ætfeolen ut ðære lare, swa swa ge eow
Ac ðonne we doð ægðer, ge we ða wætru todælað æfter kyninga herestrætum, ge eac us selfe
Ðætte on oðre wisa sint to manianne ða ðe medomlice cunnon læran, & ðeah for miclum ege &
& on oðre wisan ða ðe ðonne giet to ðæm gewintrede ne beoð ne geðiegene, & ðeah for
On oðre wisan sint to maniane ða ðe medomlice læran magon, & ðeah for micelre eaðmodnesse
On oðre ða ða ðe unmedome bioð to ðære lare oððe for gioguðe oððe for unwisdome, & ðeah
Ða ðonne sint to manigenne ðe nyttweorðlice læran meahton, & ðeah for ungemetlicere
Hwæt, hie witon, gif hiera niehstan friend weorðað wædlan, & hie feoh habbað, & his ðonne him
Hwy ne magon hie ðonne geðencean, gif hie on ðæm gesyngiað, hu micle swiður hie gesyngiað,
Be ðæm cwæð Salomon: Hu nytt bið se forholena cræft oððe ðæt forhydde gold?
Oððe gif hwelc folc bið mid hungre geswenced, & hwa his hwæte & oðhielt, hu ne wilt he ðonne
Be ðissum magon ongietan ða lareowas hwelces wites hi wyrðe bioð, ðonne hie lætað ða sawla
Bi ðæm wæs swiðe wel gecweden ðurh Salomon: Se ðe his hwæte hytt, hine wiergð ðæt folc.
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt mon his hwæte ahyde, ðæt se lareow gehyde ða word ðære halegan lare.
Forðæm he for ðære anre scylde ðære swigan bið awierged & fordemed from ðæm folce,

Eft cwæð Moyses be ðæm ilcan: Min sweord itt flæsc.
Gehieren eac ða ðe oninnan him gedigliað & gehydað ða godcundan lare ðone egeslican Godes
Eac hi sculon gehiran ðætte sanctus Paulus geliefde ðæt he swa micele unscyldigra wære his
Hi magon gehieran be ðæm cuide ðe he cwæð, he cwæð: Hwæt ge sint ealle mine gewietan ðæt
Eac hi sculon gehieran hu sanctus Iohannes wæs gemanod mid ðæs engles stemne, ða ða he
Ðæt is, se ðe ongiete ðæt he sie gecieged mid godcundre stemne, ðætte he eac cigende &
Hie scoldon gehieran hu Essaias se witga hreowsigende hine selfne tælde, ða he wæs onæled
Eac hie sculon gehieran ðætte ðurh Salomon is gehaten ðæm monnum ðe lustlice & unslawlice
Salomon cuæð: ðæs monnes sawl ðe wel spricð hio bið amæst, ond swa hwa swa oðerne drencð,
Swa eac se ðe ut wel lærð mid his wordum, he onfehð innan ðæs inngeðonces fætnesse, ðæt is
Swa eac se ðe ne wirnð ðæs wines his lare ða mod mid to oferdrencanne ðe hine gehieran willað,
Hwæt we hirdon ðætte Dauid brohte Gode to lacum ðæt ðæt he ða lare ne hæl ðe him God geaf.
Ðæt he cyðde, ða he cwæð: Dryhten, ðu wast ðæt ic ne wyrne minra welera, & ðine
Ðine hælo & ðine ryhtwisnesse ic secgge.
Gehierað hwæt on Cantica Canticorum is awriten ðæt se brydguma scolde sprecan to ðære
Ðæt is sio halige gesomnung Godes folces, ðæt eardað on æppeltunum, ðonne hie wel begað
Ðæt bið ðonne ðæt mon his stemne gehiere, ðonne ða gecorenan menn giornfulle bioð his worda
& ðonne wilnað se brydguma, ðæt is Crist, ðæt he gehire ða stemne ðære bryde, ðæt is Cristenra
Eac hie sculon gehieran hwæt Moyses dyde, ða he ongeat ðæt God wæs ðæm folce ierre, he
He cwæð: Se ðe Godes ðegn sie, ga hider to me, & do his sweord to his hype, & gað from geate
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt mon his sweord doo ofer his hype, ðæt mon ða geornfulnesse his lare læte
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt mon ierne from geate to oðrum, ðæt he ierne ðreatigende from ðara unðeawa
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt mon ierne ðurh midde ða ceastre, ðætte mon sua emn sie betweox cristenum
Be ðæm wæs swiðe ryhtlice gecweden ðæt mon scolde ofslean his broður & his freond & his
Ðonne mon ofslihð his broður & his friond & his niehstan, ðonne he for nanre sibbe ne wandað
Nu se is ðonne gehaten Godes ðegn se ðe mid ðæm andan onæled bið godcundre lufan
Ongean ðæt sint to manianne ða ðe nabbað nawðer ne ildo ne wisdom to ðon ðæt hie mægen
Ac ðonne hie him ær tide to tioð ðæt hi ne magon ne ne cunnon, ðonne is him to ondrædanne

Eac hie sint to manigenne ðæt hie geðencen, gif mon on niwne weall unadrugodne &

Be ðissum ilcan cwæð Salomon on Cantica Canticorum ðære bec be ðære halgan gesomnunge,
Sio winestre hand Godes he cwæð wære under his heafde.
Ðæt tacnað orsorgnesse ðisses andweardan lifes.
Ða hand ðonne geðrycð sio incunde lufu ðæs uplican lifes.
Sio swiðre hand hine ðonne beclipð, ðonne ðonne he hine gehielt on ðæm willan ðæt he mid
Eft wæs gecueden ðurh Salomon ðone snottran ðætte on his swiðran handa wære lang lif, & on
Ða he lærde hu we ægðer lufian sceolden, ða he mæt ðone welan & ðone wyrðmynd to ðære
Eft be ðæm ilcan cwæð se psalmsceop: Gehæle me ðin sio swiðre.
Ne cwæð he no ðin sio winestre hond, ac ðin sio swiðre.
Mid ðæm he gecyðde ðæt he ne mænde ðis andwearde lif, ac ðæs ecean lifes hælo he sohte.
Be ðæm ilcan is eft awriten on Exodo, ðæt is Moyses boc, hit is awriten: ðin swiðre hand,
Ða ðonne ðe Godes fiond bioð, ðeah hi on ðære winstran handa bion geðigene, hi beoð mid
Ðy sint to manienne ða ðe on ðisse worulde orsorglice libbað, ðæt hie geornlice ongieten ðætte
Forhwam wæs elles Canonea land Israhela folce gehaten, buton forðæmðe ðæt ungetyde folc
Ond eac forðæm ðætte hie ðy fæsðlicor & ðy untweogendlicor gelifden ðara ecena ðinga, swa
Ðæt eac gecyðde se psalmsceop swiðe openlice, ða he cwæð: He him sealde ricu oðerra kynrena,
Ac ðonne ðæt mennisce mod Godes glædmodnesse mid godum weorcum ne geandsworað, ðonne
Be ðæm wæs eft gecweden ðurh ðone salmsceop: ðu hie geniðrades, ða hi hi selfe upahofon.
Swa, ðonne ðonne unnyttan men ða godcundan gife nyllað leanian mid ryhtum weorcum, ac
Be ðys ilcan wæs eac gecweden be ðæm welegan ðe gesæd is ðætte on helle ðrowude, hit wæs
Forðæm anfehð se yfla auht goodes on ðisse worulde ðæt he eft ðy maran yfles on ðæm
Angean ðæt sint to manigenne ða ðe ðises middangeardes wilna & weolena wilniað, & him
Swa swa se læce, ðonne he ðæm siocan ne truwað, & wenð ðæt his gehelpan ne mæge, ðonne
Hwæt we eac wiernað urum cildum urra peninga mid to plegianne, ðæm ilcum ðe we eft
Ac nimen him nu be ðisse bisene gefean & tohopan ðære ecan ierfeweardnesse, ða ðe sio
Eac sint to manigenne ða ðe ðissa hwilendlicra ðinga wilniað, & him ðeah sum broc & sumu
Ða ða he ðegn wæs, he mette his feond, & ðeah for Godes ege & for ryhtwisnesse lufum he hine
Ac eft siððan he kyning wæs, for lare & for tiehtinge his agenes firenlustes he ofslog & besirede
Hwa mæg ðonne æhta oððe anwaldes oððe weorðscipes wilnian butan plio, nu se swelc plioh
Hwa mæg ðonne for ðyllecum bion gehealden butan miclum gesuince & miclum plio, nu se on
Eac hie sint to manigenne ðæt hie geðencen hu hit awriten is be Salamonne, hu he æfter swa
Nis hit no gesæd ðæt he ænig wuht wiðerweardes on ðys middangearde hæfde, ærðæmþe he
Ac siððan him se wisdom to forlæten wæs eallunga he forget hine selfne & ða lare & ðone
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðe beoð gebundne mid synrædenne, on oðre wisan ða
On oðre wisan sint to manienne ða ðe mid synnrædenne bioð gebundene, on oðre ða ðe ðara
Ða sint to manigenne ðe mid ðæm gebundene bioð, ðonne ðonne hie betwuh him ðenceað hu
& ðæt hie swa wyrcen ðisses middangeardes weorc ðæt hie ne forlæten to wilnianne ðara ðe
& ðonne hie ongieten hu gewitendlic ðis andwearde bið ðæt ðæt hie her doð, & hu
& eft se wena ðara toweardena yfela on ðæm toweardan dome hie geegesige on ðære
Forðæm ðæt mod ðara cristenra gesamhiwena, ðætte bið ægðer ge trum ge untrum & ne mæg
Ond ðeah he hæbbe hwæt eorðlices & mennisclices him on gewunan on ðys wege se weg is ðis
Ymb ðæt reahte Paulus swiðe wel mid feaum wordum on his ærendgewrite to Corinctheum, he
Se ðonne hæfð wif swelce he nan næbbe, se ðe hit hæfð for licumlicre frofre, & ðeah for ðæm
Se hæfð eac wif swelce he nan næbbe, se ðe ongiet ðætte eal ðas andweardan ðing bioð
Ðæt is ðonne ðæt mon wepe, & ne wepe, ðæt mon ða iermðo ðisses middangeardes wepe, &
& eft is ðæt mon blissige & ne blissige ðæt mon ahebbe his mod of ðissum eorðlican to ðæm
Ymb ðæt swiðe wel ðærryhte æfter rehte sanctus Paulus, ða he cwæð: ðyses middangeardes
Swelce he openlice cwæde: Ne sculon ge no eallunga to swiðe lufian ðisne middangeard,
On idelnisse ge fæstniað eower mod on him, forðæmðe he eow flihð, ðeah ge hine lufigen
To manigenne sint ða gesomhiwan, ðeah hira hwæðrum hwæthwugu hwilum mislicige on
& gebidde hira ægðer for oðer ðæt hie mægen ðurh ðæt weorðan gehælede, forðæm hit awriten
Sio lufu ðonne is Godes æ: sio æ & sio lufu us briengað monig god from Criste, & ure yfelu
Ac ðonne we onhyrigað Criste, & eac ða onhyringe gefyllað, ðonne we lustlice sellað oðrum ðæt
Ða gesinhiwan mon sceal manian, & eac gehwelcne mon, ðæt hie no læs ne ne geðencen hwæt
Forðæmðe he mæg micle ðy ieð adreogan ða tionan ðe him oðre men doð, gif he wile gemunan
Eac sint to manigenne ða gesinhiwan ðæt hi gemunen ðæt hie for nanum oðrum ðingum ne bioð
Forðæm hi sculon geðencean, gif hie to oftrædlice & to ungemetlice hie gemengað on ðæm
Forðæm wæs ðæt se getyda læce ðæs hefonlican læcedomes, ðæt wæs sanctus Paulus, ægðer ge
& eft he cwæð: God bið mannum ðæt ælc hæbbe his agen wif, & ælc wif hire ceorl, ðylæs hi on
Ægðer he dyde, ge he egesode ða ðe on unryht hæmdon, ge he liefde ðæm ðe hit forberan ne
& eft he cwæð to ðæm untrumum: Agife se wer his wife hire ryht on hira gesinscipe, & swa
Ac æfterðæmðe he hwelcehwugu gerisenlice leafe dyde ðæm gesinhiwon hira willan to
Ða he spræc gelicost ðæm ðe hit hwelchwugu syn wære, ða he cwæð ðæt he hit forgiefan wolde
Forðæm bið sio scyld ðy hraðor gehæled, forðæmðe hio ne bið unliefedo, ac ðeah hio aliefedu
Ðæt us getacnode Loth swiðe wel on him selfum, ða he fleah ða biernendan ceastre Sodoman, &
Midðæmðe he fleah ða birnendan Sodoman, he getacnode ðæt we sculon fleon ðone unliefedan
Sio heanes ðonne ðara munta getacnað ða clænnesse ðære forhæfdnesse.
Ða ðonne bioð swelce hi eardigen upp on ðæm munte ða ðe bioð gesponnene to gesinscipe, &
Ðonne hie stondað up on ðæm munte, ðonne ðæt flæsc nauht elles ne secð to ðæm oðrum buton
Ðæt is ðæt mon stonde on ðæm munte ðæt ðæt flæsc ne sie flæsclice to ðæm oðrum gefæsðnod.
Ac monige bioð ðara ðe hie gehealdað wið unryhthæmed, & swaðeah his agenra ryhthiwena ne
Loth for ut of Sodoman to Segor, & ðeah ne com he nauht hraðe onuppan ðæm muntum.
Swa, ðonne ðonne mon forlæt ðæt wyrreste lif, & ne mæg ðeah ðonne git cuman to ðæm
Sio Segor gehælde Loth fleondne.
Swa deð sio Segor ðæs medemestan lifes: ða ðe hire to befleoð hio gehælð.
Ac ðonne ða gesinhiwan hi gemengað ðurh ungemetlice unforhæfdnesse, ðær ðær hi ðone fiell
Ðæt lif ðara gesinhiwena, ðeah hit ful wundorlic ne sie on mægenum weoruldwilnungum to
Forðæm cwæð Loth to ðæm engle: Her is an lytele burg swiðe neah, ðær ic mæg min feorh on
Hio is an lytel, & ðeah ic mæg ðæron libban.
He cwæð ðæt hio wære swiðe neah, & ðeah genog fæst on his hælo.
Swa is ðæt lif ðara gesinhiwena.
Nis hit naht feor ascaden from ðisse worulde, ne eac noht fremde ðære ecan hælo, forðæm for
Be ðæm wæs swiðe ryhte gecweden ðurh ðone engel to Lothe: ðinre bene ic wille nu onfon, &
Swa bið ðæt lif ðara gesinhiwena.
Ne bið hit no fordemed beforan Gode,gif ðær gebedo æfter fylgeað.
Ymb ða illcan gebedo sanctus Paulus manode, & ðus cwæð: Ne fornime incer noðer oðer ofer
Ongean ðæt sint to manigenne ða ðe ne beoð gebundne mid ðæm gesinscipe, ða sint to
Ðonne is him micel ðearf ðætte sio unliefde byrðen ðissa eorðlicena sorga hi ne geðrysce, ðætte
& ðonne hi geæmetgade bioð ðæt hie magon bet don ðonne oðre menn, & hit swaðeah
Ac hi scoldon gehiran hwæt Paulus cwæð, ða ða he sume men manode to ðære giefe Godes
Ne cwæð he ðeah no ðæt ðæt he cwæð forðæmðe he gesinscipe tælde, ac forðæmðe he wolde ða
He cwæð: ðis ic cweðe for eowerre ðearfe, ðylæs ic eow mid ænige grine gefoo.
Ic eow secgge hwæt eow arwyrðlicost is to beganne, & hu ge fullecost magon Gode ðiowian
Forðæm of ðæm gesinscipe weaxað eorðlice ymbhogan & sorga, forðæm se æðela ðioda lareow
Forðæm, ðonne se Godes ðiow on ðæt gemearr ðære woruldsorga befehð, ðeah he ðonne hæbbe
Eac sint to manienne ða Godes ðiowas ðæt hie ne wenen ðæt hie butan ðæm demme stranges
He gecyðde hwelc sio scyld bið, ða he cwæð: Nawðer ne ða wohhæmendan, ne ða ðe
& eft he cwæð: ðæm wohhæmerum demeð Dryhten.
Forðæm hi sint to manigenne, gif hie ða halwendan forhæfdnesse gehabban ne mægen, & ða
Forðæm hit is awriten ðæt hit sie betere ðæt mon gehiewige ðonne he birne, forðæm butan
Ac se ðe mare god gehet ðonne he ær dyde, he gedeð mid ðæm ðæt læsse god unaliefed ðæt he
Hit is awrieten on ðæm godspelle ðæt nan mon ne scyle don his hond to ðære sylg, & hawian
Ne ðon ma se ðe gehat gehæt, ne wene he ðæt he sie a ðy near hefonrice, gif he hine from went
Forðæm se ðe hine selfne maran godes behæt, & ðonne forlæt ða maran god, & went hine to ðæm
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manienne ða ðe gefandod habbað ðara flæsclicra synna, on oðre
On oðre wisan sint to manienne ða ðe ongietað & witon hiera lichoman synna, on oðre ða ða ðe
Ða sint to manienne ðe hiera lichoman synna onfunden habbað, ðæt hie huru æfter ðæm
Ðætte ða ða ðe mildheortlice bioð gehealdne æfter hiora ðurhtogenum synnum, ðætte hi eft
Forðæm is gecweden to ðære syngiendan sawle, ðe næfre hire synna geswican nyle: ðu hæfst
Ðeah hie sint to manienne ðæt hie geornlice giemen, ðeah hi ðæt god hira gecynde gehal nolden
Him is ðearf ðæt hie geðencen hu micel menigu ðæra getreowfulra bið, ðe ægðer ge hi selfe
Ac hwæt cweðað hi ðonne, ðonne ða oðre stondað on anwalgre hælo, & hie nyllað æfter yfelre
Oððe hwæt cweðað hi, ðonne ða oðre briengað ægðer ge hie selfe ge eac oðre mid hiora
Ond hie, ðonne him God ðone first alefð, & him hira yffel forbierð, nyllað furðum hie selfe
Ac hie sint to manienne ðæt hie gemunen hwæt hi godes ær forleton ðæs ðe hi don meahton,
Be ðæm cwæð Dryhten to ðæm gewundedum modum ðurh Ezechiel ðone witgan, swelce he to
On Egiptum beoð hira breost gehnescod, ðonne hi ða scandlican lustas ðisses middangeardes mid
Ond eft on Egiptum bioð forbrocene ða wæstmas ðæra dela, ðonne ðæt gecyndelice gewitt ærest
Forðæm sint to manienne ða ðe hiera synna onfunden habbað, ðætte hie mid wacore mode
Swa swa he ðurh Ieremias ðone witgan cwæð, he cwæð: Gif hwelc wif forlæt hiere ceorl, &
Hwæt ðu ðonne eart forlegen wið manigne copenere, & swaðeah ic cweðe: Gecier eft to me,
He gereahte ðone ryhtestan dom be ðæm forlegenan & ðæm aworpnan wife, & swaðeah us
Of ðissum wordum we magon oncnawan, nu he us sparað mid swa micelre mildheortnesse,
Be ðære miltsunga æfter ðære laðunga is swiðe wel gesæd ðurh Essaias ðone witgan;
Hit is gecweden to ðæm wiðerweardan men: ðin eagan weorðað gesionde ðinne bebiodend, &
Eall moncynn wæs to Gode gewend, ða ða hi ærest gesceapene wæron on neorxna wonge;
& he ða hie manode andwearde, & him forgeaf ðæt hie moston stondan on frioum anwalde, &
Ða giet stodon men to him gewende.
Ac ða hie wendon hiera bæc to him, ða hi ofermodgiende his gebod forhogdon.
& ðeah, ðeah hi hine oferhogden , ne forhogde he hi no ne ne forlet: ðæt he gecyðde, ða ða he
Ða he ðis eal dyde, ða he stod æfter us gewend, & clipode æfter us, ðeah we from him gewende
& ðeah he oferhogod wære, he us eft laðude to his hyldo.
Ac swa swa we nu ðis reahton be eallum monnum, swa hit mæg æghwelc mon be him anum
Ðonne giet he stent beforan him, ðonne he hine ne forhygeð, ac for his ege forbierð ðæt he ne
Ac ðonne he forlæt his godnesse & his unsceaðfulnesse, & gecist unryhtwisnesse, & ða
Ac ðeah ðonne giet him fylgð God, & him æfter cliopað, ðonne he hine monað æfter ðære
Ða scylda he nyle gesion, & ðone fromweardan he ciegeð, & ðone greadan his arfæstnesse &
Ðonne we gehirað under bæc ðæs maniendes stemne, ðonne we to him gecierrað, ðonne ðonne
Hit is cyn ðæt we ure scomigen, ðonne he us æfter cliopað, gif we us nyllað ondrædan his
Ongean ðæt sint to manigenne ða ðe ðonne giet ungefandod habbað flæsclicra scylda, ðætte hie
Hi sint to manienne ðæt hi witen swa swa hie on hira stede gestondað swa him mare gescot &
Forðæm he ongit swa micle swiður him on feohtan swa he hine selfne untrumran gefred on his
Ac gif he ðonne ðæm wiðstent, ðonne ongit he swa micle maran sige on him selfum swa he
Ac hie sint to manienne ðæt hie unablinnendlice ðara leana wilnigen, & lustlice ðæt geswinc
Hi sculon gehieran hwæt ðurh Essaias ðone witgan gecweden is, he cwæð: ðis cwið Dryhten: ða
Hwæt elles getacniað ða afyrdan buton ða ða ðe ofðryscað ða styringe ðæs flæsclican lustes, &
Ðæm monnum is gecyðed hwelce stowe hi moton habban beforan urum fæder, swa swa we ær
Hi sculon gehieran hwæt ðurh sanctus Iohannes gecweden is, he cwæð: ðæt sindan ða ða ðe mid
Ða folgiað ðæm lambe, swa hwær swa hit færð.
Ða singað ðone sang ðe nan mon elles singan ne mæg, buton ðæt hundteontig & feowertig &
Ðæm is sundorlic sang to singanne mid ðæm lambe on ecnesse beforan eallum geleaffullum, &
Gehieren eac ða ðe ungefandod habbað ðara flæsclicana scylda hwæt sio soðfæsðnes ðurh hie
Mid ðæm worde he cyðde ðæt hit is se hiehsta cræft, forðæm he cwæð ðæt hine ealle ne
Eac sint to manienne ða ðe ungefandod habbað ðæs lichoman scylda ðæt hie witen ðæt se
Ac læten ðæt lif ðæs mægðhades beforan ðæm oðrum, & hine selfne biæftan, & ne forlæte ðeah
Hi sint to manienne ðæt hi ongieten ðætte oft gebyreð ðætte ðæt lif ðara gesinhiwena oferstigð
Be ðæm wæs swiðe wel gecweden ðurh Essaias ðone witgan to ðære byrig ðe Sidon hatte, sio
Ðæ cwæð se witga: ðios sæ cwið ðæt ðu ðin scamige, Sidon.
Swelce sio burg ða wære ðurh ðæs sæs stemne to scame geworden.
Swa bið ðis eorðlice lif oft yðgiende swa swa sæ, & ðeah bið oft swiðe acorenlic, & ðæt oðer
Oft weorðað monige æfter ðæs lichoman scylde to Gode gecerred, & hi ðonne swa micle
& oft ða, ða ðe on clænnesse hiora lichoman gehealdenne habbað, swa swa hi læsse ongietad on
Ðonon wyrð oft Gode leofre ðæt lif ðætte æfter his synnum onæled bið mid hreowsunga ðonne
Be ðæm cwæð ðæs Deman stemn, ðæt is Crist, be Marian ðære forlegisse: Hire sint forgifena
Ond eft he cwæð: Mara gefea wyrð on hefonum for anum hreowsiendum ðonne ofer nigon &
Ðæt we magon swutolor ongietan & hræðor bi urum agnum gewunan, gif we willað ongietan
Hwæt, we witon ðæt we ma lufiað ðone æcer ðe ær wæs mid ðornum aswogen, & æfterðæmðe
Eac sint to manienne ða ðe ungefandod habbað ðissa flæsclicena scylda, ðæt hie ne wenen for
Forðæmðe on ðæm dome ðæs ryhtwisan Deman onwent sio geearnung ðone had & ða
Hwa is nu ðæra ðe gesceadwis sie, & to ðæm gleaw sie ðæt he swelces hwæt tocnawan cunne,
& swaðeah ðæt bleoh ðæs welhæwnan iacintes bið betera ðonne ðæs blacan carbuncules;
Forðæm ðæs ðe sio endebyrdnes & ðæt gecynd forwiernð ðæm iacinte, se wlite his beorhtnesse
Swa bið on ðisse menniscan gecynde manige on beteran hade & on beteran endebyrdnesse
Swa ðætte oft on læwedum hade & on læwedum girelan mid godum weorcum & mid ryhte life
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to manianne ða ðe ða geworhtan synna wepað, on oðre ða ðe ða
On oðre wisan sint to manienne ða ðe hiera geworhtan synna wepað, on oðre ða ðe hira geðohtan
Ða sint to manienne ðe hira geworhtan wepað, ðætte hie ða gedonan yfelu mid fullfremedre
Be ðæm is awriten on ðæm nigon & hundsiofantigoðan sealme: God us drencte swiðe gemetlice
Hi sint eac to manienne ðæt hi unaðrotenlice ða gedonan synna gelæden beforan hira modes
Be ðæm cwæð Dauid on psalmum: Ahwyrf, Dryhten, ðin eagan from minum synnum.
& lytle ær he cwæð: Mine misdæda bioð simle beforan me.
Swelce he cwæde: Ic ðe bidde ðæt ðu no ne locige on mine synna, forðæmðe ic self him ealneg
Be ðæm eac cwæð Dryhten ðurh Essaias ðone witgan: ðinra synna ne weorðe ic gemunende, ac
Forðæm hie sint to manienne ðæt hi ælce synne geðencen ðæra ðe hi gemunan mægen, forðæm,
Be ðæm is swiðe wel gecweden ðurh Ieremias ðone witgan, ða ða he ðæra Iudea misdæda ealle
Todældu wæteru we lætað ut of urum eagum, ðonne we for synderlecum synnum synderleca
& ðonne he wierð mid ðære anre onstyred, ðonne wyrð he eallra geclænsod.
Eac hie sint to manienne ðæt hi gelefen & baldlice getruwien ðæt hi ða forgiefnesse habbað for
Ne gedyde næfre se mildheorta Dryhten, ne an his mode ne gebrohte swelce hreowsunga, gif he
He gecyðde swiðe mildheortlice ðæt he him deman nolde, ða he gedyde ðæt hi him selfe ær
Be ðæm is awriten on ðæm feower & hundnigontigoðan psalme, hit is gecweden: Wuton cuman
& eft hit wæs gecweden ðurh sanctus Paulus: ðær we us selfum demden, ðonne ne demde us no
& eft hi sint to manienne ðæt hi swa hopigen to ðære forgiefnesse ðæt hie for ðære orsorgnesse to
Forðæm oft ðæt lytige dioful ðæt mod ðæt he mid ðære synne ascrenceð, ðonne he gesihð ðæt
Ðæt wæs mid ðære biesene getacnod ðe Dinan gedon wæs Iacobes dohtor.
Hit is awriten ðæt Dina wære ut gangende sceawian ðæs londes wif.
Ða hi ða geseah Sihhem, Emmores sunu ðæs ebreiscan, se wæs aldormon ðæs londes, & ða
& ða wæs his mod gehæft mid ðæm mædene, & he ða hi swa unrote oleccende to him
Ðonne gæð Dine ut sceawian ða elðiodigan wif, ðonne hwelces monnes mod forlæt his ægne
Sihhem, ðæs landes ealdorman, geniedde ðæt mæden Dinan, ða he hie gemette swa wandrian.
Swa deð se dioful ðæt mod ðæt he gemet on unnyttum sorgum: he hit awiert.
Sihhemes mod wæs ða gehæft to Dinan.
Swa ðæt dioful, ðonne he gesihð ðæt mod on ðæm ilcan unryhtan willan ðe he bið, & ðonne eft
Be ðæm wæs swiðe ryhtlice gecweden ðætte Sihhem Dinan liðelice olehte, ða ðe he hi
Swa deð ðæt dioful ðæm mode: hwilum he gedeð ðæt him ðyncð ðæt hit nan scyld ne sie ðæt
Hwilum he gedeð ðæt him ðyncð, ðeah hit scyld sie, ðæt oðre men hefiglicor syngien;
Hwilum he fortruwað to swiðe Godes mildheortnesse;
Hwilum him ðyncð ðæt he hæbbe fierst genogne to hreowsianne.

Oft eac folgað ðæm mode swa grundleaslicu costung, & hit swa forswilgð, ðæt hit mid nanre
Ðonne hit bið onstyred mid ðære lustbærnesse, & hit onhagað to ðæm ðingum, ðonne forlæt hit

Ðæt gespan bið ðurh dioful.

Sio geðafung bið ðurhtogen ðurh ðone gæst.
Se sætere, ðæt is se dioful, he hine spænð on woh.

Be ðæm is gecweden ðurh sumne wisne mon: Gif hwa on hand nimð hwæt unclænes, & hine
Se bið aðwægen of unclænnesse, se ðe aðwihð mid hreowsunga his unclænnesse.
Se ðonne gehrinð eft ðære unclænnesse ðe syngað æfter his hreowsunga.

Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to monianne ða ðe ða unaliefedan ðing, ða ðe hi doð, herigað;
On oðre ða ðe hi tælað, & swaðeah doð.
On oðre wisan sint to manienne ða ðe ðæt unliefde herigað, & eac doð;
On oðre ða ðe hit leað, & swaðeah doð.
Ða sint to manienne, ða ðe ægðer ge hit doð ge hit herigað, ðæt hi ongieten ðæt hi oft swiðor
Forðæm, ðonne hi yfel doð, ðonne doð hi ðæt him anum, ac ðonne hi hit heriað, ðonne lærað hi
Forðy hi sint to manienne, ðonne hi licettað ðæt hi willen astyfecian ðæt yfel on him selfum,
Ac ðæt him ðynce genog on ðæm ðæt hi hit selfe dydon.
Ond eft hi sint to manienne, ðeah hi him nyllen ðæt ondrædan ðæt hi yfele sien, ðæt hi huru
Forðæm oft ðæt yfel ðæt forholen bið, hit bið fleonde, forðæm, ðonne ðæt mod sceamað ðæt hit
Hwæt, ælc unryhtwis mon, swa he scamleaslicor his yfel cyð, swa he freolicor hit ðurhtiehð, &
Swa hit him ðonne aliefedlicre ðyncð, swa he ðær diopor on gedyfð.

& ðonne ðæt mod bið innan oferswiðed mid ðæm ofermettum, hi toflowað swiðe hræðe ut, &
Forðæm sint to manienne ða ðe hi wið ða lytlan scylda gehealdað, ond ðeah hwilum gedufað on
Ac ðonne hi wenað ðæt hi of hira ægnum mægene hi hæbben gehealden wið ða lytlan scylda,
Forðæm ðæt hi ongiten feallende ðæt hie ær hiora agnes ðonces ne stodon, ðætte ðæt mod, ðe
Ac hi sint to manienne ðæt hie ongieten ðæt hie oft gesyngiað giet wyrs on ðæm ðæt hi hi
Forðæmðe hi licettað hie unscyldge, ðonne hi hi wæreniað wið ða lytlan.
Ac hi hi ne ladiað nowiht, ðonne hi wyrcað ða miclan & ða openan.
Ðæt is open yfel beforan Gode ðæt mon ða miclan do, ac ðæt is licettung haligdomes for
Be ðæm wæs gecweden on ðæm godspelle to Fariseum ðæt hi wiðbleowen ðære fleogan, &
Swelce he openlice cwæde: ða lytlan yflu ge fleoð, & ða miclan ge fretað.
Ðæt is ðæt ilce ðæt eft wæs getæled ðurh ðone muð ðære Soðfæstnesse, ðæt is Crist, he cwæð:
Nis us nawht recceleaslice to gehiranne ðætte he nemde ða undiorestan wyrta ðe on wyrttunum
Ðurh ðone stenc sint getacnode ða liceteras, ðe willað habban ðone hlisan haligdomes, & don
& ðeah hi for micel god ne don, hi wilniað ðæt hi micel ðyncen, & hi mon widherge.
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to monianne ða ðe nanwuht godes ne onginnað;
On oðre wisan ða ðe hit onginnað, & wel ne geendiað.
On oðre wisan sint to manienne ða ðe nan god ne onginnað;
On oðre ða ðe hit onginnað, & no ne geendiað.
Ða ðonne ðe nan god ne onginnað, ne sint hi no to lærenne hwæt hi don scylen, ær him si belagen
Forðæmðe hi nyllað underfon ðæt uncuðe ðæt hi gehirað, buton hi ær ongieten hu frecenlic ðæt is
Forðæm nan mon ne bitt oðerne ðæt he hine rære, gif he self nat ðæt he afeallen bið;
Ne eac se, se his wunde sar ne gefret,ne wilnað he nanes læces.
Forðy him is ærest to cyðanne hu idel ðæt is ðæt hi lufiað & hu unnytt, & siððan him is to
Ærest hi sculon ongietan ðæt hi fleon ðæt ðæt hi lufiað.
Ðonne magon hi sið ieðelice ongietan ðæt ðæt is to lufianne ðæt hi ær flugon.
Micle ðy bet hi underfoð ðæt uncuðe, gif hi on ðæm cuðan gewislice ongietað hwæt ðæron
Ðonne hi leorniað mid fulre estfulnesse ða soðan god to secanne, ðonne hi mid fulle gesceade
Ac gehiren hi ðæt ðas andweardan god bioð from ælcre lustfulnesse swiðe hrædlice gewitende,
& nu ðæt ðæt hie lyst hi sculon nedenga forlætan, & ðeah ðæt hi nu nedenga forlætað him bið
Oft ðeah weorðað men swiðe halwendlice afærde mid ðæm ilcan ðingum ðe hi ær unnytlice
Leornað ðonne to lufianne ðæt he ær forhogde.
Be ðæm wæs gecweden to Ieremie ðæm witgan, ða he wæs onsended to læranne, hit wæs
Forðæm, buton he ðæt woh ær towurpe ne meahte he noht nytwyrðlice ðæt ryht getimbrian;
Forðæm, buton he of his hieremonna mode ða ðornas ðære idlan lufan ær upatuge, unnyt he
Forðæm wæs eac ðætte sanctus Petrus ærest towearp ðæt ðæt he eft timbrede.
Ðæt wæs ða ða he Iudeas nolde nan wuht læran hwæt hi don scolden, ac him cidde, forðæmðe hi
Se ilca God hine eft aweahte to onliesanne ða gehæftan on helle.
Forðæm him ætwat Petrus ða dæde ðe he walde, siððan hi ongeaten hiora wælhreownesse, ðæt
Ða andwyrdon hin ða Iudeas, & cwædon: Hwæt magon we his nu don, broður Petrus?
Petrus andswarode, & cwæð: Doð ærest hreowsunga, & weorðað siððan gefullwade.
Ða edniwunge & ða lare hi swiðe hrædlice forsawen, ðær hi ær ne ongeten ðone hryre & ða
Gelicost ðæm ðe sancte Paule wæs, ða him ðæt leoht com of hefonum, & hine gebregde: næs
Ac ða he swa gebreged on eorðan feoll, & ascode, & cwæð: Hwæt eart ðu, Dryhten?
Ða wæs him swiðe hraðe geandwyrd: Ic eom se Nazarenisca Hælend, ðe ðu ehtst.
& ða cwæð he: Dryhten, hwæt hætst ðu me ge don?
Ða ondwyrde him Dryhten: Aris, & gong to geonre byrig;
Ðe mon sægð ðara hwæt ðu don scealt.
Loca nu, hu Dryhten wæs sprecende of hefonum to his ehtere, & hine ðreade for his ærgedonan
Ærðæmðe he him sæde hu he hine forðhealdan sceolde, ða wæs gehroren sio upahæfenes Paules
& sona æfter ðæm hryre ðære upahæfennesse he ongan timbrian eaðmodnesse.
Ða ða he wilnode lare æt Gode, ða gefeoll se egeslica ehtere to ðon ðæt he swa micle stranglicor
Swa sint to teweorpanne ærest ða ðe nan god ær ne dydon ðurh ðreaunge of ðære heardnesse
Forðæm we ceorfað heah treowu on holte ðæt we hi eft uparæren on ðæm botle, ðær ðær we
Ac swa swa hi swiður adrygde bioð on eorðan swa hi mon mæg orsorglicor upfegean.
Ongean ðæt sint to manienne ða ðe næbre nyllað fulfremman ðæt god ðæt hi onginnað, ðæt hi
Forðæm, gif ðæt ne wexð ðæt hie tiohhiað to donne, ðonne wanað ðæt ðæt hi ær dydon.
Ac ælces mannes mod on ðys middangearde hæfð scipes ðeaw.
Ðæt scip wile hwilum stigan ongean ðone stream, ac hit ne mæg, buton ða rowend hit teon, ac
Elles hit gelent mid ðy streame.
Swa deð sio forlætnes ðæs godan weorces.
Hio winð wið ða god ðe mon ær gedon hæfð, buton mon simle swincende & wyrcende sie god
Be ðæm wæs gecweden ðurh Salomon ðone snotran, he cwæð: Se ðe his willum for his slæwðe
Be ðæm wæs eac gecweden ðurh Iohannis ðone godspellere to ðære ciricean biscepe ðe Sardis
Forðæm he cwæð ðæt he forðy ne funde his weorc fulfremed beforan Gode, ða ðe he ær worhte,
Swa eac, gif we ne gebetað ðæt on us deadbæres is ðurh synna, ðonne acwilð ðæt ðætte on us ær
Eac hi sint to manienne ðæt hi geornlice geðencen ðætte hit bið wyrse ðæt mon a onginne faran
Forðæm, gif us ne lyst ðæra ærrena yfela ðe we ær worhton, ðonne ne gælð us nan ðing te
Ac hi scoldon gehiran ðone cwide ðe awriten is on ðæm ærendgewrite sancte Petres;
Hit is awriten ðæt him wære betere ðæt hi no soðfæstnesse weg ne ongeaten, ðonne hi underbæc
Eac hi sculon gehiran ðone cwide ðe be him awriten is on ðæm bocum ðe hatton Apocalipsin,
Ac forðonðe he is wlaco, & nis nauðer, ne hat, ne ceald, ðeah ic hine supe, ic hine wille eft
Se ðonne bið wearm, nalles wlaco, ðe god geornlice onginð, & eac geendað.
Ac se bið ceald ðe nan god ne onginð.
Ac swa swa ðæt cealde ærest onginð wlacian, ær hit fulwearm weorðe, swa eac ðæt wearme
Swa eac se ðe forlæt ðone cele ungetreownesse.
& wyrð wlacra treowa, & nyle ðonne ðæt wlæce oferwinnan, & wearmian oð he wealle.
Butan tweon, se ðe to lange & to fæste wunað on ðæm wlacum treowum, he geortreowð ðæt he
& ðeah he ær truwige, ðonne he ceald bið, ðæt he mæge wearm weorðan, he geortriewð, ðonne
Swa eac se ðe nu giet on synnum is, næfð he no forlæten ðone truwan & ðone tohopan his
Ac se, se ðe æfter his gehwerfnesse to lange wlæc bið, ðonne lytlað him se tohopa ðe he hæfde,
Forðæm wilnað God to ælcum men ðæt he sie oððe wearm oððe ceald, ðylæs he for wlæcnesse
Forðæm se cealda ðencð to wearmianne, & se wearma welð on godum cræftum, ðylæs he sie
Forðæm ælc wæter bið ðy unwerodre to drincanne, æfterðæmðe hit wearm bið, gif hit eft
Ðætte on oðre wisan sint to monianne ða ðe diegellice yfel doð, & god openlice;
& on oðre wisan ða ðe willað helan ðæt hi to gode doð, & of sumum ðingum openlice cyðað
On oðre wisan sint to manienne ða ðe yfel degellice doð, & god openlice;
On oðre wisan ða ðe ða god helað ðe hi doð, & ne reccað hwæt him mon ymbe ræswe.
Ða ðonne sint to manienne ða ðe yfel degellice doð, & god openlice, ðæt hi geðencen hu
Hi sint to manienne ðæt hi on ðara ðinga ende hiora modes eagan afæstnien, & gesion ðætte ðis
Ac ða dieglan yfel habbað ecne gewutan on ðæm godcundan Deman.
Ac ða god ðe hi openlice doð beforan monnum beoð fulneah swelce hi sien butan gewitnesse,
Ac hi habbað ece gewitnesse ðara yfela ðe hi diegellice doð, ðonne hi heolað from monnum ðæt
Be swelcum monnum cwæð Dryhten ðæt hi wæren gelicost deadra manna byrgennum, ða bioð
Swa bioð ða ðe hira god eowiað beforum monnum, & hira yfel helað oninnan him selfum: hi
Ac hi sint to manienne ðæt hi ne forsion ða god ðe hi doð, ac wenen him maran mede to ðonne
To swiðe hi hi forsioð, gif hi him maran mede to ne wenað ðonne eorðlices lofes, & him ðær
Hi sellað wið to lytlum weorðe ðæt ðæt hi meahton hefonrice mid gebycggan: sellað wið manna
Be ðæm cwæð Dryhten on his godspelle ðæt ðæt wære hira med.
Ac forðæmðe hi ðæt god openlice doð, & ðæt yfel diegellice, hi tacniað mid ðæm ðæt men
Ongean ðæt sint to manienne ða ðe god diegellice doð, & swaðeah on sumum weorcum
Ac ðonne hi ða yflan bisne openlice doð, & ðæt god degellice, ðonne ne helpað hi mid oðrum
Forðæm swa hwa swa hilð his godan weorc, forðæmðe he wile fleon idel gielp, ðonne ne læt he
He deð swelce he plantige treowu, & ceorfe of ða wyrtruman.
Be ðæm cwæð Crist on his godspelle: Doð eower godan weorc beforan mannum, ðæt hi mægen
Ac swaðeah is awriten on ðæm ilcan bocum ungelic cwide ðissum, ðæt is ðæt he cwæð:
Ac hwæt wile ðæt nu beon weorca ðæt us on oðerre stowe forbiet ðæt we hit beforan mannum
Ac ðær ðær us God forbead ðæt we ure ryhtwisnesse beforan monnum dyden, he us gecyðde
Ond eft ða he us het ðæt we hit beforan monnum dyden, ða cwæð he sona ðæræfter, to ðon ðæt hi
On ðæm twæm wordum he us getacnode for hwelcum ðingum we sceolden ure godan weorc
For ðæm ðingum bið ælc god weorc god, sie swa open swa degle, swæðer hit sie.
Ðonne se mon no his ægenne gielp mid ne secð, ac ðæs uplican Fæder, ðeah he hit openlice do,
Ac se ðonne se hit degellice deð, & ðeah wolde ðæt he wurde arasod, & siððan forðy hered,
Forðæm hit is betere, swa swa we ær cwædon, ðætte ælc mon adryge of oðerra monna mode
Forðæm hit gebyreð oft, ðonne hwa ne recð hu micles yfeles him mon to wene, ðeah he self nan
Be ðæm cwæð sanctus Paulus to his giongrum, ða ða he sumun liefde to ðicgganne ðætte he
Forðæm he cwæð: Lociað nu ðæt ðios eowru leaf ne weorðe oðrum monnum to biswice.
Ond eft he cwæð be ðæm ilcan: ðonne forwyrð ðin broður for ðinum ðingum, for ðone ær Crist
Swa ðonne ge gesyngiað wið eowre broðer, & ofsleað hira untruman gewit, ðonne gesyngige ge
Ðæt ilce mænde Moyses, ða he cwæð: Ne cweðe ge nan lað ðæm deafan.
& eft he cwæð: Ne screnc ðu ðone blindan.
Se ðonne cwið yfel ðæm deafan, se ðone æfweardan tælð, gif he hine unscyldigne wat.
& se screncð ðone blindan, ðe ðone ungesceadwisan mirð mid ðy ðe he his god degellice deð, &
Ymbe ðæt, hu mon monige scyndan scyle to ðæm ðætte his godan dæda ne weorðen to yflum
Ðis sint nu ða lara ðe ðæs modes hierdas & ðære sawle sceolon ealle men læran ðæt hi ealneg
Forðæm hit bið swiðe geswincful ðæt mon ælcne mon scyle on sundrum læran, hit is ðeah
& huru ðæt he self do swa swa he oðre lærð, forðon ðæt he mæge self gan orsorglice betwuxn
Ond ðæt he huru swa egesige ða ofermodan, ðæt he ða eaðmodan mid ðy to swiðe ne fære;
Ond swa lære ða oðre eaðmetta, swa he ðone ege to swiðe ðæm oðrum ne geiece;
Ðæt he swa frefre ða eaðmodan, swa ða ofermodan ne weorðen unmidlode;
Ond swa lære ða slawan geornfulnesse godes weorces, swa he ða geornfullan to ungemetlice ne
Ond swa tilige hira geswinc to gemetgianne, swa he ða idlan ne gedo orsorge, ðæt hi forðy ne
Ond ðæt he swa stiere ðæm ungeðyldegum irsunga, swa he ðone hnescan ðafettere on recceleste
& ðeah swa tilige hi to onælenne, swa hi ða hatheortan ne forbærnen;
Ond swa eac ða uncystigan cysta lære, swa he ða cystgan on merringe ne gebringe;
Ond swa eft ða rummodan fæsthafolnesse læren, swa hi ða uncystegan on yfelre hneawnesse ne
Ond swa læren ða wifgalan gesinscipe, swa hi ða forhæbbendan ne gebrengen on unryhthæmde;
& swa ða forhæbbendan læren forhæfdnesse, swa hie ne forsion ðone gesinscipe;
Ond swa wilnigen to oleccanne ðæm godum, & hi to herianne, swa hi huru ne oleccen ðæm
Ond swa herien ðæt mæste god, swa hi ðæt læste ne forsion;
Ond eft swa herie ða ðe lytel god doð, ðæt hi ne wenen ðæt hi genog don.
Ymbe ðæt hu mon ænne mon scyndan scyle, ðonne he yfle costunga monege ðrowað.
Ðæt bið eac swiðe hefig broc ðæm lareowe ðæt he scyle on gemænre lare, ðær ðær he eall folc
Forðæm hira unðeawas bioð swiðe ungelice.
& ðeah bið giet earfoðre ælcne on sundrum to læranne, forðæmðe manege bioð ðe hæbbað ða
Oft eac gebyreð ðætte sume bioð to ungemetlice bliðe for sumum gesælðum, oððe for ðæs blodes
Forðæm is to giemanne ðæm lareowe ðæt he swa swiðe stiere ðære unrotnesse ðæt he to swiðe
Ond eft swa gemidlige ða blisse ðe of ðære orsorgnesse cymð ðæt sio unrotnes to swiðe ne
Forðæm oft ða oferbliðan weorðað gedrefde for ungemetlicre onettunga, ðonne him hwæthwugu
Swa eac ða swiðe unrotan bioð oft gedrefde mid ungemetlice ege, & ðeah hwilum bioð genedde
Swa ðonne sceal se magister gemetgian ðone færlican ege ðæt ðær ðeah ne weaxe to
Hwelc wundor is ðæt, ðeah ðæs modes læcas behealden ðas lare, ðonne ðæs lichoman læcas
Ac hwilum ðeah ofðrycð ðone lichoman ungemetlicu mettrymnes.
Ongean swelce mettrymnesse mon beðorfte stronges læcedomes, ðær se mettruma lichoma hine
Forðæm is ðæm læce swiðe geornlice to giemanne ðæt he swa strangne læcedom selle ðæm
Ac ðonne he deð ðæm siocan swiðe gesceadwislicne fultum, ðonne he afliemð æt anum cierre
Ac forhwy ne mæg ðonne micle ma ðæs modes læce gehælan ða adle ðæra unðeawa monigra
Ond ðeah ðæs lichoman læcas oft æt anum cierre ægðer doð, ge ðæm lichoman gebeorgað, ge eac
Ðætte hwilum ða leohtan scylda bioð beteran to forlætenne, ðylæs ða hefigran weorðen
Oft eac gebyreð ðætte twegen unðeawas hreosað on ænne man, oðer læssa, oðer mara.
Forðæm sceal ðæs modes læce ær tilian ðæs ðe he wenð ðæt ðone mon ær mæge gebrengan on
Hwilum ðeah, ðær ðær mon oðres tiolað, ðær weaxð se oðer.
Forðæm sceal se gesceadwisa læce lætan ær weaxan ðone læssan, & tilian ðæs maran;
Oððæt sio tid cume ðæt he ðæs oðres tilian mote, buton he begra ætgæddre getilian mæge.
Ne gaderað he no mid ðy unðeawas, ac tilað ðæs gewundedan werpe ðe he bewitan sceal, oððæt
Oft weorðað ða oferswiðde mid unryhthæmde ða ðe ne magon forlætan hira gifernesse.
Oft eac gebyreð ðæm ðe him ægðer ðissa ondrædað, gif hi hi wið ægðer gehealdað, ðæt hi
Hwæðres ðonne ðara yfela is betere ær to tilianne, buton swæðres swæðer frecenlicre is?
Forðy is betere ðæt mon læte sume hwile weaxan ðæt idelgielp, oððæt mon fullice mæge
Forðæm cwæþ sanctus Paulus to his cnihte, ða he ongeat ðæt he wolde oðer twega, oððe ða giet
Ne scyle ðeah nan mon for ðæm anum ðingum don ðæt ðæt he to gode deð, ðæt he ne ðyrfe his
Forðæm se æðela lareow sanctus Paulus, ða he ongeat ðæt he ægðer ne meahte his cniht gelæran
Ða he him geðafode ðone gielp, ða forbead he him ðæt yfel, for ðæm ðæt he ðy ieð meahte ðæt
Ðætte ða untruman mod mon ne scyle ellenga to healice læran.
Þæm lareowe is to wietanne ðæt he huru nanum men mare ne beode ðonne he acuman mæge,
Forðæm sio hea lar is betere manegum monnum to helanne, & feawum to secgganne.
Be ðæm cwæð sio Soðfæstnes ðurh hi selfe, ðæt is Crist, he cwæð: Hwa wenstu ðæt sie to ðæm
Ðurh ða gemetgunge ðæs hwætes is getacnod gemetlico word, ðylæs hira mon ma geote on ðæt
Be ðæm cwæð sanctus Paulus: Ic ne mæg no to eow sprecan swa swa to gæstlicum, ac swa swa
Forðæm ge sint giet cilderu on eowrum geleafan, ðy ic sceal sellan eow giet mioloc drincan,
Forðæm wæs eac ðætte Moyses behelede ða ofermætan bierhto his ondwlitan beforan ðæm
Forðæm wæs eac beboden ðurh Moyses, gif hwa adulfe pytt, & ðonne forgiemeleasode ðæt he
Swa eac swa hwa swa becymð to ðæm hiehstan wisdome, & ðonne ne forhilð ða diogolnesse
Be ðæm cwæð Dryhten to ðæm eadgan Iobe: Hwa sealde kokke wisdom?
Ðæt getacnað ðætte æghwelc ðæra halgena lareowa ðe nu lærað on ðære ðisternesse ðisses
Ðonne græt se lareow swa swa kok on niht, ðonne he cwið: Nu us is tima ðæt we onwæcnen of
Ond eft, ðonne he cwið: Onwæcnað, ge ryhtwisan, & ne syngiað ma.
Ðæs cocces ðeaw is ðæt he micle hludor singð on uhtan ðonne on dægred.
Ac ðonne hit nealæcð dæge, ðonne singð he smælor & smicror.
Swa sceal ælc gesceadwis lareow opene lare & swutole ðæm ðiestrum modum bodian, & nane
Ac siððan hi gesion ðætte ða ðistran mod ðæra dysegena monna auht nealæcen ðæm leohte ðære
Be ðæm weorcum ðæs lareowes & be his wordum.
Hit is nu ðearf ðæt we for lufum eft cierren betwuxn oðrum spræcum to ðæm ðe we ær spræcon.
Ðæt is ðætte ælc lareow swiðor lære mid his weorcum ðonne mid his wordum.
Hwæt, se kok ðe we ær ymb spræcon, ærðæmðe he crawan wille, hefð up his fiðru, & wecð hine
Swa is ðearf ðæt se lareow ærest awecce hine selfne, ðæt he wacie on ðære geornfulnesse godra
Ac hudenige ærest hine selfne, oð he wacige, & ahrisige siððan oðre to geornfulnesse godra
Ðaccige hine selfne mid ðæm fiðrum his geðohta.
Ðæt is ðæt he behealde ðurh ða wæccan his smeaunga ærest hwæt on him selfum unnyttes sie,
Ærest he sceal wrecan on him selfum his agnu yfelu & ða hreowsian, & siððan oðerra monna
Ærest hi sculon eowian on hiora agnum weorcum eall ðæt hi eft læran willað mid hiora
Ðonne hwa ðis eall gefylled hæbbe, hu he ðonne sceal hine selfne geðencan & ongietan, ðylæs
Oft eac ða lareowas weorðað onstyrede mid diegelre blisse, ðonne hi ongietað ðæt hi gemetlice
Ac him is ðonne micel ðearf ðæt he hine hrædlice selfne gewundige mid ðy ege ðæt he him
Ðætte ðær ðær he oðerra monna wunda lacnað, he self ne weorðe aðunden on upahæfennesse for
Ðæt he hine selfne ne forlæte, ðær he oðerra freonda tilige, & him self ne afealle, ðær ðær he
Forðæm oft ða cræftas & ða mægenu weorðað te færwyrde ðæm ðe hi hæfð, ðonne hi for hira
Ac ðæt mod oft olecð him selfum, & ðonne for ðære oleccunga forlæt ðone ege his selfes
Ðonne gerest ðæt mod hit orsorglice on ðære fortruwunga.
Ðonne cymð se lytega sætere to ðæm slawan mode, & ateleð him eall ðæt he ær to gode gedyde,
Ond ðonne beforan ðæs ryhtwisan Deman eagum him wyrð ðæt gemynd ðæra mægena & ðæra
Be ðæm wæs gecweden ðurh Ezechiel ðone witgan, he cwæð: Astig eft ofdune ðonan ðe ðu
Swelce he openlice cwæde: For ðæm wlite ðinra cræfta ðu wurde upahæfen, & ðonan ðu wyrst
Eft se ilca witga sæde bispell bi Hierusalem, & tælde ðæt mod ðe for his cræftum ofermodgede,
Ðonne bið ðæt mod upahæfen for ðære fortruwunga his cræfta, ðonne hit for hiora geearnunga
Ac ðurh ða fortruwednesse hit wyrð getogen to ðon ðæt hit wyrð forlegen on ofermettum.
Ærest se awiergda gæst hit lærð utane ðone gielp, oððæt he ingæð ðurh ða ofermetta, & hit
Be ðæm worde is to ðenceanne ðe he cwæð to ðæm burgwarum: Ge eow forlægon eowres
Ðæt is ðætte ðæt mod sona swa hit God forsihð, swa secð hit his agenne gielp, & gæderað him
Wilnað mid ðy to gebrædenne his ægen lof, & higað wið ðæs ðæt he wolde hu he eallum
Se bið forlegen on his agnum willan se ðe hine selfne diofle befæst, & Dryhten forlæt for
Be ðæm cwæð Dauid on ðæm siofan & hundsiofantiogoðan psalme, he cwæð: Dryhten geðafode
Gehæft bið hiora cræft, & hira wlite on hiora feonda hond gelæd, ðonne se ealda feond onwald
Se lytega fiond wile fondian ælces monnes mid ðære upahæfennesse for godum weorcum, ge
Forðæm ælc mod swa hit bið upahæfen swa bið hit forlæten from Gode, & sona swa hit bið
Be ðæm cwæð Dauid eft on ðæm nigon & twentiogoðan psalme, he cwæð: Ic wende on minum
Ac siððan he ongeat ðæt he wæs aðunden on upahæfennesse for his godan weorcum, ða
Swelce he openlice cwæde: Ic wende ðæt ic wære swiðe strong on manegum cræftum, ac ic
& eft he cwæð on ðæm eahta & hundælleftiogoðan psalme, he cwæð: Ic swor swa swa ic
Ac he ongeat swiðe hraðe, ða he gemette ða gedrefednesse, ðæt hit næs on his agnum onwalde
& ða wende he hine sona to his gebede, & sohte him ðær fultum to, & cwæð: Ic eom gehened
Ac gecwuca me æfter ðinum wordum, Dryhten.
Swa oft sio godcunde gemetgung, ærðæmðe hio ðæm men selle cræftas & mægen, hio him
Forðæm eac wæs gecweden to Ezechiele ðæm witgan ðæt he wære monnes sunu, ærðæmðe him
Swelce hine God openlice manode, & him to cwæde: Ne beo ðu to upahæfen on ðinum mode
& ðeah ðu ðæt hehste ðurhfare, ne forgiet ðu ðeah ðæt ðu man eart, ac geðenc ðone bridel ðinre
Forðæm is micel ðearf ðæt we ures modes eagan gecerren to ðære sceawunga urre untrymnesse.
Ðonne us fullicost oleccað ða cræftas & ða mægenu, ðonne is us micel ðearf ðæt we eaðmodlice
Ðætte ure mod ðy fæstre & ðy strengre beforan Gode sie on ðæm cræftum for ðære eaðmodnesse
Forðæm oft se ælmiehtiga God forlet ðæt mod his gecorenra gesyngian on sumum lytlum
Ðæt hi hi for ðæm miclum ðingum ne mægen to upahebban, ða hwile ðe hi ne magon gebetan
Ðylæs hi dyrren ofermodgian for ðæm æðelestum weorcum, ða hwile ðe hi ne magon
Loca nu, ðu goda wer Iohannes, hu fægerne & hu wlitigne monnan ic hæbbe atæfred, swa
Ðær ic hæbbe getæht hwelc hierde bion sceal.
To ðæm ic wæs gened mid ðinre tælnesse, ðæt ic nu hæbbe manege men gelæd to ðæm stæðe
Ac ic ðe bidde ðæt ðu me on ðæm scipgebroce ðisses andweardan lifes sum bred geræce ðinra
